HISTORY_FILE = os.path.join(app.config['zhenghe'], 'data/task_history.json')


def load_task_history():
    """启动时从文件加载历史任务数据"""
    global task_history
    if os.path.exists(HISTORY_FILE):
        try:
            with open(HISTORY_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                task_history = json.load(f)
            print(f"✅ 已加载 {len(task_history)} 条历史任务记录")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ 读取历史任务失败: {e}")
            task_history = {}
    else:
        task_history = {}


def save_task_history():
    """将 task_history 持久化到文件"""
    try:
        print(f"💾 正在保存 task_history 到: {HISTORY_FILE}")
        with open(HISTORY_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(task_history, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"✅ 成功保存 {len(task_history)} 条记录到 {HISTORY_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ 保存 task_history 失败: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()



# 生成视频封面（取第一帧）
def generate_thumbnail(video_path, thumbnail_path):
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
    ret, frame = cap.read()  # 读取第一帧
    if ret:
        cv2.imwrite(thumbnail_path, frame)  # 保存为图片
    cap.release()


# 用户登录 注册
# 模拟数据库中的用户信息（实际项目中会使用真实数据库）
# curl -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/login -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"username\": \"user1\", \"password\": \"123456\"}"
import hashlib
from functools import wraps
from datetime import datetime
USER_DATA_FILE = "./user_data/users.json"  # 用户数据存储文件路径

def init_user_file():
    """初始化用户数据文件（不存在则创建空字典）"""
    if not os.path.exists(USER_DATA_FILE):
        with open(USER_DATA_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump({}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
def load_users():
    init_user_file()
    try:
        with open(USER_DATA_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            users = json.load(f)
        # 新增日志：打印所有用户名，确认是否包含"user"
        print(f"✅ 读取到的用户列表：{list(users.keys())}")
        return users
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        print("⚠️ 用户数据文件损坏，返回空列表")
        return {}
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"⚠️ 未找到用户数据文件：{USER_DATA_FILE}")  # 新增：提示文件不存在
        return {}
def save_users(users):
    """将用户数据保存到JSON文件"""
    try:
        with open(USER_DATA_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(users, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ 保存用户数据失败：{str(e)}")
        return False


def init_super_admin(super_admin_username="super_admin", super_admin_password="Super@123"):
    """初始化超级管理员（仅首次调用）"""
    users = load_users()
    if super_admin_username in users:
        print(f"⚠️ 超级管理员 {super_admin_username} 已存在")
        return False

    hashed_pwd = hashlib.sha256(super_admin_password.encode()).hexdigest()
    users[super_admin_username] = {
        "password": hashed_pwd,
        "role": "super_admin",  # 超级管理员身份
        "registered_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "profile": {"name": "超级管理员", "school": "系统默认"},
        "password_updated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }
    if save_users(users):
        print(f"✅ 超级管理员初始化成功（账号：{super_admin_username}，密码：{super_admin_password}）")
        return True
    return False
# 首次启动初始化超级管理员（注释掉表示不自动创建）
# init_super_admin()
# 原有的require_role装饰器完全兼容多角色组合，例如：
# @require_role(["super_admin"])  # 仅超级管理员可访问
# @require_role(["super_admin", "admin"])  # 超级/普通管理员可访问
# @require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 所有登录用户可访问

# ---------------------- 身份校验装饰器 ----------------------
from functools import wraps


def require_role(allowed_roles):
    def decorator(f):
        @wraps(f)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            token = request.headers.get("Authorization")
            print(f"📌 接收到的token：{token}")

            if not token or not token.startswith("token_"):
                print("❌ token不存在或格式错误")
                return jsonify({"code": 401, "message": "未授权访问，请先登录"}), 401

            # ---------------------- 修复token解析逻辑 ----------------------
            try:
                # 正确做法：用切片移除固定前缀"token_"（长度为6）
                token_without_prefix = token[6:]  # 替换lstrip("token_")
                # 按最后一个下划线分割，得到用户名和时间戳
                username_part, timestamp_part = token_without_prefix.rsplit("_", 1)
                username = username_part
                print(f"📌 从token解析出的用户名：{username}")  # 现在会正确输出"testuser01"
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                print("❌ token格式错误，无法提取用户名")
                return jsonify({"code": 401, "message": "无效的token格式"}), 401
            # -----------------------------------------------------------------

            # 验证用户存在性
            users = load_users()
            print(f"📌 装饰器中load_users读取到的用户列表：{list(users.keys())}")
            print(f"📌 尝试查找的用户名：{username}")

            user = users.get(username)
            if not user:
                print(f"❌ 装饰器判定用户不存在：{username}")
                return jsonify({"code": 401, "message": "用户不存在，token已失效"}), 401

            # 验证角色权限
            user_role = user.get("role", "user")
            if user_role not in allowed_roles:
                print(f"❌ 角色权限不足：{user_role} 不在 {allowed_roles} 中")
                return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": f"权限不足，仅允许{allowed_roles}角色访问"}), 403

            # 权限通过，调用接口函数
            print(f"✅ 装饰器校验通过：{username}（{user_role}）")
            return f(username, user_role, *args, **kwargs)

        return wrapper

    return decorator


# 注册
@app.route('/api/register', methods=['POST'])
def register():
    """
    用户注册接口
    请求体：{"username": "testuser", "password": "testpass123"}
    """
    data = request.json or {}
    username = data.get("username")
    password = data.get("password")

    # 1. 参数校验
    if not username or not password:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "用户名和密码不能为空"}), 400
    if len(username) < 3:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "用户名长度不能少于3位"}), 400
    if len(password) < 6:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "密码长度不能少于6位"}), 400

    # 2. 检查用户是否已存在
    users = load_users()
    if username in users:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "用户名已存在"}), 400

    # 3. 加密密码并保存用户（默认普通用户身份）
    hashed_pwd = hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
    users[username] = {
        "password": hashed_pwd,
        "role": "user",  # 普通用户
        "registered_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "profile": {},
        "password_updated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }

    # 4. 保存数据
    if save_users(users):
        return jsonify({"code": 200, "message": "注册成功，请登录"}), 200
    return jsonify({"code": 500, "message": "注册失败，服务器错误"}), 500


# 登录
@app.route('/api/login', methods=['POST'])
def login():
    """
    用户登录接口
    请求体：{"username": "testuser", "password": "testpass123"}
    """
    data = request.json or {}
    username = data.get("username")
    password = data.get("password")

    # 1. 参数校验
    if not username or not password:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "用户名和密码不能为空"}), 400

    # 2. 验证用户存在性
    users = load_users()
    user = users.get(username)
    if not user:
        return jsonify({"code": 401, "message": "用户名不存在"}), 401

    # 3. 验证密码
    hashed_pwd = hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
    if hashed_pwd != user["password"]:
        return jsonify({"code": 401, "message": "密码错误"}), 401

    # 4. 生成token并返回用户信息（含身份）
    token = f"token_{username}_{datetime.now().timestamp()}"
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "登录成功",
        "token": token,
        "userInfo": {
            "username": username,
            "role": user.get("role", "user"),  # 返回身份
            "registered_at": user["registered_at"],
            "profile": user.get("profile", {})
        }
    }), 200

# 编辑个人信息
import os

# 配置头像上传路径和允许的文件类型
# 建议用绝对路径，避免相对路径在不同运行环境下的歧义
AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'static', 'avatars'))
ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {'png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'gif'}

# 确保上传目录存在，并验证目录可写
try:
    os.makedirs(AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)
    # 验证目录是否可写（创建一个临时文件再删除，确保权限正常）
    test_file = os.path.join(AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER, '.test_write_permission')
    with open(test_file, 'w') as f:
        f.write('test')
    os.remove(test_file)
except Exception as e:
    # 启动时就暴露目录问题，避免后续上传失败
    raise RuntimeError(f"头像上传目录配置错误：{str(e)}") from e


def allowed_file(filename):
    """优化：兼容文件名含多个“.”的场景，取最后一个“.”后的扩展名"""
    # 先获取最后一个“.”的索引，再截取扩展名
    dot_index = filename.rfind('.')
    if dot_index == -1:  # 无扩展名
        return False
    ext = filename[dot_index+1:].lower()  # 从“.”后一位开始截取
    return ext in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS


# 编辑个人信息（支持头像上传）
@app.route('/api/edit-profile', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])
def edit_profile(current_username, current_role):
    # 混合获取表单数据和文件
    data = request.form.to_dict()
    target_username = data.get("username")

    # 权限控制核心逻辑（保持不变）
    users = load_users()
    target_user_role = users.get(target_username, {}).get("role", "user")

    if current_role == "user" and target_username != current_username:
        return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": "权限不足，仅能修改自己的资料"}), 403

    if target_username not in users:
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "用户不存在"}), 404

    # 数据验证规则（保持不变）
    valid_genders = ["男", "女"]
    valid_edu_levels = ["小学", "初中", "高中", "中职"]
    valid_grades = [
        "一年级", "二年级", "三年级", "四年级", "五年级", "六年级",
        "初一年级", "初二年级", "初三年级",
        "高一年级", "高二年级", "高三年级"
    ]
    valid_statuses = ["在职", "职前"]
    valid_titles = ["三级", "二级", "一级", "高级", "正高级", "无"]
    valid_honors = [
        "特级教师", "省级骨干教师", "市级或区县级骨干教师",
        "镇级或乡级骨干教师", "校级骨干教师", "以上都不是"
    ]

    # 初始化profile：基于原有资料，确保所有键名存在
    original_profile = users[target_username].get("profile", {})
    all_profile_keys = [
        "name", "gender", "school", "education_level", "grade",
        "status", "region", "age", "teaching_years", "title",
        "honor", "avatar_url"
    ]
    profile = {key: original_profile.get(key) for key in all_profile_keys}

    # 【必选项验证】姓名、性别、学校、地区（地区改为数组格式）
    # 1. 姓名验证（必填，不能为空字符串）
    if "name" not in data or not data["name"] or data["name"].strip() == "":
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "姓名为必填项，不能为空"}), 400
    profile["name"] = data["name"].strip()

    # 2. 性别验证（必填，且必须是有效值）
    if "gender" not in data or not data["gender"] or data["gender"].strip() == "":
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "性别为必填项，不能为空"}), 400
    gender = data["gender"].strip()
    if gender not in valid_genders:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "性别必须是'男'或'女'"}), 400
    profile["gender"] = gender

    # 3. 学校验证（必填，不能为空字符串）
    if "school" not in data or not data["school"] or data["school"].strip() == "":
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "学校为必填项，不能为空"}), 400
    profile["school"] = data["school"].strip()

    # 4. 地区验证（改为数组格式：["省", "市", "区"]）
    if "region" not in data or not data["region"]:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "地区为必填项，不能为空"}), 400

    try:
        # 解析地区为数组（前端需传递JSON字符串，如 '["安徽省", "六安市", "裕安区"]'）
        region = json.loads(data["region"])

        # 验证是否为数组
        if not isinstance(region, list):
            raise ValueError("地区格式必须为数组")

        # 验证数组长度（至少1级，最多3级）
        if len(region) < 1 or len(region) > 3:
            raise ValueError("地区层级必须为1-3级（省/市/区）")

        # 验证每个层级的地区名称不为空
        for i, level in enumerate(region):
            if not isinstance(level, str) or level.strip() == "":
                raise ValueError(f"地区第{i + 1}级不能为空")
            # 去除首尾空格
            region[i] = level.strip()

        # 保存验证后的地区数组
        profile["region"] = region

    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return jsonify(
            {"code": 400, "message": "地区格式错误，必须为JSON数组（如[\"安徽省\", \"六安市\", \"裕安区\"]）"}), 400
    except ValueError as e:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": f"地区格式错误：{str(e)}"}), 400

    # 其他可选字段更新（保持不变）
    if "education_level" in data and data["education_level"] is not None and data["education_level"].strip():
        if data["education_level"] not in valid_edu_levels:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "学段必须是'小学'、'初中'、'高中'或'中职'"}), 400
        profile["education_level"] = data["education_level"]

    if "grade" in data and data["grade"] is not None and data["grade"].strip():
        if data["grade"] not in valid_grades:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "年级选择不正确"}), 400
        profile["grade"] = data["grade"]

    if "status" in data and data["status"] is not None and data["status"].strip():
        if data["status"] not in valid_statuses:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "身份状态必须是'在职'或'职前'"}), 400
        profile["status"] = data["status"]

    # 处理年龄（可选）
    if "age" in data and data["age"] is not None and data["age"].strip():
        try:
            age = int(data["age"].strip())
            if 21 <= age <= 60:
                profile["age"] = age
            else:
                return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "年龄必须在21-60之间"}), 400
        except ValueError:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "年龄必须是数字"}), 400

    # 处理教龄（可选）
    if "teaching_years" in data and data["teaching_years"] is not None and data["teaching_years"].strip():
        try:
            years = int(data["teaching_years"].strip())
            if 0 <= years <= 40:
                profile["teaching_years"] = years
            else:
                return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "教龄必须在0-40之间"}), 400
        except ValueError:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "教龄必须是数字"}), 400

    # 处理职称（可选）
    if "title" in data and data["title"] is not None and data["title"].strip():
        if data["title"] not in valid_titles:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "职称选择不正确"}), 400
        profile["title"] = data["title"]

    # 处理称号（可选）
    if "honor" in data and data["honor"] is not None and data["honor"].strip():
        if data["honor"] not in valid_honors:
            return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "称号选择不正确"}), 400
        profile["honor"] = data["honor"]

    # 处理头像上传（可选）
    if 'avatar' in request.files:
        file = request.files['avatar']
        if file.filename.strip():  # 仅在选择了文件时处理
            try:
                # 生成唯一文件名
                original_ext = file.filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() if '.' in file.filename else ''
                if not original_ext:
                    raise Exception("文件没有扩展名")
                unique_filename = f"{uuid.uuid4()}.{original_ext}"
                file_path = os.path.join(AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER, unique_filename)

                # 保存文件
                file.save(file_path)
                if not os.path.exists(file_path) or os.path.getsize(file_path) == 0:
                    raise Exception("文件保存后为空或不存在")

                # 更新头像URL
                new_avatar_url = f"/static/avatars/{unique_filename}"
                profile["avatar_url"] = new_avatar_url

                # 删除旧头像
                old_avatar_url = original_profile.get("avatar_url")
                if old_avatar_url:
                    old_avatar_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(
                        os.path.dirname(__file__),
                        old_avatar_url.lstrip('/')
                    ))
                    if os.path.exists(old_avatar_path) and old_avatar_path.startswith(AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER):
                        try:
                            os.remove(old_avatar_path)
                        except Exception as e:
                            print(f"警告：旧头像删除失败（路径：{old_avatar_path}），错误：{str(e)}")

            except Exception as e:
                return jsonify({
                    "code": 500,
                    "message": f"头像上传失败：{str(e)}"
                }), 500

    # 保存更新后的资料
    users[target_username]["profile"] = profile
    users[target_username]["updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()

    if save_users(users):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": "资料更新成功",
            "profile": profile  # 包含所有键名，地区为数组格式
        }), 200

    return jsonify({"code": 500, "message": "资料更新失败，服务器错误"}), 500




# 个人信息查看
# 个人信息查看（包含头像信息）
@app.route('/api/getUserInfo', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def getUserInfo(current_username, current_role):
    """
    查看当前登录用户的个人信息，包括头像URL
    无需请求参数，通过Authorization token识别用户身份
    """
    try:
        # 1. 加载用户列表
        users = load_users()

        # 2. 验证当前用户是否存在
        if current_username not in users:
            return jsonify({
                "code": 404,
                "message": "失败",
                "error": "当前用户不存在或已被删除"
            }), 404

        # 3. 获取当前用户的完整信息（过滤敏感字段）
        current_user = users[current_username]

        # 构造返回数据（包含头像URL）
        user_info = {
            "username": current_username,
            "role": current_user.get("role", "user"),
            "profile": current_user.get("profile", {}),  # 此处包含头像信息avatar_url
            "created_at": current_user.get("created_at", ""),
            "updated_at": current_user.get("updated_at", ""),
            "avatar_url": current_user.get("avatar_url", ""),
            "age": current_user.get("age", ""),
            "teaching_years": current_user.get("teaching_years", "")
            # 不返回password等敏感字段
        }

        # 4. 返回成功响应
        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": "成功",
            "data": user_info
        }), 200

    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"服务器错误：{str(e)}"
        }), 500



# 头像查看
@app.route('/api/avatar/<username>', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 需登录才能访问
def get_avatar(current_username, current_role,username):
    """
    头像访问接口：仅允许本人、管理员查看指定用户的头像
    :param username: 目标用户的用户名（需要查看其头像的用户）
    :param current_username: 当前登录用户的用户名（由装饰器传入）
    :param current_role: 当前登录用户的角色（由装饰器传入）
    """
    # 1. 权限校验
    # 规则1：普通用户只能查看自己的头像
    if current_role == "user" and username != current_username:
        return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": "无权限查看他人头像"}), 403

    # 规则2：普通管理员不能查看超级管理员的头像
    users = load_users()
    target_user_role = users.get(username, {}).get("role", "user")
    if current_role == "admin" and target_user_role == "super_admin":
        return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": "无权限查看超级管理员头像"}), 403

    # 2. 验证目标用户是否存在，以及是否有头像
    target_user = users.get(username)
    if not target_user:
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "用户不存在"}), 404

    avatar_url = target_user.get("profile", {}).get("avatar_url")
    if not avatar_url:
        # 无头像时返回默认头像
        default_avatar_path = os.path.join(AVATAR_UPLOAD_FOLDER, "default.png")
        if os.path.exists(default_avatar_path):
            return send_file(default_avatar_path, mimetype='image/png')
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "用户未设置头像"}), 404

    # 3. 拼接真实的头像文件路径（从 avatar_url 转换）
    # 例如：/static/avatars/xxx.png → 项目目录/static/avatars/xxx.png
    avatar_file_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), avatar_url.lstrip('/'))

    # 4. 验证文件是否存在
    if not os.path.exists(avatar_file_path):
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "头像文件不存在"}), 404

    # 5. 返回头像文件（自动根据文件后缀设置 mimetype）
    return send_file(avatar_file_path)


# 修改密码
@app.route('/api/change-password', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])
def change_password(current_username, current_role):
    data = request.json or {}
    target_username = data.get("username")
    old_pwd = data.get("old_password")
    new_pwd = data.get("new_password")
    confirm_pwd = data.get("confirm_password")

    # 权限控制（同编辑资料）
    users = load_users()
    target_user_role = users.get(target_username, {}).get("role", "user")
    if current_role == "user" and target_username != current_username:
        return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": "权限不足，仅能修改自己的密码"}), 403
    if current_role == "admin" and target_user_role in ["super_admin", "admin"]:
        return jsonify({"code": 403, "message": "权限不足，仅能修改普通用户密码"}), 403

    # 原有参数校验逻辑（不变）
    if not all([target_username, old_pwd, new_pwd, confirm_pwd]):
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "所有密码字段都不能为空"}), 400
    if new_pwd != confirm_pwd:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "新密码和确认密码不一致"}), 400
    if len(new_pwd) < 6:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "新密码长度不能少于6位"}), 400
    if old_pwd == new_pwd:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "新密码不能与旧密码相同"}), 400

    if target_username not in users:
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "用户不存在"}), 404

    user = users[target_username]
    hashed_old_pwd = hashlib.sha256(old_pwd.encode()).hexdigest()
    if hashed_old_pwd != user["password"]:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "旧密码不正确"}), 400

    hashed_new_pwd = hashlib.sha256(new_pwd.encode()).hexdigest()
    users[target_username]["password"] = hashed_new_pwd
    users[target_username]["password_updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    users[target_username]["password_updated_by"] = current_username

    if save_users(users):
        return jsonify({"code": 200, "message": "密码修改成功，请重新登录"}), 200
    return jsonify({"code": 500, "message": "密码修改失败，服务器错误"}), 500


# 3. 新增角色管理接口（超级管理员专属）
@app.route('/api/super-admin/update-role', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin"])  # 仅超级管理员可访问
def update_user_role(current_username, current_role):  # 修正参数名，与装饰器保持一致
    """超级管理员修改用户角色（普通管理员/用户）"""
    data = request.json or {}
    target_username = data.get("target_username")
    new_role = data.get("new_role")

    # 参数校验
    if not target_username or new_role not in ["admin", "user"]:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "参数错误：target_username不能为空，new_role只能是admin或user"
        }), 400

    # 禁止修改自己的角色（避免失去超级管理员权限）
    if target_username == current_username:  # 使用修正后的参数名
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "禁止修改超级管理员自己的角色"}), 400

    # 验证目标用户存在（关键步骤：打印日志确认读取到的用户）
    users = load_users()
    print(f"当前读取到的用户列表：{list(users.keys())}")  # 新增日志，排查用户是否存在
    print(f"目标用户：{target_username}")  # 打印目标用户名，确认参数正确
    if target_username not in users:
        return jsonify({"code": 404, "message": "目标用户不存在"}), 404

    # 更新角色
    users[target_username]["role"] = new_role
    users[target_username]["role_updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
    users[target_username]["role_updated_by"] = current_username  # 使用修正后的参数名
    if save_users(users):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": f"成功将用户 {target_username} 角色修改为 {new_role}"
        }), 200
    return jsonify({"code": 500, "message": "角色修改失败，服务器错误"}), 500


# 新增用户信息查询接口
@app.route('/api/users/query', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin"])  # 仅超级管理员和普通管理员可访问
def query_users(current_username, current_role):
    """
    超级管理员：查看所有管理员（super_admin和admin）信息
    普通管理员：查看所有普通用户（user）信息
    """
    # 1. 读取所有用户数据
    users = load_users()
    if not users:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": "成功",
            "data": []
        })

    # 2. 根据角色筛选用户
    filtered_users = []
    for username, user_data in users.items():
        user_role = user_data.get("role", "user")

        # 超级管理员：筛选所有管理员（super_admin和admin）
        if current_role == "super_admin" and user_role in ["super_admin", "admin"]:
            filtered_users.append({
                "username": username,
                "role": user_role,
                "registered_at": user_data.get("registered_at", ""),
                "profile": user_data.get("profile", {}),
                "last_updated": user_data.get("password_updated_at", user_data.get("registered_at", ""))
            })

        # 普通管理员：仅筛选普通用户（user）
        elif current_role == "admin" and user_role == "user":
            filtered_users.append({
                "username": username,
                "role": user_role,
                "registered_at": user_data.get("registered_at", ""),
                "profile": user_data.get("profile", {}),
                "last_updated": user_data.get("password_updated_at", user_data.get("registered_at", ""))
            })

    # 3. 按注册时间排序（最新的在前）
    filtered_users.sort(
        key=lambda x: x["registered_at"],
        reverse=True
    )

    # 4. 返回结果
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "data": {
            "total": len(filtered_users),
            "users": filtered_users
        },
        "current_role": current_role  # 便于前端展示当前身份
    })



# 检查文件格式是否允许
def allowed_file(filename):
    return '.' in filename and \
        filename.rsplit('.', 1)[1].lower() in ALLOWED_VIDEO

# 一、暂存

# curl -X POST "http://localhost:5000/api/upload" -F "video=@C:\Users\19947\Desktop\AI课堂教学评价系统相关材料\视频材料\test.mp4"  -F "courseName=高等数学"  -F "teacherName=张老师"  -F "teachingTime=2025-07-29 上午"  -F "uploadTime=2025-07-29 10:15:23"  -F "courseScore=5"  -F "courseType=必修"  -F "school=XX大学"  -F "grade=大一"  -F "teachingBuilding=主教楼"  -F "class=计算机1班"  -F "classroom=101"  -F "operation=正常授课"
@app.route('/api/upload', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 所有登录用户可上传
def upload(current_username, current_role):
    # 获取视频文件（可能不存在）
    video_file = request.files.get('video')  # 使用get方法，不存在时返回None

    # 生成时间戳作为标识基础
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')
    # 新增：格式化各种时间戳
    upload_time = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')  # 用于uploadTime
    upload_timestamp = datetime.now().isoformat()  # 用于uploadTimestamp
    saved_filename = ""
    thumbnail_filename = ""
    original_filename = ""  # 新增：原始文件名变量

    # 处理视频文件（如果存在）
    if video_file and video_file.filename:
        # 验证文件格式
        if not allowed_file(video_file.filename):
            return jsonify({
                'code': 400,
                'message': "失败",
                'error': '不支持的文件格式，仅允许：mp4, mov, webm, avi'
            })

        # 新增：保存原始文件名
        original_filename = video_file.filename

        # 生成保存的文件名
        secure_original = secure_filename(video_file.filename)
        saved_filename = f"{timestamp}_{secure_original}"

        # 保存视频文件
        video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], saved_filename)
        video_file.save(video_path)

        # 生成缩略图
        thumbnail_filename = f"{timestamp}_thumbnail.jpg"
        thumbnail_path = os.path.join(app.config['THUMBNAIL_FOLDER'], thumbnail_filename)
        generate_thumbnail(video_path, thumbnail_path)  # 假设该函数已实现

    # 构建视频信息（包含所有要求的键）
    video_info = {
        "uploader": current_username,
        "originalFilename": original_filename,  # 新增：原始文件名
        "videoId": timestamp,  # 新增：视频ID
        "uploadTime": upload_time,  # 新增：上传时间
        "uploadTimestamp": upload_timestamp,  # 新增：上传时间戳
        'courseName': request.form.get('courseName', ''),
        'school': request.form.get('school', ''),
        'educationStage': request.form.get('educationStage', ''),
        'grade': request.form.get('grade', ''),
        'teachingAge': request.form.get('teachingAge', ''),
        'title': request.form.get('title', ''),
        'teachingTime': request.form.get('teachingTime', ''),
        'teacherName': request.form.get('teacherName', ''),
        'region': request.form.get('region', ''),
        'courseType': request.form.get('courseType', ''),
        'className': request.form.get('className', ''),
        'teacherAge': request.form.get('teacherAge', ''),
        'teacherTitle': request.form.get('teacherTitle', ''),
        'courseScore': request.form.get('courseScore', ''),

        'textbook_edition': request.form.get('textbook_edition', ''),  # 教材版本

        'filename': saved_filename,  # 无视频时为空字符串
        'teaching_build': request.form.get('teaching_build', ''),
        'ClassRoom': request.form.get('ClassRoom', ''),
        'thumbnailUrl': f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}' if thumbnail_filename else ''
    }

    # 保存视频信息到JSON文件（无论是否有视频）
    info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{timestamp}_info.json")
    with open(info_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(video_info, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    # 构建任务历史记录（包含所有要求的键）
    task_history[timestamp] = {
        "videoId": timestamp,
        "originalFilename": original_filename,  # 新增：原始文件名
        "uploadTime": upload_time,  # 新增：上传时间
        "uploadTimestamp": upload_timestamp,  # 新增：上传时间戳
        "filename": saved_filename,  # 无视频时为空字符串
        "uploader": current_username,
        "courseName": video_info['courseName'],
        "teacherName": video_info['teacherName'],
        "teachingAge": video_info['teachingAge'],
        "teacherAge": video_info['teacherAge'],
        "teacherTitle": video_info['teacherTitle'],
        "courseScore": video_info['courseScore'],
        "title": video_info['title'],
        "school": video_info['school'],
        "educationStage": video_info['educationStage'],
        "grade": video_info['grade'],
        "className": video_info['className'],
        "teachingTime": video_info['teachingTime'],
        "region": video_info['region'],
        "courseType": video_info['courseType'],

        "textbook_edition": video_info['textbook_edition'],  # 教材版本

        "status": "saved",  # 暂存状态，无论是否有视频
        "createdAt": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "startedAt": None,
        "completedAt": None,
        "error": None,
        "thumbnailUrl": video_info['thumbnailUrl'],  # 无视频时为空字符串
        "teaching_build": video_info['teaching_build'],
        "ClassRoom": video_info['ClassRoom'],
    }
    save_task_history()  # 持久化任务记录

    # 返回响应（包含所有要求的键）
    return jsonify({
        'code': 200,
        'message': "成功",
        'dataMap': {
            'videoId': timestamp,
            'originalFilename': original_filename,  # 新增：原始文件名
            'uploadTime': upload_time,  # 新增：上传时间
            'uploadTimestamp': upload_timestamp,  # 新增：上传时间戳
            'filename': saved_filename,  # 无视频时为空字符串
            'thumbnailUrl': video_info['thumbnailUrl'],  # 无视频时为空字符串
            'videoInfo': video_info
        }
    })




# 视频上传分析
# curl -X POST "http://localhost:5000/api/upload-video" ^
#   -H "Content-Type: multipart/form-data" ^
#   -F "video=@C:\Users\19947\Desktop\AI课堂教学评价系统相关材料\视频材料\test.mp4" ^
#   -F "courseName=高等数学" ^
#   -F "school=XX大学" ^
#   -F "educationStage=高等教育" ^
#   -F "grade=大一" ^
#   -F "teachingAge=10" ^
#   -F "title=副教授" ^
#   -F "teachingTime=2025-08-20 10:00" ^
#   -F "teacherName=张老师" ^
#   -F "region=北京市" ^
#   -F "courseType=理论课" ^
#   -F "className=计算机1班" ^
#   -F "teacherAge=35" ^
#   -F "teacherTitle=骨干教师" ^
#   -F "courseScore=92"
@app.route('/api/upload-video', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 所有登录用户可上传
def upload_video(current_username, current_role):
    """
    视频上传接口：保存视频文件及完整基础信息到JSON文件
    """
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str1 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    iso_time = current_time.isoformat()  # 用于 task_history 的标准时间格式

    if 'video' not in request.files:
        return jsonify({
            'code': 400,
            'message': '失败',
            'error': '未上传视频文件'
        })
    video_file = request.files['video']

    # 验证文件格式
    if not (video_file and allowed_file(video_file.filename)):
        return jsonify({
            'code': 400,
            'message': '失败',
            'error': '不支持的文件格式，仅允许：mp4, mov, webm, avi'
        })

    # 生成唯一标识符（时间戳）
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')
    original_filename = secure_filename(video_file.filename)
    saved_filename = f"{timestamp}_{original_filename}"

    # 构建完整的视频信息（包含所有上传的基础信息）
    video_info = {
        # 基础信息
        "uploader": current_username,  # 新增：记录上传者用户名（关键！用于后续筛选）
        'courseName': request.form.get('courseName', ''),  # 课程名称
        'school': request.form.get('school', ''),  # 学校
        'educationStage': request.form.get('educationStage', ''),  # 学段
        'grade': request.form.get('grade', ''),  # 年级
        'teachingAge': request.form.get('teachingAge', ''),  # 教龄
        'title': request.form.get('title', ''),  # 职称
        'teachingTime': request.form.get('teachingTime', ''),  # 授课时间
        'teacherName': request.form.get('teacherName', ''),  # 教师名称
        'region': request.form.get('region', ''),  # 地区
        'courseType': request.form.get('courseType', ''),  # 课型
        'className': request.form.get('className', ''),  # 班级
        'teacherAge': request.form.get('teacherAge', ''),  # 教师年龄
        'teacherTitle': request.form.get('teacherTitle', ''),  # 教师称号
        'courseScore': request.form.get('courseScore', ''),  # 课堂得分
        'teaching_build': request.form.get('teaching_build', ''),  # 教学楼
        'ClassRoom': request.form.get('ClassRoom', ''),  # 教室

        'textbook_edition': request.form.get('textbook_edition', ''),  # 教材版本

        # 系统信息
        'filename': saved_filename,  # 保存的文件名
        'originalFilename': original_filename,  # 原始文件名
        'videoId': timestamp,  # 视频唯一ID
        'uploadTime': time_str1,  # 上传时间
        'uploadTimestamp': iso_time  # 标准格式时间戳
    }

    # 保存视频文件
    video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], saved_filename)
    video_file.save(video_path)

    # 生成缩略图
    thumbnail_filename = f"{timestamp}_thumbnail.jpg"
    thumbnail_path = os.path.join(app.config['THUMBNAIL_FOLDER'], thumbnail_filename)
    generate_thumbnail(video_path, thumbnail_path)  # 假设该函数已实现

    # 保存完整视频信息到JSON文件（关键修改：确保所有基础信息都被保存）
    video_info['thumbnailUrl'] = f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}'
    info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{timestamp}_info.json")
    try:
        with open(info_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(video_info, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"✅ 视频基础信息已保存到: {info_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ 保存视频信息JSON失败: {str(e)}")
        return jsonify({
            'code': 500,
            'message': '失败',
            'error': f'保存视频信息失败: {str(e)}'
        })

    video_id = timestamp

    # === 添加到任务队列 ===
    new_task = {
        'video_id': video_id,
        'filename': saved_filename,
        'original_filename': original_filename,
        'upload_time': time_str1,
        'status': '排队中',
        'video_info': video_info,  # 包含完整信息
        'thumbnail_url': f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}',
        'result': None
    }

    with task_lock:
        tasks[video_id] = new_task


    # 初始化 task_history 并保存
    task_history[video_id] = {
        "videoId": video_id,
        "filename": saved_filename,
        "uploader": current_username,  # 新增：记录上传者用户名（关键！用于后续筛选）
        "courseName": video_info['courseName'],
        "teacherName": video_info['teacherName'],
        "teachingAge": video_info['teachingAge'],
        "teacherAge": video_info['teacherAge'],
        "teacherTitle": video_info['teacherTitle'],
        "courseScore": video_info['courseScore'],
        "title": video_info['title'],
        "school": video_info['school'],
        "educationStage": video_info['educationStage'],
        "grade": video_info['grade'],
        "className": video_info['className'],
        "teachingTime": video_info['teachingTime'],
        "region": video_info['region'],
        "courseType": video_info['courseType'],
        "textbook_edition": video_info['textbook_edition'],  # 版本

        "status": "saved",  # 暂存状态
        "createdAt": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "startedAt": None,
        "completedAt": None,
        "error": None,
        "thumbnailUrl": f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}',
        "teaching_build": video_info['teaching_build'],  # 教学楼
        "ClassRoom": video_info['ClassRoom'],  # 教室
        'uploadTime': time_str1,  # 上传时间
    }
    save_task_history()  # 立即持久化保存

    analysis_queue.put(video_id)  # 加入分析队列
    with task_lock:
        task_history[video_id]['status'] = '排队中'  # 覆盖初始的“saved”
        save_task_history()

    # 返回响应
    return jsonify({
        'code': 200,
        'message': '成功',
        'dataMap': {
            'videoId': video_id,
            'filename': saved_filename,
            'thumbnailUrl': f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}',
            'videoInfo': video_info,  # 返回完整信息
            'uploadTime': time_str1,
            'status': '排队中'
        }
    })


# 获取队列状态
@app.route('/api/queue-status', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def get_queue_status(current_username, current_role):
    with task_lock:
        # 1. 获取所有任务
        all_tasks = tasks.values()

        # 2. 按角色筛选任务
        if current_role == "user":
            # 普通用户：仅显示自己上传的任务（匹配uploader字段）
            filtered_tasks = [task for task in all_tasks if task.get("uploader") == current_username]
        else:
            # 管理员/超级管理员：显示所有任务
            filtered_tasks = all_tasks

        # 3. 按时间倒序排序
        task_list = sorted(
            filtered_tasks,
            key=lambda x: x['upload_time'],
            reverse=True
        )
        return jsonify({
            'code': 200,
            'message': "成功",
            'data': task_list,
            'current_role': current_role  # 便于前端展示权限
        })
