# 重新分析
@app.route('/api/reanalyze', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])
def reanalyze_video(current_username, current_role):
    """重新分析接口：修复task_progress同步问题"""
    # 1. 接收请求参数
    data = request.get_json() or {}
    video_id = data.get('videoId')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            'code': 400,
            'message': '失败',
            'error': '缺少必要参数：videoId'
        })

    with task_lock:
        # 2. 检查历史任务是否存在
        if video_id not in task_history:
            return jsonify({
                'code': 404,
                'message': '失败',
                'error': f'未找到 videoId={video_id} 的历史任务'
            })

        history_task = task_history[video_id]

        # 3. 修复：正确获取上传者
        task_uploader = history_task.get('uploader', 'unknown')
        if current_role == "user" and task_uploader != current_username:
            return jsonify({
                'code': 403,
                'message': '失败',
                'error': '权限不足：仅任务上传者或管理员可发起重新分析'
            })

        # 4. 检查当前状态是否允许重新分析
        current_status = history_task.get('status', 'unknown')
        allowed_status = ['completed', 'failed', 'saved']
        if current_status not in allowed_status:
            return jsonify({
                'code': 400,
                'message': '失败',
                'error': f'当前状态「{current_status}」不允许重新分析，仅支持{allowed_status}状态'
            })

        # 5. 检查视频文件是否存在
        saved_filename = history_task.get('filename')
        if not saved_filename:
            return jsonify({
                'code': 400,
                'message': '失败',
                'error': '历史任务中未记录视频文件名'
            })
        video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], saved_filename)
        if not os.path.exists(video_path):
            return jsonify({
                'code': 404,
                'message': '失败',
                'error': f'视频文件不存在：{saved_filename}（可能已被删除）'
            })

        # ---------------------- 核心修复：同步重置task_progress并持久化 ----------------------
        with progress_lock:
            # 1. 删除旧进度数据
            if video_id in task_progress:
                del task_progress[video_id]
            # 2. 初始化新进度结构
            steps_order = [
                "video_to_txt", "cv_behavior_emo", "discourse_analysis",
                "teach_stu_behavior", "content_sumary", "calculate_score",
                "comprehensive_assessment", "problem_improvement"
            ]
            task_progress[video_id] = {
                step: {
                    "status": "未开始",  # 初始状态为未开始（排队中）
                    "start_time": None,
                    "end_time": None,
                    "error": None
                } for step in steps_order
            }
        # 关键修复：重置后立即保存到文件，确保持久化
        save_task_progress()
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------

        # 6. 更新历史任务状态为「排队中」
        task_history[video_id].update({
            'status': '排队中',
            'error': None,
            'startedAt': None,
            'completedAt': None,
            'reanalyzeInitiateTime': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'reanalyzed': True,
            'reanalyzedBy': current_username
        })
        save_task_history()  # 同步更新任务历史文件

    # 7. 将任务加入分析队列
    analysis_queue.put(video_id)

    # 8. 返回成功响应
    return jsonify({
        'code': 200,
        'message': '成功',
        'dataMap': {
            'videoId': video_id,
            'status': '排队中',
            'filename': saved_filename,
            'reanalyzeInitiateTime': datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
            'msg': '已加入重新分析队列，等待处理'
        }
    })




# 任务个数统计
@app.route('/api/task-stats', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def task_stats(current_username, current_role):
    """
    统计任务状态：
    - 管理员：统计所有用户的任务
    - 普通用户：仅统计自己的任务
    """
    stats = {
        "total": 0,
        "processing": 0,
        "completed": 0,
        "queued": 0,
        "saved": 0,
        "failed": 0  # 新增：失败任务数
    }

    with task_lock:
        # 1. 按角色筛选任务
        if current_role == "user":
            # 普通用户：仅统计自己上传的任务
            filtered_tasks = [
                task for task in task_history.values()
                if task.get('uploader') == current_username
            ]
        else:
            # 管理员：统计所有任务
            filtered_tasks = task_history.values()

        for record in filtered_tasks:
            status = record.get('status')

            if status == 'saved':
                stats['saved'] += 1
            elif status in ['queued', '排队中']:
                stats['queued'] += 1
            elif status == 'processing':
                stats['processing'] += 1
            elif status == 'completed':
                stats['completed'] += 1
            elif status == 'failed':
                stats['failed'] += 1

        # total = 所有任务总数
        stats['total'] = len(task_history)

    return jsonify(
        {
            "code":200,
            "message":"成功",
            "data":stats,
            "current_role": current_role,
            "scope": "all" if current_role in ["super_admin", "admin"] else "self"  # 统计范围
        }
    )





# 暂存任务启动
# curl -X POST "http://localhost:5000/api/start-analysis/20250821153000" -H "Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
@app.route('/api/start-analysis/<video_id>', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def start_analysis(current_username, current_role,video_id):
    """
    将一个暂存的视频任务启动分析
    """
    # 1. 检查任务是否存在
    if video_id not in task_history:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '任务不存在'
        })

    record = task_history[video_id]

    # 2. 权限校验：仅管理员/任务上传者可启动
    task_uploader = record.get('uploader')
    if current_role == "user" and task_uploader != current_username:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 403,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '权限不足：仅任务上传者或管理员可启动分析'
        })

    # 2. 检查当前状态是否为 "saved"
    if record['status'] != 'saved':
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': f'该任务无法启动分析，当前状态：{record["status"]}'
        })

    # 3. 获取视频文件路径，检查文件是否存在
    video_filename = record['filename']
    video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], video_filename)
    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '视频文件丢失，无法分析'
        })

    # 4. 更新 task_history 状态为 "queued"
    current_time = datetime.now().isoformat()
    task_history[video_id]['status'] = 'queued'
    task_history[video_id]['createdAt'] = current_time  # 更新创建时间为启动分析时间
    task_history[video_id]['startedAt'] = current_time  # 记录开始分析时间
    task_history[video_id]['startedBy'] = current_username  # 记录启动分析的用户
    save_task_history()  # ✅ 立即持久化

    # 5. 在运行时任务池中创建任务记录（同步新字段）
    with task_lock:
        if video_id not in tasks:
            # 构建符合新参数的 video_info
            video_info = {
                "uploader": current_username,  # 新增：记录上传者用户名（关键！用于后续筛选）
                'courseName': record.get('courseName'),
                'teacherName': record.get('teacherName'),
                'school': record.get('school'),
                'educationStage': record.get('educationStage'),  # 新增：学段
                'grade': record.get('grade'),
                'teachingAge': record.get('teachingAge'),        # 新增：教龄
                'title': record.get('title'),                    # 新增：职称
                'teachingTime': record.get('teachingTime'),
                'region': record.get('region'),                  # 新增：地区
                'courseType': record.get('courseType'),
                'className': record.get('className'),            # 调整：原class改为className
                'teacherAge': record.get('teacherAge'),          # 新增：教师年龄
                'teacherTitle': record.get('teacherTitle'),      # 新增：教师称号
                'courseScore': record.get('courseScore'),        # 调整：课堂得分
                'filename': video_filename
            }

            tasks[video_id] = {
                'video_id': video_id,
                'filename': video_filename,
                'original_filename': video_filename.split('_', 1)[1] if '_' in video_filename else video_filename,
                'upload_time': record.get('uploadTime', current_time),
                'status': '排队中',
                'video_info': video_info,  # 包含新字段的视频信息
                'thumbnail_url': f'/api/thumbnails/{video_id}_thumbnail.jpg',
                'result': None,
                'uploader': task_uploader  # 同步上传者信息
            }

    # 6. 加入分析队列
    analysis_queue.put(video_id)

    # 7. 返回成功
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "dataMap": {
            'info': "任务已加入分析队列",
            'videoId': video_id,
            'status': 'queued',
            'startedBy': current_username
        }
    })



# 暂存信息编辑
@app.route('/api/update-saved-video/<video_id>', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def update_saved_video(current_username, current_role, video_id):
    """
    修改状态为 'saved' 的暂存视频元信息
    支持通过 form-data 更新字段，包括视频文件，并同步更新 info.json 文件
    """
    # 1. 检查任务是否存在
    if video_id not in task_history:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '任务不存在'
        })

    record = task_history[video_id]

    # 2. 权限校验：普通用户仅能修改自己上传的任务
    task_uploader = record.get('uploader')  # 依赖上传接口添加的"uploader"字段
    if current_role == "user" and task_uploader != current_username:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 403,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '权限不足：仅任务上传者或管理员可修改暂存任务信息'
        })

    # 3. 只允许修改状态为 'saved' 的任务
    if record['status'] != 'saved':
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': f'无法修改：当前任务状态为 "{record["status"]}"，仅允许修改状态为 "saved" 的任务。'
        })

    # 4. 获取表单数据和可能的视频文件
    form_data = request.form
    video_file = request.files.get('video')  # 可能的新视频文件

    # 5. 定义可更新的字段白名单（包含所有必要字段）
    updatable_fields = [
        'courseName', 'teacherName', 'school', 'educationStage',
        'grade', 'teachingAge', 'title', 'teachingTime',
        'region', 'courseType', 'className', 'teacherAge',
        'teacherTitle', 'courseScore', 'teaching_build', 'ClassRoom',
        # 新增视频相关字段支持
        'originalFilename','textbook_edition'  # 允许更新原始文件名（如果需要）
    ]

    updated = False
    update_time = datetime.now().isoformat()
    has_video = bool(record.get('filename', ''))  # 判断原记录是否有视频
    new_video_added = False

    # 6. 处理视频文件（如果有上传）
    if video_file and video_file.filename:
        # 验证文件格式
        if not allowed_file(video_file.filename):
            return jsonify({
                'code': 400,
                'message': "失败",
                'error': '不支持的文件格式，仅允许：mp4, mov, webm, avi'
            })

        # 生成新的文件名（保留原video_id确保关联）
        original_filename = video_file.filename
        secure_original = secure_filename(video_file.filename)
        saved_filename = f"{video_id}_{secure_original}"  # 使用原video_id而非新时间戳

        # 保存新视频文件（覆盖或新增）
        video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], saved_filename)
        video_file.save(video_path)

        # 生成新缩略图
        thumbnail_filename = f"{video_id}_thumbnail.jpg"
        thumbnail_path = os.path.join(app.config['THUMBNAIL_FOLDER'], thumbnail_filename)
        generate_thumbnail(video_path, thumbnail_path)  # 假设该函数已实现

        # 标记视频相关更新
        updated = True
        new_video_added = True
        thumbnail_url = f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}'

    # 7. 在锁内更新 task_history
    with task_lock:
        # 更新普通字段
        for field in updatable_fields:
            new_value = form_data.get(field)
            if new_value is not None:  # 允许空字符串或清空
                old_value = record.get(field, '')
                if str(new_value) != str(old_value):
                    record[field] = new_value
                    updated = True

        # 更新视频相关字段（如果有新视频）
        if new_video_added:
            record['filename'] = saved_filename
            record['originalFilename'] = original_filename
            record['thumbnailUrl'] = thumbnail_url
            record['uploadTime'] = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
            record['uploadTimestamp'] = datetime.now().isoformat()

        # 更新时间戳和修改人（仅当有实际修改时）
        if updated:
            record['updatedAt'] = update_time
            record['updatedBy'] = current_username  # 记录修改人
            save_task_history()  # 持久化保存 task_history.json

    # 8. 同步更新 info.json 文件（保持磁盘数据一致）
    if updated:
        info_file_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_info.json")
        if os.path.exists(info_file_path):
            try:
                # 读取原 info.json
                with open(info_file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    info_data = json.load(f)

                # 更新普通字段
                for field in updatable_fields:
                    if form_data.get(field) is not None:
                        info_data[field] = form_data.get(field)

                # 更新视频相关字段（如果有新视频）
                if new_video_added:
                    info_data['filename'] = saved_filename
                    info_data['originalFilename'] = original_filename
                    info_data['thumbnailUrl'] = thumbnail_url
                    info_data['uploadTime'] = record['uploadTime']
                    info_data['uploadTimestamp'] = record['uploadTimestamp']

                # 更新修改信息
                info_data['updatedAt'] = update_time
                info_data['updatedBy'] = current_username

                # 重写文件
                with open(info_file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(info_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"⚠️ 无法更新 info.json 文件: {e}")

    # 9. 返回响应，包含视频状态信息
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "dataMap": {
            'info': '视频信息更新成功' if updated else '未检测到修改',
            'videoId': video_id,
            'updated': updated,
            'hasVideo': bool(record.get('filename', '')),  # 当前是否有视频
            'newVideoAdded': new_video_added,  # 是否本次添加了视频
            'updatedAt': update_time if updated else None,
            'updatedBy': current_username if updated else None
        }
    })


# 获取视频列表接口
# curl -X GET "http://localhost:5000/api/videos"
@app.route('/api/videos', methods=['GET'])
def get_videos():
    """
    获取所有已上传视频的列表，包含封面、视频地址和元信息
    返回格式：
    [
      {
        "videoId": "20250729101523",
        "filename": "20250729101523_demo.mp4",
        "videoUrl": "/api/use/video/20250729101523_demo.mp4",
        "thumbnailUrl": "/thumbnails/20250729101523_thumbnail.jpg",
        "info": {
          "courseName": "高等数学",
          "teacherName": "张老师",
          "teachingTime": "2025-07-29 上午",
          "uploadTime": "2025-07-29 10:15:23",
          "courseScore": "5",
          "courseType": "必修",
          "school": "XX大学",
          "grade": "大一",
          "teachingBuilding": "主教楼",
          "class": "计算机1班",
          "classroom": "101",
          "operation": "正常授课"
        }
      }
    ]
    """
    videos = []
    upload_folder = app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
    thumbnail_folder = app.config['THUMBNAIL_FOLDER']

    # 如果上传目录不存在，返回空列表
    if not os.path.exists(upload_folder):
        return jsonify([])

    # 遍历上传目录
    for filename in os.listdir(upload_folder):
        file_path = os.path.join(upload_folder, filename)

        # 只处理文件（忽略目录）和允许的视频格式
        if os.path.isfile(file_path) and allowed_file(filename):
            # 提取 videoId（文件名前缀，时间戳）
            if '_' in filename:
                video_id = filename.split('_', 1)[0]
            else:
                video_id = None

            if not video_id or not video_id.isdigit():
                continue  # 跳过不符合命名规则的文件

            # 查找对应的元数据文件
            info_file = f"{video_id}_info.json"
            info_path = os.path.join(upload_folder, info_file)
            if os.path.exists(info_path):
                try:
                    with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        info = json.load(f)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"读取元数据失败 {info_path}: {e}")
                    info = {}
            else:
                # 如果没有 info.json，使用默认信息
                info = {
                    "courseName": "未知课程",
                    "teacherName": "未知教师",
                    "teachingTime": "",
                    "uploadTime": datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getctime(file_path)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
                    "courseScore": "",
                    "courseType": "",
                    "school": "",
                    "grade": "",
                    "teachingBuilding": "",
                    "class": "",
                    "classroom": "",
                    "operation": "",
                    'teaching_build':"",
                    "ClassRoom":""
                }

            # 生成封面图路径
            thumbnail_filename = f"{video_id}_thumbnail.jpg"
            thumbnail_path = os.path.join(thumbnail_folder, thumbnail_filename)
            if os.path.exists(thumbnail_path):
                thumbnail_url = f'/api/thumbnails/{thumbnail_filename}'
            else:
                thumbnail_url = '[]'  # 可选：提供默认图

            # 构造视频对象
            video_data = {
                "code": 200,
                "message": "成功",
                "videoId": video_id,
                "filename": filename,
                "videoUrl": f'/api/use/video/{filename}',
                "thumbnailUrl": thumbnail_url,
                "info": info
            }
            videos.append(video_data)

    # 按上传时间倒序排列（videoId 是时间戳）
    videos.sort(key=lambda x: x['videoId'], reverse=True)

    return jsonify({
        'code':200,
        'message':"成功",
        'data':videos
    })



import json
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from flask import request, jsonify as flask_jsonify

# 假设DATA_DIR与报告编辑接口一致（存放video_id_result.json的目录）
DATA_DIR = "./整合结果"
os.makedirs(DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)

def parse_filter_params(request_args) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """解析筛选参数，转换范围参数为数字（统一格式，优化版）"""
    # 定义所有支持的参数，避免无效参数传入
    supported_base_params = [
        'courseName', 'teacherName', 'teachingTime', 'school', 'grade',
        'courseType', 'status', 'startUploadTime', 'endUploadTime',
        'teaching_build', 'ClassRoom', 'educationStage','startTeachingDate',
        'endTeachingDate', 'region', 'teacherTitle', 'title' ,'className' # 新增三个筛选参数
    ]
    supported_range_params = [
        'minTeachingAge', 'maxTeachingAge', 'minTeacherAge',
        'maxTeacherAge', 'minCourseScore', 'maxCourseScore'
    ]

    # 过滤无效参数（只保留支持的参数）
    query_params = {}
    for param in supported_base_params + supported_range_params:
        query_params[param] = request_args.get(param, '').strip()

    # 转换范围参数为数字（空值设为None）
    try:
        return {
            'base': {k: v for k, v in query_params.items() if k in supported_base_params},
            'range': {
                'min_teaching_age': int(query_params['minTeachingAge']) if query_params['minTeachingAge'] else None,
                'max_teaching_age': int(query_params['maxTeachingAge']) if query_params['maxTeachingAge'] else None,
                'min_teacher_age': int(query_params['minTeacherAge']) if query_params['minTeacherAge'] else None,
                'max_teacher_age': int(query_params['maxTeacherAge']) if query_params['maxTeacherAge'] else None,
                'min_course_score': float(query_params['minCourseScore']) if query_params['minCourseScore'] else None,
                'max_course_score': float(query_params['maxCourseScore']) if query_params['maxCourseScore'] else None,
            }
        }
    except ValueError as e:
        # 优化错误提示，明确哪个参数无效
        raise ValueError(f"范围参数格式错误：{str(e)}（教龄/年龄需为整数，得分需为小数）")
def filter_videos(videos: List[Dict[str, Any]], parsed_params: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    base_params = parsed_params['base']
    range_params = parsed_params['range']
    filtered = []

    # 日期格式：仅处理年月日（忽略时间）
    DATE_FORMAT = "%Y-%m-%d"

    # 授课日期范围检查（核心逻辑）
    def teaching_date_range_check(video_teaching_time):
        """
        仅比较日期部分：提取视频时间的年月日，与筛选日期范围匹配
        :param video_teaching_time: 视频的teachingTime字段（如"2025-08-20 10:00"）
        :return: True=日期在范围内，False=不在
        """
        start_date_str = base_params['startTeachingDate']
        end_date_str = base_params['endTeachingDate']

        # 无筛选日期时直接匹配
        if not start_date_str and not end_date_str:
            return True

        # 1. 提取视频时间的日期部分（容错处理）
        try:
            # 先按完整格式解析，再提取日期
            full_time = datetime.strptime(video_teaching_time.strip(), "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            video_date = full_time.date()  # 仅保留年月日
        except (ValueError, AttributeError):
            # 格式错误（如"2025/08/20"）或空值，视为不匹配
            return False

        # 2. 解析筛选的开始/结束日期（仅年月日）
        start_date = None
        if start_date_str:
            try:
                start_date = datetime.strptime(start_date_str.strip(), DATE_FORMAT).date()
            except ValueError:
                # 开始日期格式错误，忽略该条件
                pass

        end_date = None
        if end_date_str:
            try:
                end_date = datetime.strptime(end_date_str.strip(), DATE_FORMAT).date()
            except ValueError:
                # 结束日期格式错误，忽略该条件
                pass

        # 3. 日期范围判断（仅比较年月日）
        if start_date and end_date:
            return start_date <= video_date <= end_date
        elif start_date:
            return video_date >= start_date
        elif end_date:
            return video_date <= end_date

        return True  # 参数格式错误时视为匹配

    # 其他匹配函数（fuzzy_match/exact_match等保持不变）
    def fuzzy_match(param_key, video_key):
        nonlocal match
        param = base_params[param_key]
        if not param:
            return
        video_val = str(video.get(video_key, "")).strip()
        if not video_val or param not in video_val:
            match = False

    def exact_match(param_key, video_key):
        nonlocal match
        param = base_params[param_key]
        if not param:
            return
        video_val = str(video.get(video_key, "")).strip()
        if video_val != param:
            match = False

    def range_check_int(range_key_min, range_key_max, video_key):
        nonlocal match
        min_val = range_params[range_key_min]
        max_val = range_params[range_key_max]
        if min_val is None and max_val is None:
            return
        video_val_str = str(video.get(video_key, "")).strip()
        if not video_val_str:
            match = False
            return
        try:
            video_val = int(video_val_str)
        except ValueError:
            match = False
            return
        if min_val is not None and video_val < min_val:
            match = False
        if max_val is not None and video_val > max_val:
            match = False

    def range_check_float(range_key_min, range_key_max, video_rating_path):
        nonlocal match
        min_val = range_params[range_key_min]
        max_val = range_params[range_key_max]
        if min_val is None and max_val is None:
            return
        # 修改点2：从嵌套的teaching_rating中获取"课堂综合评分"，增加容错
        try:
            # 先获取teaching_rating字典，再取"课堂综合评分"（原数据是浮点数，直接转换为字符串）
            teaching_rating = video.get('teaching_rating', {})
            video_val = teaching_rating.get('课堂综合评分')  # 直接获取浮点数（原数据格式）
            if video_val is None:  # 若该字段缺失，视为不匹配
                match = False
                return
            video_val_str = str(video_val).strip()  # 转为字符串保持原有逻辑一致
        except AttributeError:
            # 若teaching_rating不是字典（格式错误），视为不匹配
            match = False
            return

        if not video_val_str:
            match = False
            return
        try:
            video_val = float(video_val_str)
        except ValueError:
            match = False
            return
        if min_val is not None and video_val < min_val:
            match = False
        if max_val is not None and video_val > max_val:
            match = False

    # 遍历筛选视频
    for video in videos:
        match = True
        video_id = video.get('videoId', '未知ID')

        # 授课日期范围筛选（核心）
        video_teaching_time = str(video.get('teachingTime', "")).strip()
        if not teaching_date_range_check(video_teaching_time):
            match = False

        # 其他筛选条件（保持不变）
        if match:
            # 模糊匹配
            fuzzy_match('courseName', 'courseName')
            fuzzy_match('teacherName', 'teacherName')
            fuzzy_match('school', 'school')

            # 精准匹配
            exact_match('grade', 'grade')
            exact_match('courseType', 'courseType')
            exact_match('status', 'status')
            exact_match('teaching_build', 'teaching_build')
            exact_match('ClassRoom', 'ClassRoom')
            exact_match('educationStage', 'educationStage')
            exact_match('region', 'region')
            exact_match('teacherTitle', 'teacherTitle')
            exact_match('title', 'title')
            exact_match('className', 'className')

            # 上传时间筛选
            upload_time = str(video.get('uploadTime', "")).strip()
            start_time = base_params['startUploadTime']
            end_time = base_params['endUploadTime']
            if start_time and upload_time < start_time:
                match = False
            if end_time and upload_time > end_time:
                match = False

            # 范围筛选
            if match:
                range_check_int('min_teaching_age', 'max_teaching_age', 'teachingAge')
                range_check_int('min_teacher_age', 'max_teacher_age', 'teacherAge')
                range_check_float('min_course_score', 'max_course_score', 'teaching_rating.课堂综合评分')

        if match:
            filtered.append(video)
    return filtered


def get_file_path(video_id: str) -> str:
    """根据video_id生成对应的JSON报告文件路径"""
    return os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f"{video_id}_result.json")
def calculate_average(data_list: List[float]) -> float:
    """工具函数：计算列表平均值（处理空列表情况）"""
    if not data_list or all(v == 0 for v in data_list):
        return 0.0
    valid_data = [v for v in data_list if v > 0]
    return round(sum(valid_data) / len(valid_data), 2) if valid_data else 0.0
def jsonify1(data: Dict[str, Any]):
    """自定义jsonify函数，解决中文Unicode转义问题"""
    response = flask_jsonify(data)
    response.data = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode('utf-8')
    response.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json; charset=utf-8'
    return response


# 支持多条件筛选接口 页码
# curl "http://localhost:5000/api/videos/filter?page=1&per_page=20&minTeachingAge=3&maxTeachingAge=100&minTeacherAge=0&maxTeacherAge=100&minCourseScore=10"
import math

import math

@app.route('/api/videos/filter', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 需登录才能筛选
def filter_videos_api(current_username, current_role):   # 接收当前用户和角色
    """带分页功能的视频筛选接口（复用公共筛选逻辑）"""
    # 1. 分页参数处理
    try:
        page = int(request.args.get('page', 1))
        per_page = int(request.args.get('per_page', 20))
        per_page = min(max(1, per_page), 100)  # 限制每页1-100条
    except ValueError:
        return jsonify1({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "page/per_page 必须是有效整数"
        })

    # 2. 获取原始数据（与分析接口共用数据源）
    try:
        task_history_res = get_task_history()  # 调用内部task-history接口
        task_history_json = task_history_res.get_json()
        all_videos = task_history_json.get('data', [])
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify1({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"获取任务历史数据失败：{str(e)}"
        })

    if current_role == "user":
        # 普通用户仅能查看自己上传的视频（匹配uploader字段）
        filtered_by_role = [video for video in all_videos if video.get("uploader") == current_username]
    else:
        # 超级管理员/普通管理员可查看所有视频
        filtered_by_role = all_videos

    # 新增：保存权限过滤后的总数量（这是需要展示的全部数据个数）
    total_all_items = len(filtered_by_role)

    # 3. 解析筛选参数
    try:
        parsed_params = parse_filter_params(request.args)
    except ValueError as e:
        return jsonify1({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": str(e)
        })

    # 优化：打印筛选参数日志
    print(f"📌 筛选参数：{parsed_params}")
    print(f"📌 权限过滤后视频数量（全部）：{total_all_items}")
    # 4. 筛选视频（复用公共函数）
    filtered_videos = filter_videos(filtered_by_role, parsed_params)
    # 优化：打印筛选结果日志
    print(f"📌 筛选后视频数量（当前页）：{len(filtered_videos)}")

    # 5. 排序（保持原有逻辑）
    sort_by = request.args.get('sort_by', 'videoId').strip()
    order = request.args.get('order', 'desc').strip().lower()
    valid_sort_fields = {
        'videoId': lambda x: x.get('videoId', ''),
        'courseScore': lambda x: float(x.get('courseScore', 0) or 0),
        'teachingTime': lambda x: x.get('teachingTime', ''),
        'uploadTime': lambda x: x.get('uploadTime', ''),
    }
    if sort_by not in valid_sort_fields:
        return jsonify1({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"不支持的排序字段：{sort_by}，可选值：{list(valid_sort_fields.keys())}"
        })
    if order not in ['asc', 'desc']:
        return jsonify1({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "order 参数必须为 'asc' 或 'desc'"
        })
    filtered_videos.sort(key=valid_sort_fields[sort_by], reverse=(order == 'desc'))

    # 6. 分页处理（仅对筛选后的数据分页）
    total_filtered_items = len(filtered_videos)  # 筛选后的数量（用于分页计算）
    total_pages = math.ceil(total_filtered_items / per_page) if total_filtered_items > 0 else 1
    start_idx = (page - 1) * per_page if page <= total_pages else 0
    paginated_videos = filtered_videos[start_idx:start_idx + per_page]

    # 7. 响应结果
    return jsonify1({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "data": {"items": paginated_videos},  # 筛选后的列表数据格式不变
        "dataMap": {
            "total_items": total_all_items,  # 改为：权限过滤后的全部数据个数
            "total_pages": total_pages,      # 分页仍基于筛选后的数据计算
            "current_page": page,
            "per_page": per_page,
            "note": "普通用户仅显示自己上传的视频" if current_role == "user" else "管理员显示所有用户的视频",
            "filtered_items": total_filtered_items  # 可选：补充筛选后数量，便于前端理解
        }
    })



# 细分项得分
@app.route('/api/analyze_teaching_data', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅登录用户可访问
def analyze_teaching_data(current_username, current_role):
    """
    教学数据分析接口（所有结果严格基于筛选后的数据计算）
    """
    # 1. 解析筛选参数
    try:
        parsed_params = parse_filter_params(request.args)
    except ValueError as e:
        return jsonify({"code": 400, "message": "失败", "error": str(e)}), 400

    # 2. 获取原始视频数据并按角色筛选
    try:
        task_history_res = get_task_history()
        task_history_json = task_history_res.get_json()
        all_videos = task_history_json.get('data', [])
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"error": f"获取任务历史数据失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

    # 按角色筛选（基础权限过滤）
    if current_role == "user":
        filtered_by_role = [video for video in all_videos if video.get("uploader") == current_username]
    else:
        filtered_by_role = all_videos

    # 3. 应用筛选条件（核心筛选逻辑）
    filtered_videos = filter_videos(filtered_by_role, parsed_params)  # 仅保留符合筛选条件的视频
    total_filtered = len(filtered_videos)  # 筛选后的总视频数（未去重）

    # 4. 处理筛选后的视频ID（去重）
    target_video_ids = list({video.get('videoId') for video in filtered_videos if video.get('videoId')})
    unique_filtered_count = len(target_video_ids)  # 筛选后去重的视频数

    # 5. 若无符合条件的视频，返回空结果（所有数据均为筛选后结果）
    if unique_filtered_count == 0:
        return jsonify({
            'code': 200,
            'message': '成功',
            'data': {
                'plot_data': {
                    'date': [],
                    '课堂总得分': [],
                    '师生行为得分': [],
                    '师生话语得分': [],
                    '师生情绪得分': []
                },
                'course_count': 0,  # 筛选后成功处理的视频数（0）
                '课堂平均得分': 0.0,  # 基于筛选后数据（无数据则为0）
                '平均课堂语速': 0.0,
                '平均课堂专注度': 0.0,
                'debug_info': f"筛选后共0个有效视频（原始筛选结果：{total_filtered}个，去重后0个）"
            }
        })

    # 6. 仅处理筛选后的视频数据
    course_data: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    processed_count = 0  # 筛选后成功处理的视频数

    for video_id in target_video_ids:
        file_path = get_file_path(video_id)
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            app.logger.warning(f"筛选后的视频{video_id}无报告文件：{file_path}")
            continue

        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = json.load(f)

            # 提取教学评分数据
            teaching_rating = data.get('teaching_rating', {})
            current_behavior = round(float(teaching_rating.get('关键行为得分', 0.0)), 2)
            current_discourse = round(float(teaching_rating.get('关键能力得分', 0.0)), 2)
            current_emotion = round(float(teaching_rating.get('情感体验得分', 0.0)), 2)
            current_total = calculate_average([current_behavior, current_discourse, current_emotion])

            # 提取语速数据
            current_speak_speed = 0.0
            speaking_analyze = data.get('speaking_analyze', {}).get('result', {})
            other_analysis_str = speaking_analyze.get('其他话语分析结果', '').strip()
            if other_analysis_str:
                try:
                    other_analysis = json.loads(other_analysis_str)
                    speak_speed_list = other_analysis.get('other_discourse_analysis', {}).get('speak_speed', [])
                    if isinstance(speak_speed_list, list) and speak_speed_list:
                        current_speak_speed = calculate_average([float(v) for v in speak_speed_list])
                except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError) as e:
                    app.logger.warning(f"视频{video_id}语速解析失败：{str(e)}")

            # 提取专注度数据
            current_focus = 0.0
            try:
                cv_content = data.get('CV_Recognise_content', {})
                cv_result = cv_content.get('result', {})
                if cv_result.get('code') == 0 and cv_result.get('msg') == 'ok':
                    cv_data = cv_result.get('data', {})
                    stu_raise_head = cv_data.get('stu_raiseHead_num', {})
                    stu_max_num = cv_data.get('stu_max_num', 1)
                    if stu_raise_head and stu_max_num > 0:
                        raise_head_values = [float(v) for k, v in stu_raise_head.items() if isinstance(v, (int, float))]
                        if raise_head_values:
                            avg_raise_head = calculate_average(raise_head_values)
                            current_focus = round((avg_raise_head / stu_max_num) * 100, 2)
            except Exception as e:
                app.logger.warning(f"视频{video_id}专注度解析失败：{str(e)}")

            # 提取日期（从筛选后的video_id中）
            try:
                date_str = video_id[:8]
                formatted_date = f"{date_str[:4]}-{date_str[4:6]}-{date_str[6:8]}"
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                formatted_date = "未知日期"

            course_data.append({
                'video_id': video_id,
                'date': formatted_date,
                'total_score': current_total,
                'behavior_score': current_behavior,
                'discourse_score': current_discourse,
                'emotion_score': current_emotion,
                'speak_speed_avg': current_speak_speed,
                'focus_avg': current_focus
            })
            processed_count += 1  # 仅计数筛选后成功处理的视频

        except Exception as e:
            app.logger.error(f"处理筛选后的视频{video_id}时出错：{str(e)}")
            continue

    # 7. 按日期分组计算平均值（仅用筛选后的数据）
    from collections import defaultdict
    date_groups = defaultdict(list)
    for item in course_data:
        date_groups[item['date']].append(item)

    date_stats = []
    for date in sorted(date_groups.keys()):
        videos_in_date = date_groups[date]
        date_stats.append({
            'date': date,
            'avg_total': calculate_average([v['total_score'] for v in videos_in_date]),
            'avg_behavior': calculate_average([v['behavior_score'] for v in videos_in_date]),
            'avg_discourse': calculate_average([v['discourse_score'] for v in videos_in_date]),
            'avg_emotion': calculate_average([v['emotion_score'] for v in videos_in_date])
        })

    # 8. 计算整体平均值（仅基于筛选后成功处理的数据）
    all_total_scores = [item['total_score'] for item in course_data]
    all_speak_speeds = [item['speak_speed_avg'] for item in course_data]
    all_focus_avgs = [item['focus_avg'] for item in course_data]

    # 9. 所有返回结果均基于筛选后的数据
    return jsonify({
        'code': 200,
        'message': '成功',
        'data': {
            'plot_data': {
                'date': [stat['date'] for stat in date_stats],
                '课堂总得分': [stat['avg_total'] for stat in date_stats],
                '师生行为得分': [stat['avg_behavior'] for stat in date_stats],
                '师生话语得分': [stat['avg_discourse'] for stat in date_stats],
                '师生情绪得分': [stat['avg_emotion'] for stat in date_stats]
            },
            'course_count': processed_count,  # 筛选后成功处理的视频数
            '课堂平均得分': calculate_average(all_total_scores) if all_total_scores else 0.0,
            '平均课堂语速': calculate_average(all_speak_speeds) if all_speak_speeds else 0.0,
            '平均课堂专注度': calculate_average(all_focus_avgs) if all_focus_avgs else 0.0,
            'debug_info': (
                f"筛选后总视频数：{total_filtered}个（原始结果），"
                f"去重后有效视频：{unique_filtered_count}个，"
                f"成功处理（含报告）：{processed_count}个"
            )
        }
    })



# 分析结果读取
# curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/get_json_data -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"video_id\": \"20250815213329\"}"
JSON_FILES_DIR = r".\整合结果"
@app.route('/api/get_json_data', methods=['POST'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅新增：拦截未登录用户
def get_json_data(current_username, current_role):
    try:
        # 从请求中获取video_id参数
        data = request.get_json()
        video_id = data.get('video_id')

        if not video_id:
            return jsonify({
                "code":400,
                "message":"失败",
                "error": "缺少必要参数: video_id"
            })

        # ---------------------- 仅新增：权限校验 ----------------------
        # 加载任务历史，确认视频的上传者
        def load_task_history():
            TASK_HISTORY_FILE = "./整合结果/data/task_history.json"  # 替换为你的实际路径
            try:
                with open(TASK_HISTORY_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                return {}

        task_history_dict = load_task_history()
        task_info = task_history_dict.get(video_id)

        # 普通用户仅能读取自己上传的视频结果
        if current_role == "user" and task_info:
            task_uploader = task_info.get("uploader")
            if task_uploader != current_username:
                return jsonify({
                    "code": 403,
                    "message": "失败",
                    "error": "权限不足：仅任务上传者或管理员可查看分析结果"
                })

        # 拼接完整的文件名和路径
        filename = f"{video_id}_result.json"
        file_path = os.path.join(JSON_FILES_DIR, filename)
        print("###########",file_path)

        # 检查文件是否存在
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return jsonify({
                "code": 404,
                "message": "失败",
                "error": f"文件不存在: {filename}"
            })

        # 读取JSON文件内容
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json_data = json.load(f)

        # 将JSON数据返回给前端
        return jsonify(
            {
                'code':200,
                'message':"成功",
                'data':json_data
            })

    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "文件内容不是有效的JSON格式"}), 500
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"处理请求时发生错误: {str(e)}"}), 500



# 删除
import psutil
from flask import jsonify, request
import json
# curl -X DELETE "http://localhost:5000/api/delete-video" -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"videoId\": \"20250815212652\"}"
@app.route('/api/delete-video', methods=['DELETE'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])  # 仅新增：拦截未登录用户
def delete_video(current_username, current_role):
    """
    :return:
    {
      "deleted": [
        "video"
      ],
      "msg": "视频及相关资源已成功删除",
      "success": true,
      "videoId": "20250815212652"
    }
    """
    print("=== 收到删除请求 ===")
    print("Raw body:", request.get_data())
    print("Headers:", dict(request.headers))

    # 强制解析 JSON
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    if not data:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '请求体为空或不是有效 JSON'
        })

    video_id = data.get('videoId')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '缺少 videoId'
        })

    # ---------------------- 仅新增：权限校验 ----------------------
    # 加载任务历史，确认视频的上传者
    def load_task_history():
        TASK_HISTORY_FILE = "./整合结果/data/task_history.json"  # 替换为你的实际路径
        try:
            with open(TASK_HISTORY_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            return {}

    task_history_dict = load_task_history()
    task_info = task_history_dict.get(video_id) or task_history.get(video_id)  # 同时检查内存和持久化数据

    # 普通用户仅能删除自己上传的视频
    if current_role == "user" and task_info:
        task_uploader = task_info.get("uploader")
        if task_uploader != current_username:
            return jsonify({
                "code": 403,
                "message": "失败",
                "error": "权限不足：仅任务上传者或管理员可删除视频"
            })

    print(f"正在删除 videoId: {video_id}")

    # 从任务中移除
    with task_lock:
        tasks.pop(video_id, None)
        history_record = task_history.pop(video_id, None)

    # 尝试确定文件名
    filename = None
    if history_record:
        filename = history_record.get('filename')  # 如：20250815212652_test.mp4
    else:
        # 备用：查找以 videoId 开头的视频文件
        upload_folder = app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER']
        for f in os.listdir(upload_folder):
            if f.startswith(video_id) and f.endswith(('.mp4', '.mov', '.webm', '.avi')):
                filename = f
                break

    if not filename:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': '未找到该视频的文件'
        })

    # 构建路径
    video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename)
    info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_info.json")
    thumbnail_path = os.path.join(app.config['THUMBNAIL_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_thumbnail.jpg")

    deleted_items = []
    errors = []

    # 检查文件是否被占用
    def is_file_locked(path):
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return False
        try:
            os.rename(path, path)  # 尝试重命名自己，测试是否可写
            return False
        except OSError:
            return True

    # 删除视频文件
    try:
        if os.path.exists(video_path):
            if is_file_locked(video_path):
                errors.append("视频文件正在被使用（可能被播放器、系统预览占用）")
            else:
                os.remove(video_path)
                deleted_items.append('video')
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(f"删除视频失败: {str(e)}")

    # 删除 info.json
    try:
        if os.path.exists(info_path):
            os.remove(info_path)
            deleted_items.append('info')
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(f"删除 info.json 失败: {str(e)}")

    # 删除缩略图
    try:
        if os.path.exists(thumbnail_path):
            os.remove(thumbnail_path)
            deleted_items.append('thumbnail')
    except Exception as e:
        errors.append(f"删除缩略图失败: {str(e)}")

    # 持久化 task_history
    if history_record:
        save_task_history()

    # 返回结果
    if errors:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
            "dataMap":{
                'videoId': video_id,
                'deleted': deleted_items,
                'errors': errors
                }
        })

    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "dataMap":{
            'videoId': video_id,
            'msg': '视频及相关资源已成功删除',
            'deleted': deleted_items
        }
    })

VIDEO_DIRECTORY = "./uploads"
# 确保视频目录存在
if not os.path.exists(VIDEO_DIRECTORY):
    os.makedirs(VIDEO_DIRECTORY)


# 视频下载
# http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/download-video?videoId=20250807101658
@app.route('/api/download-video', methods=['GET'])
def download_video():
    """
    通过视频ID下载视频文件
    请求参数: videoId - 视频唯一标识
    """
    # 获取前端传递的视频ID参数
    video_id = request.args.get('videoId')

    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "缺少视频ID参数：videoId"
        })

    # 查找匹配的视频文件（videoId是文件名的前缀）
    video_filename = None
    # 遍历视频目录下的所有文件
    for filename in os.listdir(VIDEO_DIRECTORY):
        # 检查文件名是否以videoId开头，且是视频文件
        if filename.startswith(video_id) and filename.lower().endswith(('.mp4', '.mov', '.webm', '.avi')):
            video_filename = filename
            break

    # 检查是否找到对应视频文件
    if not video_filename:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"未找到ID为 '{video_id}' 的视频文件"
        })

    # 构建完整视频路径
    video_path = os.path.join(VIDEO_DIRECTORY, video_filename)

    # 确定视频MIME类型
    def get_video_mimetype(filename):
        if filename.lower().endswith('.mp4'):
            return 'video/mp4'
        elif filename.lower().endswith('.mov'):
            return 'video/quicktime'
        elif filename.lower().endswith('.webm'):
            return 'video/webm'
        elif filename.lower().endswith('.avi'):
            return 'video/x-msvideo'
        return 'application/octet-stream'

    mimetype = get_video_mimetype(video_filename)

    # 发送文件供下载
    return send_from_directory(
        VIDEO_DIRECTORY,
        video_filename,
        as_attachment=True,  # 强制浏览器下载而不是播放
        mimetype=mimetype,
        download_name=video_filename  # 指定下载时的文件名
    )

师生话语分析：

师生情绪：   刘宇轩

# 视频 转 CSV文本
# curl "http://localhost:5000/api/video_to_txt?video_id=20250807103203"
# http://localhost:5000/api/video_to_txt?video_id=20250807103203
from modules.main import audio_to_data
import shutil
@app.route('/api/video_to_txt')
def video_to_txt():
    """
    :return:
    {
      "cv_speak_path": "./csv_CV_result/20250820110529_result_cv.json",
      "output_file": "./csv_results/20250820110529_result.csv"
    }
    """
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"
        })

    # 2. 检查该videoId对应的CSV是否已存在（缓存检查）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"  # 用videoId命名CSV，确保唯一
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    nlp_filename = f"{video_id}_nlp.json"
    nlp_json = os.path.join(app.config['nlp_json'], nlp_filename)


    cv_speak = f"{video_id}_result_cv.json"  # 用videoId命名json，确保唯一
    cv_speak_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_SPEAK'], cv_speak)

    # 确保对话文本和CV数据都存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        if os.path.exists(cv_speak_path):
            if os.path.exists(nlp_json):
                info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_info.json")
                video_path = ""
                if os.path.exists(info_path):
                    with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        video_info = json.load(f)
                    video_filename = video_info.get('filename')
                    if video_filename:
                        video_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], video_filename))
            # 已存在，直接返回缓存的CSV路径
                return jsonify({
                    "code": 400,
                    "message": "失败",
                    "output_file": os.path.abspath(csv_path),
                    "cv_speak_path":os.path.abspath(cv_speak_path),
                    "nlp_json":os.path.abspath(nlp_json),
                    "video_path":video_path
                }
                )


    # 3. 缓存不存在，需要生成：通过videoId找到原始视频路径
    # 3.1 读取视频信息文件（upload接口保存的info.json）
    info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_info.json")
    if not os.path.exists(info_path):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"视频不存在（videoId：{video_id}）"}
        )


    # 3.2 从info.json中获取视频文件名，构造视频路径
    with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        video_info = json.load(f)
    video_filename = video_info.get('filename')
    if not video_filename:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"视频信息损坏（videoId：{video_id}）"
        })

    video_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], video_filename)
    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": f"视频文件已删除（videoId：{video_id}）"
        })


    # 4. 调用工具生成CSV（仅第一次执行）
    # 阿里云api
    LLM_API_KEY = api_key1


    audio_processor = audio_to_data(APPID, SECRET_KEY, video_path, LLM_API_KEY)
    temp_csv_path = audio_processor.process_audio_file()  # 临时CSV路径

    print("##############",temp_csv_path)
    # 5. 将生成的CSV保存到缓存目录（用videoId命名，便于后续查找）
    shutil.copy(temp_csv_path[3], nlp_json)  # nlp 的json文件
    shutil.copy(temp_csv_path[1], csv_path)  # 复制到目标路径
    shutil.copy(temp_csv_path[2], cv_speak_path)  # 复制到目标路径
    print(f"视频转CSV完成（videoId：{video_id}），speak保存路径：{csv_path}")
    print(f"视频转json完成（videoId：{video_id}），cv_speak保存路径：{cv_speak_path}")
    print(f"视频转json完成（videoId：{video_id}），nlp_json保存路径：{nlp_json}")
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "output_file": os.path.abspath(csv_path),
        "cv_speak_path":os.path.abspath(cv_speak_path),
        "nlp_json":os.path.abspath(nlp_json),
        "video_path":os.path.abspath(video_path)
    })




# 将CSV文件内容传递到前端
# 师生话语 对话内容 video_id
import csv
@app.route('/api/csv-data')
def get_csv_data():
    """专门用于提供CSV数据的接口"""
    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "失败",
            "error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"})

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        input_file = csv_path
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
                "error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"})

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({
                "code": 500,
                "message": "失败",
                "error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"})

        input_file = txt_csv_path['output_file']


    # 检查CSV文件是否存在
    if not os.path.exists(input_file):
        return jsonify({
            "code": 404,
            'status': 'error',
            'message': f'未找到CSV文件 {input_file}'
        })

    # 读取CSV文件内容
    data = []
    try:
        with open(input_file, mode='r', encoding='utf-8') as file:
            csv_reader = csv.reader(file)
            # 获取表头
            headers = next(csv_reader)
            # 获取数据行
            rows = [row for row in csv_reader]

            data = {
                'headers': headers,
                'rows': rows
            }

        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": "成功",
            'data': data
        })
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 500,
            "message": "失败",
            'error': f'读取CSV文件失败: {str(e)}'
        })



import pandas as pd
EXCEL_FILE = "编码结果表格.xlsx"
# 将excel文件内容传递到前端
@app.route('/api/excel-data')
def get_excel_data():
    """提供Excel数据的API接口"""
    try:
        # 检查Excel文件是否存在
        if not os.path.exists(EXCEL_FILE):
            return jsonify({
                'status': 'error',
                'message': f'未找到Excel文件 {EXCEL_FILE}'
            }), 404

        # 使用pandas读取Excel文件
        # 支持.xlsx和.xls格式
        df = pd.read_excel(EXCEL_FILE)

        # 将数据转换为字典格式
        headers = df.columns.tolist()
        rows = df.values.tolist()

        # 返回JSON格式数据
        return jsonify({
            'status': 'success',
            'data': {
                'headers': headers,
                'rows': rows,
                'sheet_name': 'Sheet1',  # 默认第一个工作表
                'row_count': len(rows),
                'column_count': len(headers)
            }
        })

    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            'status': 'error',
            'message': f'处理Excel文件时出错: {str(e)}'
        }), 500

from modules.excel_column import excel_column_to_txt
from modules.bsd.content_sumary import ClassNoteSummarizer
@app.route('/api/content_sumary')
def content_sumary():

    API_KEY = api_key1  # 请替换为你的实际API密钥
    # 创建总结器实例
    summarizer = ClassNoteSummarizer(api_key=API_KEY)

    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        input_file = csv_to_excel(csv_path)
        input_txt_path = excel_column_to_txt(input_file, "内容")
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500

        # 转换为Excel文件
        input_file = csv_to_excel(txt_csv_path['output_file'])
        input_txt_path = excel_column_to_txt(input_file, "内容")


    try:
        # 处理口水稿文件
        # file_path = r".\modules\bsd\draft\mmexport1747634869973_processed_full_transcript.txt"
        file_path = input_txt_path
        response = summarizer.get_result(file_path)["content_summary"]
        print(response)
        result = {"response": response}
        return jsonify(result)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"处理失败: {str(e)}")
        return jsonify({"result":str(e)})

from modules.bsd.calculate_weight import AHPWeightCalculator  # 计算权重
from modules.bsd.calculate_score import ClassroomScorer  # 计算评分
@app.route('/api/calculate_score')
def calculate_score():
    # 接收CSV文件路径参数
    # teacher_behavior_csv = request.args.get('teacher_behavior_csv')
    discourse_form_csv = request.args.get('discourse_form_csv')
    discourse_function_csv = request.args.get('discourse_function_csv')
    teacher_emotion_csv = request.args.get('teacher_emotion_csv')

    # 检查必要参数
    # if not all([teacher_behavior_csv, discourse_form_csv, discourse_function_csv, teacher_emotion_csv]):
    if not all([discourse_form_csv, discourse_function_csv, teacher_emotion_csv]):
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少必要的CSV文件路径参数"}), 400

    # 计算权重
    data_file = r".\modules\bsd\test\ap_test\ap_test_1.json"
    calculator = AHPWeightCalculator(file_path=data_file)
    # 加载数据
    experts_data = calculator.load_data()
    # 处理所有专家数据
    results = calculator.process_all_experts(experts_data)
    # 保存结果
    output_file = calculator.save_results(results)
    print(output_file)


    csv_files = {
        '话语形式': discourse_form_csv,
        '话语功能': discourse_function_csv,
        '师生情绪': teacher_emotion_csv
    }

    weight_file_path = output_file

    try:
        # 创建评分器实例
        scorer = ClassroomScorer(csv_files=csv_files, weight_data=weight_file_path)
        # 打印详细报告
        result = scorer.print_detailed_report()
        print(result)
        return jsonify(result)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"程序执行错误: {e}")
        return jsonify({"result": str(e)})

@app.route('/api/generate_radar_chart')
def generate_radar_chart():
    """
    主函数，用于打印详细信息并保存图片
    """
    # 计算权重
    data_file = r".\modules\bsd\test\ap_test\ap_test_1.json"
    calculator = AHPWeightCalculator(file_path=data_file)
    # 加载数据
    experts_data = calculator.load_data()
    # 处理所有专家数据
    results = calculator.process_all_experts(experts_data)
    # 保存结果
    output_file = calculator.save_results(results)
    print(output_file)

    # 计算评分
    csv_files = {
        '师生行为': r".\modules\bsd\doc\师生行为.csv",
        '话语形式': r".\modules\bsd\doc\话语形式.csv",
        '话语功能': r".\modules\bsd\doc\话语功能.csv",
        '师生情绪': r".\modules\bsd\doc\师生情绪.csv"
    }
    # weight_file_path = r'.\modules\bsd\result\weight\local_weight_vectors_20250801_115218.json'
    weight_file_path = output_file
    scorer = ClassroomScorer(csv_files=csv_files, weight_file_path=weight_file_path)
    scores = scorer.get_scores()


    save_dir = r".\radar_chart"
    generator = RadarChartGenerator(save_dir=save_dir)
    fig, overall_score, save_path = generator.generate_and_save(scores=scores)
    # 关闭图形以释放内存
    plt.close(fig)


    result = {"雷达图片保存路径":save_path}
    print(result)

    # return jsonify(result)
    return send_file(save_path, mimetype='image/jpeg')

    }

    # data_file = r".\modules\bsd\test\ap_test\ap_test_1.json"
    # calculator = AHPWeightCalculator(file_path=data_file)
    # # 加载数据
    # experts_data = calculator.load_data()

    # results = calculator.process_all_experts(experts_data)
    # # 保存结果
    # output_file = calculator.save_results(results)
    # print(output_file)

    # weight_file_path = r'.\modules\bsd\result\weight\local_weight_vectors_20250801_115218.json'
    # weight_file_path = output_file

##################
    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    if os.path.exists(csv_path):

            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",

        )

            videos_response.raise_for_status()

            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()

        file_path1 = input_txt_path

    try:
        # 处理口水稿文件

        print(response)

        return jsonify({"result": str(e)})



# 五、话语功能
# 知识理解、个人表达、表达交流、实践应用、创造迁移、数据解读
# curl "http://localhost:5000/api/discourse?video_id=20250807103203"
from modules.discourse_analyzer import DiscourseAnalyzer111
@app.route('/api/discourse', methods=['GET'])
def discourse():
    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        input_file = csv_to_excel(csv_path)
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500

        # 转换为Excel文件
        input_file = csv_to_excel(txt_csv_path['output_file'])

    # 4. 执行分析逻辑（原有逻辑不变）
    analyzer = DiscourseAnalyzer111(
        api_key=api_key1,
        base_url="https://dashscope.aliyuncs.com/compatible-mode/v1",
        excel_path=input_file,
        output_dir="./分析结果"
    )

    try:
        results = analyzer.analyze()
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"error": f"分析过程出错：{str(e)}"}), 500

    return jsonify(results)

    if not video_id:

            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500

        input_file = csv_to_excel(txt_csv_path['output_file'])


    # 创建分析器实例并执行分析
    analyzer = ClassroomDialogueAnalyzer111(
        api_key=API_KEY,
        base_url=BASE_URL,
        input_file=input_file,
        output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR
    )

    results = analyzer.run_analysis()

    print("\n分析结果文件:")
    for key, path in results.items():
        print(f"{key}: {path}")

    summary_csv = csv_to_json(results['summary_csv'])
    with open(results['analysis_json'], 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        analysis_json = json.load(f)
    # analysis_json = json.load(results['analysis_json'])
    result = {"summary_csv":summary_csv,"analysis_json":analysis_json}
    return jsonify(result)



# 五、其他话语分析
# 语速、关键词、口头语、违规语、数据解读
# curl "http://localhost:5000/api/other_discourse_get?video_id=20250807103203"
import modules.other_discourse as od
@app.route('/api/other_discourse_get')
def other_discourse_get():
    """
    json格式字符串：
    speak_speed: 按顺序，课堂每一分钟的教师发言字数
    proportion: 学生发言在课堂中的占比
    keywords: 课堂关键字 - 频次
    orals: 课堂教师口头用语 - 频次
    forbidden: 违禁词
    conclusion: 结论
    :return:
    {
      "other_discourse_analysis": {
        "speak_speed": [
          222, 74, 148, 185, 37, 74, 163, 228, 76, 124, 175, 66, 187, 52, 27, 149, 169, 146, 157, 179, 68, 105, 211, 202, 193, 63, 32, 48, 36, 76, 71, 238, 211, 123, 179, 15, 26, 27, 127, 160, 30, 127, 126, 82, 175, 198
        ],
        "proportion": "18.31%",
        "keywords": {
          "直线": 25,
          "平面": 18,
          "垂直": 16,
          "定义": 8,
          "相交": 6,
          "旗杆": 5,
          "地面": 5,
          "判定": 5,
          "符号": 4,
          "语言": 4
        },
        "orals": {
          "大家": 12,
          "我们": 10,
          "什么": 9,
          "那么": 8,
          "一个": 6,
          "这个": 6,
          "可以": 5,
          "所以": 5,
          "就是": 5,
          "两条": 5
        },
        "forbidden": [],
        "conclusion": "对该节课的话语进行分析后可见，该节课教师语速波动较大，既有快速和中速的部分，也有较多慢速甚至极慢速的情况。学生发言占比为18.31%，相对较低，可能表明课堂以教师讲授为主。关键词中“直线”和“平面”出现频率最高，表明这是本节课的核心内容。口头语中“大家”和“我们”使用较多，有助于拉近师生距离。未检测到违规语的使用。整体来看，课堂内容围绕直线与平面的垂直关系展开，教师通过多种方法和实例进行讲解，学生参与度有待提高。"
      }
    }
    """

    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        input_file = csv_path
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500
        # 得到CSV文件
        input_file = txt_csv_path['output_file']


    # r'D:\soft\pycharm\code\pythonProject\1_10bsd项目\1_整体接口\mmexport1747634869973_fix.csv',
    result = od.other_discourse_get(
        input_file,
        'sk-e8cbdfdf5f8841aa96ab1d2b7a81aeb4')

    print(result)
    results = {"results":result}
    return jsonify(results)



# 五、师生话语分析（整合结果）
# http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/discourse_analysis?video_id=20250815203212
from modules.src.discourse_form import ClassroomDialogueAnalyzer111
from modules.src.discourse_function import DiscourseAnalyzer111
from modules.src.other_discourse.AiAnalyze import other_discourse_get
@app.route('/api/discourse_analysis')
def discourse_analysis():

    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        INPUT_FILE = csv_path
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )

        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500
        # 得到CSV文件
        INPUT_FILE = txt_csv_path['output_file']



    API_KEY = api_key1
    # INPUT_FILE = r"D:\soft\pycharm\code\pythonProject\1_10bsd项目\1_整体接口\modules\result\20250730184852_课堂视频_processed_sentences.csv"
    OUTPUT_DIR1 = r".\话语形式分析结果"
    OUTPUT_DIR2 = r".\话语功能分析结果"
    # OUTPUT_DIR3 = r".\其他话语分析结果"

    # 话语形式分析
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str1 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    discourse_form = ClassroomDialogueAnalyzer111(
        api_key=API_KEY,
        input_file=INPUT_FILE,
        output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR1,
        time_stamp = video_id
    )

    discourse_form_results, discourse_form_json = discourse_form.run_analysis()

    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str2 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

    print("\n话语形式分析结果文件:")
    print("话语形式_分类对话",discourse_form_results["classified_file"])
    print("话语形式_总结",discourse_form_results["summary_csv"])
    print("话语形式_返回文本",discourse_form_results["analysis_json"])

    for key, path in discourse_form_results.items():
        print(f"{key}: {path}")

        # classified_file: .\话语形式分析结果\话语形式_分类对话_20250821_214019.csv
        # summary_csv: .\话语形式分析结果\话语形式_总结_20250821_214019.csv
        # analysis_json: .\话语形式分析结果\话语形式_返回文本_20250821_214019.json
    print("\n=== 话语形式分析结果 ===")
    print(discourse_form_json)

    # 话语功能分析
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str3 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

    discourse_function = DiscourseAnalyzer111(
        api_key=API_KEY,
        csv_path=INPUT_FILE,
        output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR2,
        time_stamp=video_id
    )
    discourse_function_results = discourse_function.analyze()
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str4 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print("分类后文本：",discourse_function_results['a1'])
    print("分类结果汇总：",discourse_function_results['a2'])
    print("返回文本：",discourse_function_results['a3'])
    #     print(results["a1"])  # 分类后文本 _20250821_095304.txt
    #     print(results["a2"])  # 分类结果汇总_20250821_095304.csv
    #     print(results["a3"])  # 返回文本_20250821_095304.json
    print("\n=== 话语功能分析结果 ===")
    print(discourse_function_results["discourse_form"])

    # 其他话语分析
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str5 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    other_discourse_result = other_discourse_get(
        api_key=API_KEY,
        path=INPUT_FILE,
    )
    current_time = datetime.now()
    time_str6 = current_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print("\n=== 其他话语分析结果 ===")
    print(other_discourse_result)


    result = {
              "话语形式分析结果":discourse_form_json,
              "话语功能分析结果":discourse_function_results["discourse_form"],
              "其他话语分析结果":other_discourse_result,
              "话语形式分析开始时间":time_str1,
              "话语形式分析结束时间":time_str2,
              "话语功能分析开始时间":time_str3,
              "话语功能分析结束时间":time_str4,
              "其他话语分析开始时间":time_str5,
              "其他话语分析结束时间":time_str6
              }
    csv_data = {
        "话语形式csv":discourse_form_results["summary_csv"],
        "话语功能csv":discourse_function_results['a2']
    }
    return jsonify({
        "result":result,
        'csv_data':csv_data
        })
    # print("话语形式_分类对话",discourse_form_results["classified_file"])
    # print("话语形式_总结",discourse_form_results["summary_csv"])
