# 五个时间段
import unicodedata
from openai import OpenAI

ALLOWED_CSV_ROOT = "./csv_results"  # 允许访问的CSV根目录
TARGET_SEGMENTS = [
    "课堂导入",
    "新知探究",
    "练习巩固",
    "课堂小结"
]
EXPECTED_COLUMNS = ["时间", "角色", "内容"]  # 预期的列名

def clean_column_name(col_name: str) -> str:
    """
    清洗列名：去除不可见字符，标准化格式
    :param col_name: 原始列名
    :return: 清洗后的列名
    """
    if not col_name:
        return ""

    # 1. 去除前后空格、制表符
    cleaned = col_name.strip(" \t")

    # 2. 去除换行符、回车符
    cleaned = cleaned.replace("\n", "").replace("\r", "")

    # 3. 过滤Unicode控制字符（保留可打印字符）
    # 原理：通过unicodedata.category判断字符类型，去除"Cc"（控制字符）和"Cf"（格式字符）
    cleaned = "".join(
        char for char in cleaned
        if unicodedata.category(char) not in ("Cc", "Cf")
    )

    # 4. 全角空格转半角空格，再去除空格（避免"时 间"这类带空格的列名）
    cleaned = cleaned.replace("　", " ").replace(" ", "")

    # 5. 去除特殊字符（如全角标点、符号，按需调整）
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^\u4e00-\u9fa5a-zA-Z0-9]", "", cleaned)

    return cleaned
def validate_file_path(file_path: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """验证文件路径合法性（复用原有逻辑）"""
    try:
        abs_file_path = os.path.abspath(file_path)
        abs_allowed_root = os.path.abspath(ALLOWED_CSV_ROOT)

        if not abs_file_path.startswith(abs_allowed_root + os.sep):
            return False, f"路径不允许：仅支持 {ALLOWED_CSV_ROOT} 目录下的文件"
        if not os.path.exists(abs_file_path):
            return False, f"文件不存在：{abs_file_path}"
        if not os.path.isfile(abs_file_path):
            return False, f"不是文件：{abs_file_path}"
        if not abs_file_path.endswith(".csv"):
            return False, "文件格式错误：仅支持CSV文件"
        if not os.access(abs_file_path, os.R_OK):
            return False, f"文件不可读：{abs_file_path}（检查权限）"

        return True, abs_file_path
    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"路径验证失败：{str(e)}"
def read_and_parse_csv(abs_file_path: str) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
    """
    读取CSV并解析：新增列名清洗步骤
    :param abs_file_path: CSV绝对路径
    :return: (格式化文本/None, 错误信息)
    """
    try:
        # 尝试utf-8编码读取
        try:
            with open(abs_file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                # 先读取第一行获取原始列名（不直接用DictReader，便于清洗）
                first_line = f.readline()
                if not first_line:
                    return None, "CSV文件为空"

                # 解析原始列名（支持逗号分隔）
                raw_columns = [col.strip() for col in first_line.split(",")]
                # 清洗列名
                cleaned_columns = [clean_column_name(col) for col in raw_columns]

                # 调试日志：打印清洗前后的列名（开发环境可开启）
                app.logger.debug(f"原始列名：{raw_columns}")
                app.logger.debug(f"清洗后列名：{cleaned_columns}")

                # 校验清洗后的列名是否匹配预期
                if sorted(cleaned_columns) != sorted(EXPECTED_COLUMNS):
                    return None, f"CSV列名不匹配：预期{EXPECTED_COLUMNS}，实际清洗后为{cleaned_columns}（可能存在不可见字符）"

                # 重新定位文件指针，用DictReader读取数据（指定清洗后的列名）
                f.seek(0)
                reader = csv.DictReader(f, fieldnames=EXPECTED_COLUMNS)
                next(reader)  # 跳过第一行（已解析列名）
                rows = [row for row in reader if any(row.values())]  # 过滤空行

        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            # fallback到gbk编码（同上逻辑）
            with open(abs_file_path, "r", encoding="gbk") as f:
                first_line = f.readline()
                if not first_line:
                    return None, "CSV文件为空"

                raw_columns = [col.strip() for col in first_line.split(",")]
                cleaned_columns = [clean_column_name(col) for col in raw_columns]

                app.logger.debug(f"原始列名（gbk）：{raw_columns}")
                app.logger.debug(f"清洗后列名（gbk）：{cleaned_columns}")

                if sorted(cleaned_columns) != sorted(EXPECTED_COLUMNS):
                    return None, f"CSV列名不匹配：预期{EXPECTED_COLUMNS}，实际清洗后为{cleaned_columns}（可能存在不可见字符）"

                f.seek(0)
                reader = csv.DictReader(f, fieldnames=EXPECTED_COLUMNS)
                next(reader)
                rows = [row for row in reader if any(row.values())]

        # 格式化内容（按时间排序）
        formatted_content = "视频文本内容（按时间排序）：\n"
        for row in rows:
            # 确保时间、角色、内容字段不为空（避免脏数据）
            time = row["时间"].strip() if row["时间"] else "未知时间"
            role = row["角色"].strip() if row["角色"] else "未知角色"
            content = row["内容"].strip() if row["内容"] else "无内容"
            formatted_content += f"时间：{time} | 角色：{role} | 内容：{content}\n"

        return formatted_content, ""

    except Exception as e:
        return None, f"CSV处理失败：{str(e)}"
def call_qwen_model(api_key: str, csv_content: str) -> dict:
    result_template = {seg: "无" for seg in TARGET_SEGMENTS}

    try:
        client = OpenAI(
            api_key=api_key,
            base_url="https://dashscope.aliyuncs.com/compatible-mode/v1",
        )
    except Exception as e:
        return {**result_template, "error": f"客户端初始化失败：{str(e)}"}

    # 🌟 强制四段连续切分的 Prompt
    prompt = f"""
你是一名课堂教学结构分析专家。请将以下整节课视频文本**严格划分为四个连续、无间隙、无重叠的教学阶段**，每个阶段必须对应一个固定环节，顺序不可更改。

【四个阶段及其典型特征】
1. 课堂导入：课程开始部分（通常含情境创设、提问、复习、目标展示等），必须从 0:00 开始。
2. 新知探究：教师讲解新知识、引导学生探究、实验、讨论核心概念。
3. 练习巩固：学生做题、小组练习、教师讲评、应用训练等。
4. 课堂小结：总结知识点、强调重点、布置作业、结束语等，必须延伸到视频最后一刻。

【硬性规则】
✅ 必须输出全部四个环节，每个环节必须有时间段，禁止出现“无”。
✅ 时间段必须连续：前一段的结束时间 = 后一段的开始时间。
✅ 第一段必须从 "0:00" 开始，最后一段必须以视频中最后一个时间戳结束。
✅ 时间格式："开始-结束"，如 "0:00-2:30" 或 "1:05:20-1:10:45"。
✅ 时间只能来自文本中的实际时间点（可取最近邻值），但必须覆盖全程。
✅ 仅输出 JSON，键为四个环节名称，值为时间段字符串，无任何其他内容。

【视频文本内容】
{csv_content}
"""

    try:
        completion = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="qwen-plus",
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": "你只输出符合上述规则的JSON，不加任何解释或额外字符。"},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
            ],
            extra_body={"enable_thinking": False},
            temperature=0.0,
            timeout=30
        )

        raw_response = completion.choices[0].message.content.strip()

        # 清洗可能的 Markdown
        if raw_response.startswith("```json"):
            raw_response = raw_response[7:]
        if raw_response.endswith("```"):
            raw_response = raw_response[:-3]
        raw_response = raw_response.strip()

        try:
            model_result = json.loads(raw_response)
            filtered_result = {
                seg: model_result.get(seg, "无")
                for seg in TARGET_SEGMENTS
            }

            # 验证是否四个都有（非"无"）
            for seg in TARGET_SEGMENTS:
                if filtered_result[seg] == "无":
                    return {**result_template, "error": f"模型未返回{seg}的时间段", "raw_response": raw_response[:150]}

            # 验证时间格式和连续性
            time_pattern = re.compile(r'^(\d+:\d+|\d+:\d+:\d+)-(\d+:\d+|\d+:\d+:\d+)$')
            segments = []
            for seg in TARGET_SEGMENTS:
                ts = filtered_result[seg]
                if not time_pattern.match(ts):
                    return {**result_template, "error": f"{seg}时间格式错误: {ts}"}
                start, end = ts.split('-')
                if not is_valid_time_order(start, end):
                    return {**result_template, "error": f"{seg}时间顺序错误: {ts}"}
                segments.append((start, end))

            # 检查连续性：前一段end == 后一段start
            for i in range(1, len(segments)):
                prev_end = segments[i-1][1]
                curr_start = segments[i][0]
                if prev_end != curr_start:
                    # 可选：自动修正 or 报错
                    return {**result_template, "error": f"时间段不连续：{segments[i-1][1]} → {segments[i][0]}"}

            return filtered_result

        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return {**result_template, "error": "JSON解析失败", "raw_response": raw_response[:150]}

    except Exception as e:
        return {**result_template, "error": f"模型调用失败: {str(e)}"}
def is_valid_time_order(start_time: str, end_time: str) -> bool:
    """
    验证开始时间是否早于结束时间

    :param start_time: 开始时间字符串（如"0:04"或"1:20:30"）
    :param end_time: 结束时间字符串（如"0:56"或"1:45:20"）
    :return: 时间顺序是否有效
    """
    try:
        # 转换为秒数进行比较
        start_seconds = time_to_seconds(start_time)
        end_seconds = time_to_seconds(end_time)
        return start_seconds <= end_seconds
    except ValueError:
        return False
def time_to_seconds(time_str: str) -> int:
    """
    将时间字符串转换为总秒数，支持"分:秒"或"时:分:秒"格式

    :param time_str: 时间字符串
    :return: 总秒数
    :raises ValueError: 格式错误时抛出异常
    """
    parts = list(map(int, time_str.split(':')))
    if len(parts) == 2:  # 分:秒
        return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
    elif len(parts) == 3:  # 时:分:秒
        return parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"无效的时间格式: {time_str}")


@app.route("/api/analyze-teaching-segments", methods=["GET"])
def analyze_teaching_segments():
    """
    教学环节分析接口（GET请求+列名清洗）
    URL参数：api_key（必填）、file_path（必填）
    示例：http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/analyze-teaching-segments?api_key=sk-xxx&file_path=./allowed_csvs/video.csv
    """
    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        # 直接使用已存在的CSV文件
        input_file = csv_path
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        # 调用视频转CSV接口生成文件
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        # 处理接口调用错误
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        # 检查返回结果是否包含有效路径
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500

        input_file = txt_csv_path['output_file']

    # 1. 校验URL参数
    api_key = api_key1
    file_path = input_file

    # 2. 验证文件路径
    is_valid_path, path_msg = validate_file_path(file_path)
    if not is_valid_path:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": f"路径错误：{path_msg}",
            "result": {}
        }), 400
    abs_file_path = path_msg

    # 3. 读取并解析CSV（含列名清洗）
    csv_content, csv_msg = read_and_parse_csv(abs_file_path)
    if not csv_content:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": f"CSV解析错误：{csv_msg}",
            "result": {}
        }), 400

    # 4. 调用模型分析
    result = call_qwen_model(api_key, csv_content)

    # 5. 返回结果
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "分析成功",
        "data": result
    }), 200

# CV
# 读取JSON文件 视频文件
def read_json_file(file_path):
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as file:
            data = json.load(file)
            return data
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"文件 {file_path} 未找到")
        return None
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        print(f"JSON解析错误: {e}")
        return None

@app.route('/api/cv_behavior_emo')
def cv_behavior_emo():
    query_dict = {
        "videoUrl": r"C:\Users\Administrator\Desktop\test.mp4",
        "videoText": {
            "sentenceContent": "下课",
            "beginTime": 5,
            "endTime": 6,
            "role": "1",
            "roleName": "老师"
        },
        "videoID": "a4adeb78808b4381b569d96bd2c72c09",
        "aiCodes": [
            {"aiCode": "TEACHER_ACTION", "frameInterval": 1},
            {"aiCode": "TEACHER_ATTENTION", "frameInterval": 1},
            {"aiCode": "TEACHER_TRAJ", "frameInterval": 1},
            {"aiCode": "STU_EXPRESSION", "frameInterval": 1},
        ]
    }

    video_path = request.args.get('video_path')
    audio_text_file_path = request.args.get('cv_speak_path')


    # 替换为你的音频 JSON 文件路径
    # audio_text_file_path = r'D:\pycharm\project_bsd\pythonProject4\csv_CV_result\20250825130432_result_cv.json'
    audio_text_data = read_json_file(audio_text_file_path)
    print(f"读取 JSON 结果: {audio_text_data is not None}")  # 检查是否读取成功
    if audio_text_data:
        query_dict['videoText'] = audio_text_data['videoText']
        # query_dict['videoUrl'] = r'C:\Users\Administrator\Desktop\test.mp4'
        query_dict['videoUrl'] = video_path

    # ---------------------------
    # 通过 HTTP POST 调用 FastAPI 服务
    # ---------------------------
    url = "http://127.0.0.1:8090/analyze"  # 服务地址
    response = requests.post(url, json=query_dict)

    if response.status_code == 200:
        result = response.json()
        print("调用成功:", json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        print(result)
        with open("./CV结果/1.json",'w',encoding='utf8') as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(result['result'], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return jsonify({"result": result,"cv_path":"./CV结果/1.json"})
    else:
        print("调用失败:", response.status_code, response.text)
        return jsonify({"response.status_code":response.status_code,"response.text":response.text})

import json
import os
import shutil
from flask import jsonify, request, send_from_directory
import requests

from modules.tea_stu_behavior import behavior_analyze

# 目标目录（可配置）
OUTPUT_DIR = './师生情绪与行为'
@app.route('/api/teach_stu_behavior')
def call_module4():
    # 确保目标目录存在
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    cv_path = request.args.get('cv_path')

    # with open(r'.\modules\tea_stu_behavior\input\cv_result.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as file:
    with open(cv_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as file:
        cv_obj = json.load(file)


    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致）
    csv_filename = f"{video_id}_result.csv"
    csv_path = os.path.join(app.config['CSV_FOLDER'], csv_filename)

    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
        print(f"CSV文件已存在（videoId：{video_id}），直接使用缓存：{csv_path}")
        input_file = csv_path
    else:
        print(f"CSV文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}），调用接口生成")
        videos_response = requests.get(
            "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/video_to_txt",
            params={"video_id": video_id}
        )
        try:
            videos_response.raise_for_status()
        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:
            return jsonify({"error": f"调用视频转文本接口失败：{str(e)}"}), 500

        txt_csv_path = videos_response.json()
        if not txt_csv_path or 'output_file' not in txt_csv_path:
            return jsonify({"error": "视频转文本接口返回格式错误"}), 500

        input_file = txt_csv_path['output_file']

    # 调用行为分析模块
    result = behavior_analyze(api_key=api_key1,
                              class_audio_csv=input_file,
                              cv_obj_json=cv_obj)
    result_dict = json.loads(result)

    # === 处理两个CSV文件：重命名并移动到目标目录 ===
    updated_result = result_dict.copy()  # 保留原始结果

    ls = []
    for key in ['teach_student_emotions', 'teach_student_behavior']:
        if key in result_dict and result_dict[key]:
            src_path = result_dict[key]
            if not os.path.exists(src_path):
                print(f"警告：文件不存在，跳过处理 {key}: {src_path}")
                continue

            # 提取原文件名
            filename = os.path.basename(src_path)
            # 添加 video_id 前缀
            new_filename = f"{video_id}_{filename}"
            dst_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, new_filename)

            # 复制文件（保留原文件）
            shutil.copy2(src_path, dst_path)
            print(f"已复制并重命名文件: {src_path} -> {dst_path}")

            # 更新返回的路径为新路径
            updated_result[key] = dst_path
            ls.append(dst_path)
    print("@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@",ls[0])
    print(ls[1])
    # 返回更新后的结果
    return jsonify({
        "code": 200,
        "message": "成功",
        "data": updated_result,
        "teach_student_emotions":ls[0],
        "teach_student_behavior":ls[1]
    })



""" 多接口调用 """

# 任务步骤映射：接口标识 → 中文名称（与你需要的任务名称对应）
TASK_STEPS = {
    "video_to_txt": "提取语音和文本",
    "cv_behavior_emo": "CV师生情绪行为",
    "discourse_analysis": "话语分析",
    "teach_stu_behavior": "师生情绪与师生行为",
    "content_sumary": "教学内容总结",
    "calculate_score": "教学评分",
    "comprehensive_assessment": "综合评价",
    "problem_improvement": "问题改进"
}

# 任务进度存储（线程安全）
# 格式：{video_id: {step_key: {"status": "排队中/分析中/分析完成/失败", "start_time": "...", "end_time": "..."}, ...}}
task_progress = {}
# 进度锁（确保多线程读写安全）
progress_lock = Lock()

TASK_PROGRESS_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "整合结果", "data", "task-process.json")
def ensure_dir(path):
    """确保目录存在，不存在则创建"""
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
def save_task_progress():
    """将当前task_progress保存到JSON文件"""
    try:
        # 确保存储目录存在
        ensure_dir(os.path.dirname(TASK_PROGRESS_FILE))

        # 加锁保证数据一致性（防止写入时被其他线程修改）
        with progress_lock:
            # 深拷贝避免序列化时的线程冲突（可选，视数据量而定）
            data_to_save = json.dumps(task_progress, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

            # 写入文件
            with open(TASK_PROGRESS_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(data_to_save)
        # print(f"✅ 任务进度已保存到 {TASK_PROGRESS_FILE}")  # 调试用

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ 保存任务进度失败: {str(e)}")
def load_task_progress():
    """从JSON文件加载任务进度到task_progress"""
    global task_progress  # 声明操作全局变量
    try:
        if not os.path.exists(TASK_PROGRESS_FILE):
            print(f"⚠️ 任务进度文件不存在，将创建新文件: {TASK_PROGRESS_FILE}")
            return

        # 读取文件内容
        with open(TASK_PROGRESS_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)

        # 加锁更新全局task_progress
        with progress_lock:
            task_progress = data  # 直接覆盖现有内存数据
        print(f"✅ 已从 {TASK_PROGRESS_FILE} 加载任务进度，共 {len(task_progress)} 个任务")

    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        print(f"❌ 任务进度文件损坏，将重新创建: {TASK_PROGRESS_FILE}")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ 加载任务进度失败: {str(e)}")



from datetime import datetime
# 后台处理线程函数
def init_task_progress(video_id):
    """初始化任务进度跟踪"""
    with progress_lock:
        task_progress[video_id] = {
            step_key: {
                "task_name": TASK_STEPS[step_key],
                "status": "排队中",
                "start_time": None,
                "end_time": None,
                "api_url": f"http://localhost:5000/api/{step_key}"
            } for step_key in TASK_STEPS
        }
    save_task_progress()  # 初始化后立即保存
def update_task_step(video_id, step_key, status, start_time=None, end_time=None):
    """更新任务步骤状态"""
    with progress_lock:
        if video_id in task_progress and step_key in task_progress[video_id]:
            task_progress[video_id][step_key]["status"] = status
            if start_time:
                task_progress[video_id][step_key]["start_time"] = start_time
            if end_time:
                task_progress[video_id][step_key]["end_time"] = end_time
    save_task_progress()  # 初始化后立即保存
def csv_to_complete_kv(csv_file_path, encoding=None):
    """CSV文件转字典工具函数"""
    if not csv_file_path or not os.path.exists(csv_file_path):
        return {"error": f"CSV文件不存在: {csv_file_path}"}

    def detect_encoding(file_path):
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            return chardet.detect(f.read(10240))['encoding'] or 'utf-8'

    def fix_encoding(s):
        if not s:
            return s
        for enc in ['utf-8', 'gbk', 'gb2312', 'utf-16']:
            try:
                return s.encode('latin-1').decode(enc)
            except UnicodeError:
                continue
        return ''.join([c for c in s if c.isprintable()])

    target_encoding = encoding or detect_encoding(csv_file_path)
    try:
        with open(csv_file_path, 'r', encoding=target_encoding, errors='replace') as f:
            reader = csv.reader(f)
            headers = [fix_encoding(h.strip()) for h in next(reader, [])]
            if not headers:
                return {"error": "CSV文件无表头数据"}

            result = {}
            for row_index, row in enumerate(reader):
                row_data = {}
                for i, header in enumerate(headers):
                    row_data[header] = row[i].strip() if i < len(row) else ''
                result[row_index] = row_data
            return result

    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        if not encoding:
            return csv_to_complete_kv(csv_file_path, encoding='gbk')
        return {"error": f"无法用{encoding}编码读取文件"}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": f"处理CSV失败: {str(e)}"}


import requests
from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter
from urllib3.util.retry import Retry

def requests_retry_session(
        retries=3,  # 重试3次
        backoff_factor=1,  # 重试间隔：1s, 2s, 4s...
        status_forcelist=(500, 502, 503, 504, 429),  # 需重试的状态码
        session=None,
        timeout=60  # 默认超时60s，可按需调整
    ):
    """创建带重试和超时的请求会话"""
    session = session or requests.Session()
    retry = Retry(
        total=retries,
        read=retries,
        connect=retries,
        backoff_factor=backoff_factor,
        status_forcelist=status_forcelist,
    )
    adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry)
    session.mount('http://', adapter)
    session.mount('https://', adapter)

    # 包装请求方法，添加全局超时
    original_request = session.request

    def wrapped_request(method, url, **kwargs):
        kwargs.setdefault('timeout', timeout)
        return original_request(method, url, **kwargs)

    session.request = wrapped_request
    return session


# 增加综合评分到history
def add_content_to_original_json(video_id, new_content):
    """
    直接在原始JSON文件中，向指定video_id追加内容（不创建新文件，不覆盖原有字段）

    参数:
        json_file_path: 原始JSON文件路径（将直接在此文件上修改）
        video_id: 要追加内容的目标video_id
        new_content: 要追加的键值对（字典格式）
    """
    # 1. 读取原始JSON数据到内存
    json_file_path = HISTORY_FILE
    with open(json_file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        # 加载为Python字典（修改内存中的字典不会影响原始文件，直到写入步骤）
        data = json.load(f)

    # 2. 检查目标video_id是否存在，避免报错
    if video_id not in data:
        print(f"错误：video_id '{video_id}' 不存在于原始文件中，无法追加内容！")
        return f"错误：video_id '{video_id}' 不存在于原始文件中，无法追加内容！"

    # 3. 向指定video_id追加内容（核心步骤）
    # 用字典合并的方式：new_content的键如果和原有重复，会覆盖；不重复则追加
    # 如果你想严格禁止覆盖原有字段，可以用下面的注释代码替代
    data[video_id].update(new_content)  # 允许覆盖重复键（如需禁止，见下方说明）
    # 禁止覆盖原有字段的写法：data[video_id] = {**data[video_id], **new_content}

    # 4. 写回到原始文件（直接覆盖原始文件，完成追加）
    with open(json_file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        # ensure_ascii=False：保留中文等非ASCII字符；indent=2：保持JSON格式美观
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"成功！已在原始文件 '{json_file_path}' 的 video_id '{video_id}' 中追加内容")
    return f"成功！已在原始文件 '{json_file_path}' 的 video_id '{video_id}' 中追加内容"


# 多线程
def process_queue():
    """完整的后台任务处理函数，带进度跟踪"""
    print("✅ 后台分析队列处理器已启动")

    while True:
        try:
            # 获取任务
            video_id = analysis_queue.get()
            if video_id is None:
                print("⚠️ 收到退出信号，队列处理器准备停止")
                analysis_queue.task_done()
                continue

            print(f"🔄 开始处理任务: {video_id}")
            init_task_progress(video_id)  # 初始化进度跟踪
            time_format = lambda: datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

            # 1. 加载视频信息
            info_path = os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], f"{video_id}_info.json")
            video_info = {}
            if os.path.exists(info_path):
                try:
                    with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        video_info = json.load(f)
                    print(f"✅ 已加载视频信息: {video_id}")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"⚠️ 加载视频信息失败: {str(e)}")

            # 2. 恢复任务上下文
            task_restored = False
            with task_lock:
                if video_id not in tasks:
                    print(f"⚠️ 尝试从task_history恢复任务 {video_id}")

                    if video_id not in task_history:
                        print(f"❌ 任务 {video_id} 不存在于task_history")
                        analysis_queue.task_done()
                        continue

                    record = task_history[video_id]
                    filename = record['filename']
                    original_filename = filename.split('_', 1)[1] if '_' in filename else filename

                    tasks[video_id] = {
                        'video_id': video_id,
                        'filename': filename,
                        'original_filename': original_filename,
                        'upload_time': record.get('uploadTime', datetime.now().isoformat()),
                        'status': '正在分析',
                        'video_info': video_info,
                        'thumbnail_url': f'/api/thumbnails/{video_id}_thumbnail.jpg',
                        'result': None,
                        'start_time': time_format()
                    }
                    task_restored = True
                    print(f"✅ 已恢复任务 {video_id}")

            if video_id not in tasks:
                print(f"❌ 任务 {video_id} 无法恢复，跳过")
                analysis_queue.task_done()
                continue

            # 更新任务状态
            with task_lock:
                if not task_restored:
                    tasks[video_id]['status'] = '正在分析'
                    tasks[video_id]['start_time'] = time_format()
                if video_id in task_history:
                    task_history[video_id]['status'] = 'processing'
                    task_history[video_id]['startedAt'] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                    save_task_history()

            # 3. 初始化结果
            result = {
                "video_basic_info": video_info,
                "CV_rating": {"师生行为": None, "师生情绪": None},
                "teach_stu_speak_content": [],
                "CV_Recognise_content": [],
                "speaking_analyze": {"analysis": ""},
                "teach_stu_emo_behavior": {"error": ""},
                "teaching_content_summary": {"summary": "", "key_points": []},
                "teaching_rating": {"total_score": 0, "criteria": {}},
                "comprehensive_review": {"evaluation": ""},
                "improvement_opinion": "",
                "question_analyze": ""
            }

            # 4. 接口调用（带进度跟踪）
            cv_speak_path = None
            video_path = None
            cv_path = None
            speak_csv_path = None
            teach_stu_emo = None
            teach_stu_beha = None


            timeout = 6000 # 秒
            # 4.1 提取语音和文本
            print("@@@======提取语音和文本======")
            step_key = "video_to_txt"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            speak_output_file = None
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)
                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/video_to_txt", params={"video_id": video_id})
                resp.raise_for_status()  # 非 200 状态码抛出异常

                # resp = requests.get("http://localhost:5000/api/video_to_txt", params={"video_id": video_id})
                # resp.raise_for_status()
                cv_speak_path = resp.json()["cv_speak_path"]
                video_path = resp.json()["video_path"]
                speak_output_file = resp.json()["output_file"]

                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())


            print("@@@======五个时间段提取======")
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)
                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/analyze-teaching-segments", params={"video_id": video_id})
                resp.raise_for_status()  # 非 200 状态码抛出异常
                result['analyze-teaching-segments'] = resp.json()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"话语内容失败 {video_id}: {e}")
                result['teach_stu_speak_content'] = []


            # 4.2 话语内容
            print("@@@======话语内容======")
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)
                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/csv-data", params={"video_id": video_id})
                resp.raise_for_status()  # 非 200 状态码抛出异常

                # resp = requests.get("http://localhost:5000/api/csv-data", params={"video_id": video_id})
                # resp.raise_for_status()
                result['teach_stu_speak_content'] = resp.json().get("data", [])
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"话语内容失败 {video_id}: {e}")
                result['teach_stu_speak_content'] = []

            # 4.3 CV师生情绪行为
            print("@@@======CV师生情绪行为======")
            step_key = "cv_behavior_emo"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)

                resp = session.get(
                    "http://localhost:5000/api/cv_behavior_emo",
                    params={"video_path": video_path, "cv_speak_path": cv_speak_path}
                )
                resp.raise_for_status()
                cv_path = resp.json()["cv_path"]
                result['CV_Recognise_content'] = resp.json()['result']
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.4 话语分析
            print("@@@======话语分析======")
            step_key = "discourse_analysis"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)

                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/discourse_analysis", params={"video_id": video_id})
                resp.raise_for_status()
                speak_csv_path = resp.json()['csv_data']
                result['speaking_analyze'] = resp.json()
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.5 师生情绪与行为
            print("@@@======师生情绪与行为======")
            step_key = "teach_stu_behavior"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)

                resp = session.get(
                    "http://localhost:5000/api/teach_stu_behavior",
                    params={"video_id": video_id, "cv_path": cv_path}
                )
                resp.raise_for_status()
                teach_stu_emo = resp.json()["teach_student_emotions"]
                teach_stu_beha = resp.json()["teach_student_behavior"]
                result['teach_stu_emo_behavior'] = resp.json()
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.6 教学内容总结
            print("@@@======教学内容总结======")
            step_key = "content_sumary"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)

                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/content_sumary", params={"video_id": video_id})
                resp.raise_for_status()
                result['teaching_content_summary'] = resp.json()
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.7 教学评分
            print("@@@======教学评分======")
            step_key = "calculate_score"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                discourse_form_csv = speak_csv_path["话语形式csv"] if (
                            speak_csv_path and isinstance(speak_csv_path, dict)) else ""
                discourse_function_csv = speak_csv_path["话语功能csv"] if (
                            speak_csv_path and isinstance(speak_csv_path, dict)) else ""

                csv_params = {
                    "discourse_form_csv": discourse_form_csv,
                    "discourse_function_csv": discourse_function_csv,
                    "teacher_emotion_csv": teach_stu_emo
                }
                print("教学评分##################",csv_params)

                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)

                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/calculate_score", params=csv_params)
                resp.raise_for_status()
                result['teaching_rating'] = resp.json()
                print("@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@教学评分")
                print({"teaching_rating":resp.json()})
                aaa = resp.json()
                # try :
                #     a = add_content_to_original_json(video_id=video_id, new_content={"teaching_rating":resp.json()})
                #     print(a)
                # except Exception as e:
                #     print("123456@@@@@@@@@@",e)

                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.8 综合评价
            step_key = "comprehensive_assessment"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)
                resp = session.get(
                    "http://127.0.0.1:5000/api/comprehensive_assessment",
                    params={
                        "video_id": video_id,
                        "teacher_behavior_csv": teach_stu_beha,
                        "discourse_form_csv": discourse_form_csv,
                        "discourse_function_csv": discourse_function_csv,
                        "teacher_emotion_csv": teach_stu_emo
                    }
                )
                print("@@@@@@@@@@@@@#############",teach_stu_beha,discourse_form_csv,discourse_function_csv,teach_stu_emo)
                resp.raise_for_status()
                result['comprehensive_review'] = resp.json()
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 4.9 CV评分数据整理
            print("@@@@@@评分数据写入中")
            try:
                if teach_stu_beha and os.path.exists(teach_stu_beha):
                    result['CV_rating']['师生行为'] = csv_to_complete_kv(teach_stu_beha)
                else:
                    result['CV_rating']['师生行为'] = {"error": f"师生行为CSV路径无效: {teach_stu_beha}"}

                if teach_stu_emo and os.path.exists(teach_stu_emo):
                    result['CV_rating']['师生情绪'] = csv_to_complete_kv(teach_stu_emo)
                else:
                    result['CV_rating']['师生情绪'] = {"error": f"师生情绪CSV路径无效: {teach_stu_emo}"}
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ CV评分数据整理失败: {str(e)}")

            # 4.10 问题改进
            step_key = "problem_improvement"
            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析中", start_time=time_format())
            try:
                csv_params = {
                    "teacher_behavior_csv": speak_output_file,   # 更改成对话内容
                    "discourse_form_csv": discourse_form_csv,
                    "discourse_function_csv": discourse_function_csv,
                    "teacher_emotion_csv": teach_stu_emo
                }

                session = requests_retry_session(timeout=timeout)
                resp = session.get("http://localhost:5000/api/problem_improvement", params=csv_params)
                resp.raise_for_status()
                data = resp.json()
                # result['improvement_opinion'] = data.get("改进建议", "")
                # result['question_analyze'] = data.get("问题分析", "")
                result['improvement_opinion'] = data
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, "分析完成", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = f"❌ {TASK_STEPS[step_key]}失败: {str(e)}"
                print(err_msg)
                update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())

            # 5. 保存结果
            try:
                result_path = os.path.join(app.config['zhenghe'], f"{video_id}_result.json")
                os.makedirs(app.config['zhenghe'], exist_ok=True)
                with open(result_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(result, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                print(f"✅ 分析结果已保存: {result_path}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ 保存结果文件失败: {str(e)}")

            # 6. 更新任务状态为完成
            with task_lock:
                tasks[video_id]['status'] = '已完成'
                tasks[video_id]['result'] = result
                tasks[video_id]['completed_time'] = time_format()

                if video_id in task_history:
                    task_history[video_id]['status'] = 'completed'
                    task_history[video_id]['completedAt'] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                    task_history[video_id]['teaching_rating'] = aaa
                    print("@@@@@",aaa)
                    save_task_history()

            print(f"✅ 任务 {video_id} 分析完成")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"🔥 处理任务时发生严重错误 {video_id}: {str(e)}")
            try:
                with task_lock:
                    if 'video_id' in locals() and video_id in tasks:
                        tasks[video_id]['status'] = f'分析失败: {str(e)[:50]}...'
                    if 'video_id' in locals() and video_id in task_history:
                        task_history[video_id]['status'] = 'failed'
                        task_history[video_id]['error'] = str(e)
                        task_history[video_id]['failedAt'] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                        save_task_history()
                if 'video_id' in locals():
                    for step_key in TASK_STEPS:
                        if task_progress.get(video_id, {}).get(step_key, {}).get("status") == "分析中":
                            update_task_step(video_id, step_key, f"失败: {str(e)[:20]}", end_time=time_format())
            except Exception as inner_e:
                print(f"❌ 更新失败状态时出错: {str(inner_e)}")

        finally:
            try:
                analysis_queue.task_done()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"⚠️ task_done() 出错: {str(e)}")


def recover_broken_tasks():
    print("🔧 正在恢复中断的任务状态...")

    broken_count = 0
    now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()

    with task_lock:
        for video_id, record in list(task_history.items()):  # 使用 list 避免迭代中修改问题
            status = record.get('status')
            if status in ['queued', 'processing']:
                print(f"🔍 发现未完成任务: {video_id}, status={status}")

                # ✅ 直接修改 task_history 中的 record
                task_history[video_id]['status'] = 'failed'
                task_history[video_id]['error'] = '程序意外终止，任务未完成'
                task_history[video_id]['recoveredAt'] = now_iso
                task_history[video_id]['failure_reason'] = 'crash_recovery'

                print(f"✅ 已将任务 {video_id} 的 status 从 '{status}' 改为 'failed'")
                broken_count += 1

        # ✅ 确保调用保存
        save_task_history()  # 这一步必须有！

    print(f"✅ 恢复完成，共 {broken_count} 个任务被标记为 failed")
    return broken_count

# 任务流管理
@app.route('/api/task-progress', methods=['GET'])
@require_role(["super_admin", "admin", "user"])
def get_task_progress(current_username, current_role):
    """实时获取任务流信息接口"""
    # 1. 获取并验证参数
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id', '').strip()
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({
            "code": 400,
            "message": "参数错误",
            "error": "缺少必填参数video_id"
        }), 400

    # 2. 权限校验
    def load_task_history():
        TASK_HISTORY_FILE = "./整合结果/data/task_history.json"
        try:
            with open(TASK_HISTORY_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return {}

    task_history_dict = load_task_history()
    task_info = task_history_dict.get(video_id) or task_history.get(video_id)

    if current_role == "user" and task_info:
        task_uploader = task_info.get("uploader")
        if task_uploader != current_username:
            return jsonify({
                "code": 403,
                "message": "权限不足",
                "error": "仅任务上传者或管理员可查看任务进度"
            }), 403

    # 3. 读取任务进度（线程安全）
    with progress_lock:
        if video_id not in task_progress:
            return jsonify({
                "code": 404,
                "message": "任务不存在",
                "error": f"未找到video_id为{video_id}的任务或任务未开始"
            }), 404

        # 4. 整理进度数据（按执行顺序排序）
        steps_order = [
            "video_to_txt", "cv_behavior_emo", "discourse_analysis",
            "teach_stu_behavior", "content_sumary", "calculate_score",
            "comprehensive_assessment", "problem_improvement"
        ]
        progress_list = [task_progress[video_id][step_key] for step_key in steps_order]

        # ---------------------- 核心修复：优化整体状态判断 ----------------------
        # 状态优先级：分析中 > 排队中 > 已完成/部分失败
        overall_status = "排队中"  # 默认排队中（所有步骤未开始）
        if any(step["status"] == "分析中" for step in progress_list):
            overall_status = "分析中"
        elif all(step["status"] in ["分析完成", "失败"] for step in progress_list):
            # 所有步骤已结束：全成功则"已完成"，否则"部分失败"
            overall_status = "已完成" if all(step["status"] == "分析完成" for step in progress_list) else "部分失败"
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------

        # 5. 返回结果
        return jsonify({
            "code": 200,
            "message": "success",
            "data": {
                "video_id": video_id,
                "overall_status": overall_status,
                "total_steps": len(progress_list),
                "completed_steps": sum(1 for step in progress_list if step["status"] == "分析完成"),
                "steps": progress_list
            }
        })

DATA_DIR = "./整合结果"
os.makedirs(DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# 定义允许更新的键路径配置
ALLOWED_KEY_PATHS = {
    # 顶级键
    "improvement_opinion": [],
    "question_analyze": [],
    "video_basic_info": [
        "courseName", "school", "educationStage", "grade",
        "teachingAge", "title", "teachingTime", "teacherName",
        "region", "courseType", "className", "teacherAge",
        "teacherTitle", "courseScore", "filename", "originalFilename",
        "videoId", "uploadTim", "uploadTimestamp"
    ],
    # 嵌套键
    "comprehensive_review": ["response"],
    "teaching_content_summary": ["response"],
    "teach_stu_emo_behavior": {
        "data": ["behavior_conclusion", "emotion_conclusion"]
    },
    "speaking_analyze": {
        "result": {
            "话语形式分析结果": {
                "discourse_form": ["summary"]
            },
            "话语功能分析结果": {
                "discourse_form": ["summary"]
            },
            "其他话语分析结果": ["conclusion"]
        }
    }
}

def get_file_path(video_id):
    """根据video_id生成文件路径"""
    # 文件名格式：video_id_result.json
    filename = f"{video_id}_result.json"
    return os.path.join(DATA_DIR, filename)
def is_valid_value(value):
    """判断值是否为有效非空值"""
    if value is None:
        return False
    if isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip():
        return False
    if isinstance(value, (dict, list)) and not value:
        return False
    return True
def update_nested_data(data, path_parts, value):
    """递归更新嵌套数据，仅处理有效值"""
    # 只更新有效非空值
    if not is_valid_value(value):
        return False

    if len(path_parts) == 1:
        data[path_parts[0]] = value
        return True

    current_key = path_parts[0]
    remaining_path = path_parts[1:]

    if current_key in data and isinstance(data[current_key], dict):
        return update_nested_data(data[current_key], remaining_path, value)
    return False
def process_updates(data, updates, parent_keys=None):
    """处理更新数据，仅更新允许的键和有效值"""
    if parent_keys is None:
        parent_keys = []
    updated = False  # 标记是否有实际更新

    if isinstance(updates, dict):
        for key, value in updates.items():
            current_path = parent_keys + [key]
            current_key = current_path[0]

            # 检查是否为允许更新的顶级键
            if current_key not in ALLOWED_KEY_PATHS:
                continue

            # 跳过无效值
            if not is_valid_value(value):
                continue

            # 处理不同层级的更新
            if len(current_path) == 1:
                # 顶级键处理
                if isinstance(ALLOWED_KEY_PATHS[current_key], dict):
                    # 递归处理嵌套结构
                    if key in data and isinstance(data[key], dict):
                        nested_updated = process_updates(data[key], value, current_path)
                        updated = updated or nested_updated
                else:
                    # 直接更新值（适用于非嵌套的顶级键）
                    data[key] = value
                    updated = True

            else:
                # 嵌套键处理
                nested_updated = update_nested_data(data, current_path, value)
                updated = updated or nested_updated

    return updated
@app.route('/api/edit-json', methods=['POST'])
def edit_json():
    try:
        # 获取请求参数
        video_id = request.json.get('video_id')
        updates = request.json.get('updates', {})

        if not video_id:
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "请提供video_id参数"}), 400

        # 生成文件路径
        file_path = get_file_path(video_id)

        # 检查文件是否存在
        if not os.path.exists(file_path):
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": f"文件不存在，路径：{file_path}",
                "suggestion": f"请确认该video_id对应的文件已生成，文件名应为：{os.path.basename(file_path)}"
            }), 404

        # 读取JSON文件
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            try:
                json_data = json.load(f)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "JSON文件格式错误"}), 400

        # 深拷贝原始数据用于比较
        original_data = json.dumps(json_data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

        # 处理更新（仅更新允许的键和有效值）
        has_updated = process_updates(json_data, updates)

        # 只有当有实际更新时才写入文件
        if has_updated:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(json_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            message = "JSON文件更新成功"
        else:
            message = "未检测到有效更新，文件未修改"

        return jsonify({
            "status": "success",
            "message": message,
            "has_updated": has_updated,
            "file_path": file_path
        })

    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"操作失败: {str(e)}"
        }), 500

def _format_seconds(sec):
    """将秒数格式化为 HH:MM:SS 字符串（便于横轴显示）"""
    hours = int(sec // 3600)
    minutes = int((sec % 3600) // 60)
    seconds = int(sec % 60)
    return f"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{seconds:02d}"
def clean_text(raw_text):
        # 移除Markdown标题符号（#及后面的空格）
        cleaned = re.sub(r'#{1,6}\s+', '', raw_text)
        # 移除分割线（---）
        cleaned = re.sub(r'---+', '', cleaned)
        # 移除加粗符号（**）
        cleaned = re.sub(r'\*\*', '', cleaned)
        # 移除括号内的百分比对比（如(4.92% vs. 12%)）
        cleaned = re.sub(r'\(\d+\.\d+% vs\. \d+%\)', '', cleaned)
        # 移除多余空行
        cleaned = re.sub(r'\n\s*\n', '\n', cleaned).strip()
        return cleaned
def generate_wordcloud(data, title, background_color='white'):
    """辅助函数：生成词云图（解决中文显示问题）"""
    # 处理空数据
    if not data:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))
        ax.text(0.5, 0.5, "无数据", ha='center', va='center', fontsize=14)
        ax.axis('off')
        return fig

    # 查找系统中的中文字体（解决中文显示问题）
    def get_chinese_font():
        # 常见中文字体路径列表，根据系统自动适配
        font_candidates = [
            # Windows系统
            'C:/Windows/Fonts/simhei.ttf',  # 黑体
            'C:/Windows/Fonts/msyh.ttc',  # 微软雅黑
            'C:/Windows/Fonts/simsun.ttc',  # 宋体
            # Mac系统
            '/System/Library/Fonts/PingFang.ttc',  # 苹方
            '/Library/Fonts/Songti.ttc',  # 宋体
            # Linux系统
            '/usr/share/fonts/opentype/noto/NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc',
            '/usr/share/fonts/wqy-zenhei/wqy-zenhei.ttc'
        ]

        # 选择第一个存在的字体
        for font in font_candidates:
            if os.path.exists(font):
                return font
        return None  # 若未找到，使用默认字体（可能显示异常）

    # 生成词云
    wordcloud = WordCloud(
        font_path=get_chinese_font(),  # 关键：指定中文字体路径
        background_color=background_color,
        width=800,
        height=600,
        max_words=50,
        max_font_size=100,
        random_state=42
    ).generate_from_frequencies(data)

    # 创建图表
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))
    ax.imshow(wordcloud, interpolation='bilinear')
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
    ax.axis('off')
    plt.tight_layout(pad=0)

    return fig

import os
import shutil
from docx import Document
# 注意：包名是docx2pdf，导入名也是docx2pdf
import subprocess
@app.route('/api/report')
def report1():
    class ReportGenerator:
        def __init__(self, title="报告文档", output_dir="output"):
            self.document = Document()
            self.title = title
            self.output_dir = output_dir
            self.temp_images = []
            self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH = Inches(6.5)  # 后续图像约束用

            if not os.path.exists(output_dir):
                os.makedirs(output_dir)

            # 1. 正文样式：11pt + 1.2倍行间距
            normal_style = self.document.styles['Normal']
            normal_font = normal_style.font
            normal_font.name = 'SimHei'
            normal_font.size = Pt(11)  # 原10pt → 11pt
            normal_style.element.rPr.rFonts.set(qn('w:eastAsia'), 'SimHei')
            normal_style.paragraph_format.line_spacing = 1.2  # 行间距
            normal_style.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(6)  # 段后留白

            # 2. 标题样式层级化
            heading_styles = {
                1: {'size': Pt(16), 'space_after': Pt(10)},  # 章节标题（第X章）
                2: {'size': Pt(14), 'space_after': Pt(8)},  # 小节标题（X.X）
                3: {'size': Pt(12), 'space_after': Pt(6)}  # 子小节标题
            }
            for level, style_info in heading_styles.items():
                heading_style = self.document.styles[f'Heading {level}']
                heading_font = heading_style.font
                heading_font.name = 'SimHei'
                heading_font.size = style_info['size']
                heading_font.bold = True
                heading_style.element.rPr.rFonts.set(qn('w:eastAsia'), 'SimHei')
                heading_style.paragraph_format.space_after = style_info['space_after']

            # 3. 文档大标题单独处理（add_title方法中用）
            self.title_font_size = Pt(20)

        def save_docx(self, filename=None):
            """保存为docx文件"""
            if not filename:
                filename = f"{self.title}.docx"
            if not filename.endswith(".docx"):
                filename += ".docx"

            filepath = os.path.join(self.output_dir, filename)
            self.document.save(filepath)
            print(f"✅ DOCX文档已保存：{filepath}")
            return filepath

        def convert_to_pdf(self, docx_path=None, pdf_filename=None):
            if not docx_path:
                docx_path = self.save_docx()

            pdf_path = os.path.splitext(docx_path)[0] + ".pdf"

            # 显式指定 soffice 路径（避免 PATH 问题）
            soffice_path = r"C:\Program Files\LibreOffice\program\soffice.exe"
            if not os.path.exists(soffice_path):
                print("❌ LibreOffice 未安装或路径错误")
                return None

            try:
                result = subprocess.run([
                    soffice_path,
                    "--headless",
                    "--convert-to", "pdf",
                    "--outdir", os.path.dirname(pdf_path),
                    docx_path
                ], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)

                if result.returncode == 0 and os.path.exists(pdf_path) and os.path.getsize(pdf_path) > 1024:
                    print(f"✅ PDF文档已保存：{pdf_path}")
                    return pdf_path
                else:
                    print(f"❌ 转换失败: {result.stderr}")
                    return None

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ 转换异常：{e}")
                return None

        # 添加文档标题
        def add_title(self):
            # 大标题：20pt + 居中
            title_para = self.document.add_heading(self.title, 0)
            title_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            title_run = title_para.runs[0]
            title_run.font.size = self.title_font_size  # 20pt
            title_run.font.bold = True

            # 日期：11pt + 与标题间距12pt
            # date_para = self.document.add_paragraph(datetime.now().strftime("%Y年%m月%d日"))
            # date_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            # date_para.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(12)
            # self.document.add_page_break()  分页

        # 添加章节标题
        def add_chapter(self, chapter_num, title):
            """添加章节标题"""
            self.document.add_heading(f"{chapter_num} {title}", level=1)

        # 创建临时图片
        def _create_temp_image(self):
            """创建临时图片"""
            temp_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.png', delete=False)
            temp_path = temp_file.name
            temp_file.close()
            self.temp_images.append(temp_path)
            return temp_path

        # 清理临时图片
        def clean_temp_files(self):
            """清理临时图片"""
            for path in self.temp_images:
                try:
                    os.remove(path)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"⚠️  临时文件清理失败：{path}（错误：{str(e)}）")

        # 图表生成函数
        def add_text_content(self, content):
            para = self.document.add_paragraph(content)
            # 段落前添加3pt间距，避免与上一个元素拥挤
            para.paragraph_format.space_before = Pt(3)

        # 柄图1
        def add_discourse_form_pie(self, discourse_data, title="话语形式分布饼图"):
            # 定义目标标签顺序
            target_labels = ["反馈", "管理", "陈述", "提问", "回答"]
            target_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)
            label_index_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(target_labels)}

            # 解析输入数据（核心修改：移除百分号处理，直接读取数字）
            for item in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                category_name = item.get("primary_category_name", "")
                # 直接读取数字（新数据是float类型，无需处理%）
                percent_value = item.get("ratio", 0.0)

                # 确保是数字类型（容错）
                if not isinstance(percent_value, (int, float)):
                    percent_value = 0.0

                if category_name in label_index_map:
                    target_values[label_index_map[category_name]] = percent_value

            # 数据验证
            if sum(target_values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效话语形式分析数据可展示")
                return

            # 配置样式（保持不变）
            colors = ["#FF6B6B", "#4ECDC4", "#45B7D1", "#96CEB4", "#FECA57"]
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False

            # 缩小画布尺寸（保持不变）
            cm_to_inch = 0.3937
            pie_size_cm = 8
            fig_size = pie_size_cm * cm_to_inch
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_size, fig_size + 0.8))

            # 绘制饼图（保持不变）
            explode = (0.05, 0, 0, 0, 0)
            wedges, texts, autotexts = ax.pie(
                target_values,
                labels=target_labels,
                colors=colors,
                autopct='%1.1f%%',  # 显示时自动加百分号
                explode=explode,
                startangle=90,
                textprops={'fontsize': 9, 'fontweight': 'bold'},
                wedgeprops={'edgecolor': 'white', 'linewidth': 1.5}
            )

            # 百分比文字样式（保持不变）
            for autotext in autotexts:
                autotext.set_color('white')
                autotext.set_fontsize(8)
                autotext.set_fontweight('bold')

            # 图例配置（保持不变）
            legend_elements = [plt.Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, facecolor=color, edgecolor='white', linewidth=1.5)
                               for color in colors]
            plt.legend(
                legend_elements,
                target_labels,
                loc='center',
                bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.15),
                fontsize=8,
                ncol=len(target_labels),
                frameon=False,
                columnspacing=1.2,
                handletextpad=0.5
            )

            # 标题和保存（保持不变）
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=10)
            ax.axis('equal')

            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.tight_layout()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150, facecolor='white')
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(fig_size))

        # 话语功能一级指标饼图
        def add_discourse_function_pie(self, discourse_data, title="话语功能一级指标占比饼图"):
            """生成话语功能一级指标饼图（知识理解、表达交流、实践应用、创造迁移）"""
            # 定义一级指标标签及对应颜色
            primary_labels = ["知识理解", "表达交流", "实践应用", "创造迁移"]
            colors = ["#3498db", "#2ecc71", "#f39c12", "#e74c3c"]

            # 提取数据
            values = [0.0] * len(primary_labels)
            label_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(primary_labels)}

            for item in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                name = item.get("primary_category_name", "")
                ratio = item.get("ratio", 0.0)
                if name in label_map:
                    try:
                        values[label_map[name]] = float(ratio)
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        continue

            # 数据验证
            if sum(values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效话语功能一级指标数据可展示")
                return

            # 配置样式
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False

            # 创建饼图 - 缩小画布尺寸，从(8,8)改为(5,5)
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5, 5))
            wedges, texts, autotexts = ax.pie(
                values,
                labels=primary_labels,
                colors=colors,
                autopct='%1.1f%%',
                startangle=90,
                explode=(0.05, 0, 0, 0),  # 突出显示第一个指标
                textprops={'fontsize': 10, 'fontweight': 'bold'},  # 适当减小字体
                wedgeprops={'edgecolor': 'white', 'linewidth': 1.5}  # 略微减细边框
            )

            # 优化百分比标签
            for autotext in autotexts:
                autotext.set_color('white')
                autotext.set_fontweight('bold')
                autotext.set_fontsize(9)  # 减小百分比标签字体

            # 减小标题字体和间距
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=15)
            ax.axis('equal')

            # 保存并添加到文档
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150, facecolor='white')
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

            # 优化：缩小插入文档时的图像宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            # 减小图像宽度，例如设置为原来的60-70%
            reduced_width = self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH * 0.6
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=reduced_width)

        # 话语功能一级指标对比图（柱形图+折线图）
        def add_discourse_function_primary_chart(self, discourse_data, title="话语功能一级指标与优质课对比"):
            """生成一级指标对比图表：柱状图显示当前占比，折线图显示优质课指标"""
            # 定义一级指标标签
            primary_labels = ["知识理解", "表达交流", "实践应用", "创造迁移"]
            current_values = [0.0] * len(primary_labels)
            excellent_values = [0.0] * len(primary_labels)
            label_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(primary_labels)}

            # 提取数据
            for item in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                name = item.get("primary_category_name", "")
                if name in label_map:
                    idx = label_map[name]
                    # 当前课程占比
                    try:
                        current_values[idx] = float(item.get("ratio", 0.0))
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pass
                    # 优质课指标
                    try:
                        excellent_values[idx] = float(item.get("Quality_lessons", 0.0))
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        pass

            # 数据验证
            if sum(current_values) == 0 and sum(excellent_values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效话语功能一级指标数据可展示")
                return

            # 配置样式
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            x = np.arange(len(primary_labels))
            width = 0.4

            # 创建图表
            fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))

            # 柱状图（当前课程）
            bars = ax1.bar(
                x - width / 2,
                current_values,
                width,
                label='当前课程占比',
                color='#3498db',
                edgecolor='black'
            )

            # 柱状图标签
            for bar in bars:
                height = bar.get_height()
                ax1.annotate(f'{height:.1f}',
                             xy=(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, height),
                             xytext=(0, 3),
                             textcoords="offset points",
                             ha='center', va='bottom',
                             fontsize=10, fontweight='bold')

            # 折线图（优质课指标）
            ax2 = ax1.twinx()
            ax2.plot(
                x,
                excellent_values,
                color='#e74c3c',
                marker='o',
                linestyle='--',
                linewidth=2,
                markersize=8,
                label='优质课指标'
            )

            # 折线图标签
            for i, v in enumerate(excellent_values):
                ax2.annotate(f'{v:.0f}',
                             xy=(i, v),
                             xytext=(0, 5),
                             textcoords="offset points",
                             ha='center', va='bottom',
                             fontsize=10, fontweight='bold',
                             color='#e74c3c')

            # 配置坐标轴
            ax1.set_xlabel('话语功能一级指标', fontsize=12)
            ax1.set_ylabel('当前课程占比', fontsize=12, color='#3498db')
            ax2.set_ylabel('优质课指标', fontsize=12, color='#e74c3c')
            ax1.set_xticks(x)
            ax1.set_xticklabels(primary_labels, fontsize=10)

            # 合并图例
            lines1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()
            lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
            ax1.legend(lines1 + lines2, labels1 + labels2, loc='upper left', fontsize=10)

            ax1.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', pad=20)
            plt.tight_layout()

            # 保存并添加到文档
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150, facecolor='white')
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(7))

        def add_speech_speed_chart(self, other_discourse_data, title="课堂堂语速分布折线线图"):
            """
            绘制课堂语速折线分布图，纵轴为时间（分钟），纵轴为语速等级（快、中、慢）

            参数说明：
            - other_discourse_data: 其他话语分析结果数据（包含"other_discourse_analysis"键）
            - title: 图表标题
            """
            # 1. 解析语速数据
            try:
                # 若输入为字符串格式，先进行JSON解析
                if isinstance(other_discourse_data, str):
                    other_discourse_data = json.loads(other_discourse_data)

                # 提取语速数据列表（单位：字/分钟）
                speed_data = other_discourse_data.get("other_discourse_analysis", {}).get("speak_speed", [])

                # 数据验证
                if not speed_data or not all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in speed_data):
                    self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效语速数据可展示")
                    return
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 语速数据解析失败")
                return

            # 2. 定义语速等级划分标准（可根据实际情况调整）
            def get_speed_level(speed):
                if speed < 120:
                    return "慢"
                elif 120 <= speed <= 200:
                    return "中"
                else:
                    return "快"

            # 3. 处理数据：生成时间轴（分钟）和对应等级
            time_points = list(range(1, len(speed_data) + 1))  # 第1分钟、第2分钟...
            speed_levels = [get_speed_level(s) for s in speed_data]

            # 4. 配置图表样式
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False

            # 创建图表
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))

            # 5. 绘制折线图（带标记点）
            # 为不同语速等级分配颜色
            color_map = {"慢": "#3498db", "中": "#2ecc71", "快": "#e74c3c"}
            colors = [color_map[level] for level in speed_levels]

            # 绘制折线
            ax.plot(
                time_points,
                speed_data,
                color="#7f8c8d",
                linestyle="-",
                linewidth=2,
                zorder=1  # 折线置于底层
            )

            # 绘制带颜色的标记点（表示不同等级）
            scatter = ax.scatter(
                time_points,
                speed_data,
                c=colors,
                s=80,  # 点大小
                edgecolors="white",
                linewidth=1.5,
                zorder=2  # 标记点置于上层
            )

            # 6. 添加数据标签（显示具体语速值）
            for i, (time, speed) in enumerate(zip(time_points, speed_data)):
                ax.annotate(
                    f"{speed}字/分",
                    xy=(time, speed),
                    xytext=(0, 10),  # 偏移量
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha="center",
                    va="bottom",
                    fontsize=9,
                    fontweight="bold",
                    bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.3", fc="white", ec=colors[i], alpha=0.8)
                )

            # 7. 配置坐标轴
            ax.set_xlabel("时间（分钟）", fontsize=12)
            ax.set_ylabel("语速（字/分钟）", fontsize=12)
            ax.set_xticks(time_points)
            ax.set_xticklabels([f"{t}" for t in time_points], fontsize=10)

            # 添加网格线
            ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.7)

            # 设置标题
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight="bold", pad=20)

            # 添加图例（显示快、中、慢对应的颜色）
            legend_elements = [
                plt.Line2D([0], [0], marker='o', color='w', markerfacecolor=color_map["快"],
                           markersize=10, label='快 (>200字/分)'),
                plt.Line2D([0], [0], marker='o', color='w', markerfacecolor=color_map["中"],
                           markersize=10, label='中 (120-200字/分)'),
                plt.Line2D([0], [0], marker='o', color='w', markerfacecolor=color_map["慢"],
                           markersize=10, label='慢 (<120字/分)')
            ]
            ax.legend(handles=legend_elements, loc="upper right", fontsize=10)

            # 8. 保存图表并添加到文档
            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=150, facecolor="white")
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(7))

        # 添加饼图
        def add_pie_chart(self, labels, sizes, title="饼图"):
            """添加饼图，增大圆形大小，显示标签和数值，颜色提示在下方"""
            # 设置饼图大小为3x3英寸
            fig_size = 4
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_size, fig_size))

            # 绘制饼图，增大圆形比例
            wedges, texts, autotexts = ax.pie(
                sizes,
                labels=labels,  # 显示标签
                autopct='%1.1f%%',  # 显示百分比
                startangle=90,
                textprops={'fontsize': 9},
                pctdistance=0.85,  # 百分比标签距离中心的距离
                labeldistance=1.05  # 类别标签距离中心的距离
            )
            ax.axis('equal')
            plt.title(title, fontsize=11, pad=10)

            # 调整子图边距，减少空白区域
            plt.subplots_adjust(left=0.1, right=0.9, top=0.85, bottom=0.25)

            # 颜色图例处理
            legend_elements = []
            for i in range(len(wedges)):
                color = wedges[i].get_facecolor()
                if len(color) == 3:
                    color = (*color, 1.0)
                legend_elements.append(
                    plt.Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, facecolor=color, edgecolor='black')
                )

            # 底部图例
            plt.legend(legend_elements, labels, loc='center', bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.1),
                       fontsize=8, ncol=min(3, len(labels)))

            # 保存图表
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close()

            # 添加到文档
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

            # 图像居中
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(fig_size))

        # 添加柱状图
        def add_bar_chart(self, categories, values1, values2, label1="数据组1", label2="数据组2", title="分组柱状图",
                          color1='#1f77b4', color2='#ff7f0e'):
            """
            添加分组柱状图（一个类别对应两个柱形），优化尺寸和柱形宽度

            参数说明：
            - categories: 横轴类别列表（如 ["A", "B", "C"]）
            - values1: 第一组数据（与categories长度相同）
            - values2: 第二组数据（与categories长度相同）
            - label1: 第一组数据的标签（用于图例）
            - label2: 第二组数据的标签（用于图例）
            - title: 图表标题
            - color1: 第一组柱形的颜色
            - color2: 第二组柱形的颜色
            """
            # 验证输入数据长度匹配
            if len(categories) != len(values1) or len(categories) != len(values2):
                print("❌ 类别与数据长度不匹配")
                return

            # 设置图表尺寸（长度3，高度2）
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6, 4))

            # 优化柱形宽度（根据类别数量动态调整，避免过宽）
            bar_width = 0.25 if len(categories) <= 5 else 0.2
            index = np.arange(len(categories))  # 类别索引

            # 绘制两组柱形
            bars1 = ax.bar(index - bar_width / 2, values1, bar_width,
                           label=label1, color=color1, alpha=0.8, edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5)
            bars2 = ax.bar(index + bar_width / 2, values2, bar_width,
                           label=label2, color=color2, alpha=0.8, edgecolor='white', linewidth=0.5)

            # 添加标题和标签（调整字体大小适配小尺寸图表）
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=8, pad=5)
            ax.set_xticks(index)
            ax.set_xticklabels(categories, rotation=0, ha='center', fontsize=6)  # 不旋转标签
            ax.tick_params(axis='y', labelsize=6)  # 纵轴刻度字体

            # 显示图例（缩小尺寸和位置）
            ax.legend(fontsize=5, loc='upper right', bbox_to_anchor=(1.15, 1.1), borderaxespad=0)

            # 添加网格线使数据更易读（弱化网格）
            ax.grid(axis='y', linestyle='--', alpha=0.4, linewidth=0.5)

            # 移除顶部和右侧边框，使图表更简洁
            ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False)
            ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)

            # 调整布局避免标签被截断
            plt.tight_layout()

            # 保存到临时文件（提高DPI保证清晰度）
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=200)
            plt.close()

            # 添加到文档
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

            # 图像居中显示
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(3))  # 使用设定的宽度

        # 添加时序图
        def add_student_action_timeline(self, stu_action_list, action_mapping=None, title="学生行为时序图",
                                        ylabel="行为类型"):
            """
            生成学生行为时序图（确保显示纵轴标签，处理零值和空数据）

            参数说明：
            - stu_action_list: 行为数据列表
            - action_mapping: 类型映射字典
            - title: 图表标题
            - ylabel: 纵轴总标签
            """
            # A4纸张适配尺寸（厘米转英寸）
            cm_to_inch = 0.3937
            # 调整为横向8cm，纵向10cm
            fig_width = 15 * cm_to_inch  # 8cm宽度
            fig_height = 10 * cm_to_inch  # 10cm高度

            # 确保action_mapping至少是个空字典
            action_mapping = action_mapping or {}

            # 验证输入数据
            if not isinstance(stu_action_list, list):
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 学生行为数据格式错误，应为列表类型")
                stu_action_list = []

            # 数据预处理与验证
            processed = []
            all_times = []
            type_total_duration = {}
            valid_data = False

            # 即使没有数据，也保留映射中的所有类型
            all_types_from_mapping = set(action_mapping.keys())
            all_types_from_data = set()

            for idx, action in enumerate(stu_action_list):
                try:
                    # 验证必要字段
                    if not isinstance(action, dict):
                        raise ValueError(f"数据项不是字典: {action}")

                    required_fields = ["startTime", "endTime", "type"]
                    if not all(k in action for k in required_fields):
                        missing = [k for k in required_fields if k not in action]
                        raise ValueError(f"缺少必要字段: {missing}")

                    # 解析时间和类型
                    start = float(action["startTime"]) if action["startTime"] not in [None, ""] else 0
                    end = float(action["endTime"]) if action["endTime"] not in [None, ""] else 0
                    action_type = int(action["type"]) if action["type"] not in [None, ""] else -1

                    all_types_from_data.add(action_type)

                    # 处理时间异常
                    if end <= start:
                        # 确保至少有1秒的显示长度
                        start = max(0, start)
                        end = start + 1
                        print(f"警告: 修正异常时间数据（索引{idx}）: start={start}, end={end}")

                    duration = end - start
                    if duration <= 0:
                        duration = 1  # 强制最小1秒的持续时间
                        end = start + duration
                        print(f"警告: 修正零或负持续时间（索引{idx}）为1秒")

                    valid_data = True

                    # 累计每种行为的总时长
                    if action_type in type_total_duration:
                        type_total_duration[action_type] += duration
                    else:
                        type_total_duration[action_type] = duration

                    # 获取映射的类别信息
                    if action_type in action_mapping:
                        type_info = action_mapping[action_type]
                        name = type_info.get("name", f"类型{action_type}")
                        color = type_info.get("color", "#cccccc")
                    else:
                        name = f"未映射类型({action_type})"
                        color = "#cccccc"

                    processed.append({
                        "start": start,
                        "end": end,
                        "duration": duration,
                        "type": action_type,
                        "name": name,
                        "color": color
                    })
                    all_times.extend([start, end])

                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"处理数据时出错（索引{idx}）: {str(e)}")
                    continue

            # 确保包含映射中定义的所有类型，即使没有数据
            all_types = all_types_from_mapping.union(all_types_from_data)
            # 为没有数据的类型初始化时长为0
            for action_type in all_types:
                if action_type not in type_total_duration:
                    type_total_duration[action_type] = 0

            # 计算总时长和各类别的占比
            total_duration = sum(type_total_duration.values())
            type_percentage = {}
            for t, d in type_total_duration.items():
                type_percentage[t] = (d / total_duration) * 100 if total_duration > 0 else 0

            # 时间轴范围处理
            if not all_times:
                # 如果没有时间数据，创建一个默认范围
                min_t, max_t = 0, 10  # 显示10秒的默认范围
            else:
                min_t, max_t = min(all_times), max(all_times)

            margin = (max_t - min_t) * 0.1 if max_t > min_t else 1
            x_range = [min_t - margin, max_t + margin]

            # 创建图表
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(fig_width, fig_height))

            # 纵轴位置映射（按类别名称排序）
            def get_type_name(t):
                if t in action_mapping and "name" in action_mapping[t]:
                    return action_mapping[t]["name"]
                return f"类型{t}"

            unique_types = sorted(all_types, key=lambda x: get_type_name(x))
            type_pos = {t: i + 1 for i, t in enumerate(unique_types)}

            # 绘制行为条
            for action in processed:
