                ax.barh(
                    y=type_pos[action["type"]],
                    width=action["duration"],
                    left=action["start"],
                    height=0.3,  # 细柱形
                    color=action["color"],
                    alpha=0.8,
                    edgecolor="#333"
                )

            # 纵轴设置（确保显示所有标签，包括没有数据的类型）
            ytick_labels = []
            for t in unique_types:
                # 获取类型名称
                if t in action_mapping and "name" in action_mapping[t]:
                    name = action_mapping[t]["name"]
                else:
                    name = f"未映射类型({t})"

                # 获取占比
                percentage = type_percentage.get(t, 0)
                ytick_labels.append(f"{name} ({percentage:.1f}%)")

            ax.set_yticks([type_pos[t] for t in unique_types])
            ax.set_yticklabels(ytick_labels, fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
            ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=14, labelpad=10)

            # 横轴设置
            ax.set_xlim(x_range)
            ax.set_xlabel("时间（秒）", fontsize=14)

            # 时间格式化
            def format_seconds(s, _):
                return f"{s:.0f}"

            ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(plt.FuncFormatter(format_seconds))

            plt.xticks(rotation=45, ha="right", fontsize=12)

            # 标题设置
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=16, pad=15)

            # 添加底部浅色网状线
            ax.grid(axis="x", linestyle="--", alpha=0.3, color="#999999")

            # 调整布局
            plt.tight_layout()

            # 保存并添加到文档
            try:
                temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
                plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=150)
                plt.close()

                title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
                title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
                title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

                self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(fig_width))
            except Exception as e:
                self.document.add_paragraph(f"⚠️ 图表生成失败: {str(e)}")
                print(f"图表生成错误: {str(e)}")

        # 添加点状分布图
        def add_scatter_plot(
                self,
                patrol_list=None,
                json_data=None,
                title="教室人员分布与区域划分图",
                # 新增参数：可自定义画布范围（默认适配常见坐标场景）
                x_range=(0, 1000),  # X轴固定范围
                y_range=(0, 600),  # Y轴固定范围（上下各一半划分区域）
        ):
            """
            添加上下各一半的固定分区图（所有数据点强制绘制在教师区域）
            区域划分：上半部分=教师区域，下半部分=学生区域（固定各占50%）
            """
            # 1. 数据解析（保持原有容错逻辑）
            if json_data is not None:
                try:
                    patrol_list = json_data["CV_Recognise_content"]["result"]["data"]["patrolList"]
                except (KeyError, TypeError) as e:
                    self.document.add_paragraph(f"数据解析失败：找不到 patrolList 路径（错误：{str(e)}）")
                    return
            elif patrol_list is None:
                self.document.add_paragraph("无有效数据（未传入 patrol_list 或 json_data）")
                return

            # 2. 数据校验与过滤（仅保留有效 x_show/y_show 坐标）
            if not isinstance(patrol_list, list) or len(patrol_list) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("无巡逻数据可展示")
                return

            valid_points = []
            for item in patrol_list:
                if not isinstance(item, dict) or "x_show" not in item or "y_show" not in item:
                    continue
                x = item["x_show"]
                y = item["y_show"]
                if isinstance(x, (int, float)) and isinstance(y, (int, float)):
                    valid_points.append({"x": x, "y": y})

            if not valid_points:
                self.document.add_paragraph("无有效人员坐标数据（字段缺失或格式错误）")
                return

            # 3. 固定画布与区域划分（核心修改）
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5, 3.5))
            x_total_min, x_total_max = x_range  # 固定X轴范围
            y_total_min, y_total_max = y_range  # 固定Y轴范围
            mid_y = (y_total_min + y_total_max) / 2  # 中间线（上下各一半）

            # 颜色方案（保持原有高对比度）
            teacher_color = '#e74c3c'  # 教师点颜色（所有数据点用此色）
            student_area_color = '#e6f7ff'  # 学生区域底色（下半部分）
            teacher_area_color = '#fff2e8'  # 教师区域底色（上半部分）
            split_line_color = '#999999'

            # 点大小配置（保持适配小画布）
            point_size = 140  # 所有点统一用教师点大小
            point_border_width = 1.2
            marker = 'o'

            # 4. 绘制固定分区背景（上下各一半）
            # 学生区域（下半部分）
            student_area = Rectangle(
                (x_total_min, y_total_min),
                x_total_max - x_total_min,
                mid_y - y_total_min,
                fill=True,
                alpha=0.6,
                color=student_area_color,
                edgecolor='none'
            )
            ax.add_patch(student_area)

            # 教师区域（上半部分）
            teacher_area = Rectangle(
                (x_total_min, mid_y),
                x_total_max - x_total_min,
                y_total_max - mid_y,
                fill=True,
                alpha=0.6,
                color=teacher_area_color,
                edgecolor='none'
            )
            ax.add_patch(teacher_area)

            # 绘制分区线和文字
            ax.axhline(y=mid_y, color=split_line_color, linestyle='--', linewidth=2)

            ax.text(
                x_total_min + (x_total_max - x_total_min) * 0.05,
                mid_y + (y_total_max - mid_y) * 0.5,
                "教师区域",
                fontsize=12,
                fontweight='bold',
                color='#c0392b',
                bbox=dict(facecolor='white', alpha=0.7, boxstyle='round,pad=0.4')
            )

            ax.text(
                x_total_min + (x_total_max - x_total_min) * 0.05,
                y_total_min + (mid_y - y_total_min) * 0.5,
                "学生区域",
                fontsize=12,
                fontweight='bold',
                color='#2980b9',
                bbox=dict(facecolor='white', alpha=0.7, boxstyle='round,pad=0.4')
            )

            # 5. 强制所有数据点绘制在教师区域（上半部分）
            for p in valid_points:
                # 保持原始X坐标，Y坐标强制映射到教师区域（mid_y 到 y_total_max 之间）
                x = p["x"]
                # 计算Y坐标映射比例：将原始Y值缩放到教师区域范围内
                original_y_range = (min([p["y"] for p in valid_points]), max([p["y"] for p in valid_points]))
                if original_y_range[0] == original_y_range[1]:
                    # 所有点Y坐标相同，直接放在教师区域中间
                    y = mid_y + (y_total_max - mid_y) / 2
                else:
                    # 线性映射到教师区域
                    y = mid_y + ((p["y"] - original_y_range[0]) / (original_y_range[1] - original_y_range[0])) * (
                                y_total_max - mid_y)

                # 绘制教师区域的点
                ax.scatter(
                    x, y,
                    color=teacher_color,
                    alpha=0.85,
                    s=point_size,
                    marker=marker,
                    edgecolor='white',
                    linewidth=point_border_width,
                    zorder=3  # 置于顶层，避免被背景遮挡
                )

            # 6. 坐标轴和图例配置（仅保留教师位置图例，因所有点都是教师）
            ax.set_xlim(x_total_min, x_total_max)
            ax.set_ylim(y_total_min, y_total_max)
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, pad=15)
            ax.set_xlabel("X坐标", fontsize=11)
            ax.set_ylabel("Y坐标", fontsize=11)
            ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.3)
            ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')

            # 图例：仅显示教师位置（所有数据点都是教师）
            ax.scatter([], [], color=teacher_color, s=120, marker=marker,
                       edgecolor='white', linewidth=point_border_width, label='教师位置')
            ax.legend(loc='upper right', fontsize=9)

            # 7. 保存并插入文档（保持原有优化）
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.tight_layout()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(5.5))

        def add_teacher_attention_rect_chart(self, attention_data, title="教师课堂注意力方向分布"):
            """
            绘制教师注意力随时间分布的序列图，适配A4页面
            """
            if not attention_data:
                self.document.add_paragraph("无注意力数据可展示")
                return

            directions = [
                "tea_face_upperLeft_num", "tea_face_up_num", "tea_face_upperRight_num",
                "tea_face_left_num", "tea_face_right_num",
                "tea_face_lowerLeft_num", "tea_face_down_num", "tea_face_lowerRight_num"
            ]
            direction_labels = [
                "左上", "上", "右上",
                "左", "右",
                "左下", "下", "右下"
            ]
            colors = plt.cm.tab10.colors[:len(directions)]

            times = sorted([float(t) for t in attention_data.keys()])

            # 1. 缩小画布尺寸，从10x8调整为8x6，更适合A4页面
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))

            for idx, dir in enumerate(directions):
                dir_values = [attention_data[str(int(t))].get(dir, 0) for t in times]
                ax.scatter(
                    times,
                    [idx] * len(times),
                    s=[v * 20 for v in dir_values],  # 保持点大小系数
                    color=colors[idx],
                    alpha=0.7,
                    label=direction_labels[idx]
                )

            # 2. 优化时间刻度显示，避免拥挤
            if len(times) > 8:  # 如果时间点过多，只显示部分
                step = max(1, len(times) // 8)
                ax.set_xticks(times[::step])
            else:
                ax.set_xticks(times)

            # 3. 调整字体大小
            ax.set_yticks(range(len(directions)))
            ax.set_yticklabels(direction_labels, fontsize=10)  # 缩小纵轴标签字体
            ax.set_xlabel("时间（分钟）", fontsize=11)  # 缩小x轴标签
            ax.set_ylabel("注意力方向", fontsize=11)  # 缩小y轴标签
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13, pad=15)  # 缩小标题并减少边距
            ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6)
            ax.legend(loc='upper right', fontsize=9)  # 缩小图例

            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close()

            # 4. 缩小插入文档的图片宽度，从7英寸调整为6英寸
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(6))

        def add_discourse_function_chart(self, discourse_data, title="话语功能二级指标与优质课对比分析"):
            """
            适配“话语功能分析结果”的二级指标对比图表：
            柱状图显示当前课程占比（ratio），折线图显示优质课指标（Quality_lessons）

            参数说明：
            - discourse_data: 话语功能分析数据（需包含"classifications"键，格式同用户提供）
            - title: 图表标题
            """
            # 1. 定义当前数据中的二级指标标签（从用户数据中提取，确保完全匹配）
            target_labels = [
                "观察记忆", "概括理解", "说明论证",  # 知识理解下的二级指标
                "经历经验", "主观看法", "情感态度",  # 表达交流下的二级指标
                "分析计算", "推测解释", "简单问题解决",  # 实践应用下的二级指标
                "综合问题解决", "猜想探究", "发现创新"  # 创造迁移下的二级指标
            ]
            current_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)  # 当前课程占比（ratio）
            excellent_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)  # 优质课指标（Quality_lessons）
            label_index_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(target_labels)}  # 标签-索引映射

            # 2. 解析数据：提取二级指标的ratio和Quality_lessons
            for primary_item in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                # 遍历每个一级分类下的二级分类
                secondary_list = primary_item.get("secondary_classifications", [])
                for secondary_item in secondary_list:
                    # 获取当前二级指标的名称、当前占比、优质课指标
                    sec_label = secondary_item.get("secondary_category_name", "")
                    sec_ratio = secondary_item.get("ratio", 0.0)  # 当前课程占比（无%符号，直接用数值）
                    sec_quality = secondary_item.get("Quality_lessons", 0.0)  # 优质课指标

                    # 验证数据类型（避免非数值数据导致错误）
                    try:
                        sec_ratio = float(sec_ratio)
                        sec_quality = float(sec_quality)
                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
                        sec_ratio = 0.0
                        sec_quality = 0.0

                    # 若当前二级标签在目标列表中，更新对应位置数据
                    if sec_label in label_index_map:
                        idx = label_index_map[sec_label]
                        current_values[idx] = sec_ratio
                        excellent_values[idx] = sec_quality

            # 3. 数据有效性检查
            if sum(current_values) == 0 and sum(excellent_values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 未读取到有效话语功能二级指标数据")
                return

            # 4. 图表样式配置（解决中文显示、布局优化）
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']  # 中文支持
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False  # 负号显示正常
            x = np.arange(len(target_labels))  # 横轴标签位置
            bar_width = 0.4  # 柱状图宽度（避免拥挤）

            # 5. 创建双轴图表（左轴：柱状图，右轴：折线图）
            fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 8))  # 宽屏布局，适配12个二级标签

            # 5.1 左轴：绘制当前课程占比柱状图
            bars = ax1.bar(
                x - bar_width / 2,  # 柱状图位置（左移半宽，与折线图错开）
                current_values,
                bar_width,
                label="当前课程占比",
                color="#2E86AB",  # 蓝色系，区分度高
                edgecolor="#1A5276",  # 深色边框，增强轮廓
                alpha=0.8  # 透明度，避免视觉沉重
            )

            # 为柱状图添加数值标签（仅显示非0值，避免杂乱）
            for bar in bars:
                height = bar.get_height()
                if height > 0.1:  # 过滤极小值标签
                    ax1.annotate(
                        f"{height:.1f}",  # 保留1位小数
                        xy=(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, height),
                        xytext=(0, 3),  # 向上偏移3个像素
                        textcoords="offset points",
                        ha="center", va="bottom",
                        fontsize=8,
                        fontweight="bold",
                        color="#1A5276"
                    )

            # 5.2 右轴：绘制优质课指标折线图
            ax2 = ax1.twinx()  # 创建共享横轴的右轴
            line = ax2.plot(
                x,  # 折线图位置（横轴中心）
                excellent_values,
                color="#E74C3C",  # 红色系，与柱状图对比鲜明
                marker="o",  # 圆形标记点，便于定位
                markersize=6,
                linestyle="--",  # 虚线，区分于柱状图
                linewidth=2,
                label="优质课指标"
            )

            # 为折线图添加数值标签（仅显示非0值）
            for i, value in enumerate(excellent_values):
                if value > 0.1:  # 过滤极小值标签
                    ax2.annotate(
                        f"{value:.0f}",  # 优质课指标为整数，保留0位小数
                        xy=(i, value),
                        xytext=(0, 5),  # 向上偏移5个像素
                        textcoords="offset points",
                        ha="center", va="bottom",
                        fontsize=8,
                        fontweight="bold",
                        color="#E74C3C"
                    )

            # 6. 坐标轴与图例配置
            # 横轴：标签旋转45度，避免重叠
            ax1.set_xticks(x)
            ax1.set_xticklabels(target_labels, fontsize=9, rotation=45, ha="right")
            ax1.set_xlabel("话语功能二级指标", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", labelpad=10)

            # 左轴（当前课程占比）
            ax1.set_ylabel("当前课程占比", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color="#2E86AB", labelpad=10)
            ax1.tick_params(axis="y", colors="#2E86AB", labelsize=10)
            ax1.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.3, color="#2E86AB")  # 网格线辅助读数

            # 右轴（优质课指标）
            ax2.set_ylabel("优质课指标", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color="#E74C3C", labelpad=10)
            ax2.tick_params(axis="y", colors="#E74C3C", labelsize=10)

            # 合并图例（左轴+右轴）
            handles1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()
            handles2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
            ax1.legend(
                handles1 + handles2,
                labels1 + labels2,
                loc="upper right",  # 图例位置（右上，不遮挡数据）
                fontsize=10,
                framealpha=0.9  # 图例背景半透明
            )

            # 7. 标题与布局调整
            ax1.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight="bold", pad=20)
            plt.tight_layout()  # 自动调整布局，防止标签被截断

            # 8. 保存图表并添加到文档
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(
                temp_path,
                bbox_inches="tight",  # 紧凑布局，去除多余空白
                dpi=150,  # 高分辨率，避免模糊
                facecolor="white"  # 白色背景，适配文档
            )
            plt.close()

            # 将图表插入文档
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(8.5))  # 适配文档宽度

        # 添加折线面积图
        def add_line_area_chart(self, x_data, y_data, title="折线面积图"):
            """添加折线面积图"""
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 6))
            ax.fill_between(x_data, y_data, color="skyblue", alpha=0.4)
            ax.plot(x_data, y_data, color="Slateblue", alpha=0.8, linewidth=2)
            plt.title(title, fontsize=12)

            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=100)
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            # 优化2：图像居中 + 强制最大宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH)  # 约束宽度

        # 添加词云图
        def add_word_cloud(self, text, title="词云图"):
            """添加词云图"""
            font_path = r"C:\Windows\Fonts\simhei.ttf"
            if not os.path.exists(font_path):
                print(f"⚠️  未找到黑体字体！请确认路径：{font_path}")
                return

            wordcloud = WordCloud(
                font_path=font_path,
                width=800,
                height=400,
                background_color='white',
                max_words=100
            ).generate(text)

            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
            ax.imshow(wordcloud, interpolation='bilinear')
            ax.axis("off")
            plt.title(title, fontsize=12)

            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=100)
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            # 优化2：图像居中 + 强制最大宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH)  # 约束宽度

        # 柱形图+折线图
        def add_discourse_combined_chart(self, target_labels, discourse_data, title="话语形式占比与优质课指标对比"):
            """
            生成组合图表：柱状图显示当前课程话语形式占比，折线图显示优质课指标
            """
            current_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)
            excellent_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)
            label_index_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(target_labels)}

            # 解析输入数据（核心修改：移除百分号处理，直接读取数字）
            for item in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                category_name = item.get("primary_category_name", "")
                # 直接读取数字（新数据是float类型）
                current_value = item.get("ratio", 0.0)
                excellent_value = item.get("Quality_lessons", 0.0)

                # 容错：确保是数字类型
                if not isinstance(current_value, (int, float)):
                    current_value = 0.0
                if not isinstance(excellent_value, (int, float)):
                    excellent_value = 0.0

                if category_name in label_index_map:
                    idx = label_index_map[category_name]
                    current_values[idx] = current_value
                    excellent_values[idx] = excellent_value

            # 数据验证（保持不变）
            if sum(current_values) == 0 and sum(excellent_values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效话语形式分析数据可展示")
                return

            # 图表样式和绘制（保持不变）
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
            plt.rcParams['font.size'] = 12
            x = np.arange(len(target_labels))
            width = 0.4

            fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5))

            bars = ax1.bar(
                x - width / 2,
                current_values,
                width,
                label='当前课程占比',
                color='#4ECDC4',
                edgecolor='black'
            )

            for bar in bars:
                height = bar.get_height()
                ax1.annotate(
                    f'{height:.1f}%',  # 显示时加百分号
                    xy=(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, height),
                    xytext=(0, 3),
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha='center', va='bottom',
                    fontsize=11, fontweight='bold'
                )

            ax2 = ax1.twinx()
            ax2.plot(
                x,
                excellent_values,
                color='#FF6B6B',
                marker='o',
                linestyle='--',
                linewidth=2.5,
                markersize=9,
                label='优质课指标'
            )

            for i, v in enumerate(excellent_values):
                ax2.annotate(
                    f'{v:.1f}%',  # 显示时加百分号
                    xy=(i, v),
                    xytext=(0, 5),
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha='center',
                    va='bottom',
                    fontsize=11,
                    fontweight='bold',
                    color='#FF6B6B'
                )

            ax1.set_xlabel('话语形式', fontsize=14)
            ax1.set_ylabel('当前课程占比（%）', fontsize=14, color='#4ECDC4')
            ax2.set_ylabel('优质课指标（%）', fontsize=14, color='#FF6B6B')
            ax1.set_xticks(x)
            ax1.set_xticklabels(target_labels, fontsize=12, rotation=15)

            lines1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()
            lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
            ax1.legend(
                lines1 + lines2,
                labels1 + labels2,
                loc='upper left',
                fontsize=12,
                frameon=True,
                shadow=True
            )

            ax1.set_title(title, fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', pad=20)

            max_value = max(max(current_values), max(excellent_values)) if (current_values or excellent_values) else 100
            ax1.set_ylim(0, max_value * 1.2)
            ax2.set_ylim(0, max_value * 1.2)

            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(
                temp_path,
                bbox_inches='tight',
                dpi=150,
                facecolor='white',
                edgecolor='none'
            )
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(6.5))

        def add_teacher_emotion_chart(self, emotion_data, title="教师情绪水平变化折线图"):
            """
            绘制教师情绪水平折线图，展示积极、中性、消极三种情绪随时间的变化

            参数说明：
            - emotion_data: 教师每分钟情绪数据，格式为列表，元素为"1"(消极)、"2"(中性)、"3"(积极)
            - title: 图表标题
            """
            # 1. 数据验证与转换
            if not isinstance(emotion_data, list) or len(emotion_data) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效教师情绪数据可展示")
                return

            # 转换情绪代码为数值（1=消极，2=中性，3=积极）
            try:
                emotion_values = [int(val) for val in emotion_data]
            except ValueError:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 情绪数据格式错误，无法解析")
                return

            # 2. 准备时间轴数据
            time_points = list(range(1, len(emotion_values) + 1))  # 第1分钟到第n分钟

            # 3. 配置情绪类别映射（数值→文字→颜色）
            emotion_mapping = {
                1: {"label": "消极", "color": "#e74c3c"},  # 红色
                2: {"label": "中性", "color": "#f39c12"},  # 橙色
                3: {"label": "积极", "color": "#2ecc71"}  # 绿色
            }

            # 4. 配置图表样式
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']  # 解决中文显示
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))

            # 5. 绘制折线图（主趋势线）
            ax.plot(
                time_points,
                emotion_values,
                color="#34495e",  # 深灰色主线
                linestyle="-",
                linewidth=2,
                marker="o",  # 标记点
                markersize=6,
                markerfacecolor="#3498db",  # 标记点填充色
                markeredgewidth=1.5
            )

            # 6. 为每个时间点添加颜色标记（根据情绪类别着色）
            for i, (time, val) in enumerate(zip(time_points, emotion_values)):
                emotion_info = emotion_mapping.get(val, emotion_mapping[2])  # 默认为中性
                ax.scatter(
                    time,
                    val,
                    color=emotion_info["color"],
                    s=80,  # 点大小
                    zorder=3  # 置于主线之上
                )

                # 添加情绪标签（显示文字类别）
                ax.annotate(
                    emotion_info["label"],
                    xy=(time, val),
                    xytext=(0, 10),  # 向上偏移10像素
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha="center",
                    va="bottom",
                    fontsize=9,
                    fontweight="bold",
                    bbox=dict(
                        boxstyle="round,pad=0.3",
                        fc=emotion_info["color"],
                        ec="white",
                        alpha=0.7,
                        color=emotion_info["color"]
                    ),
                    color="white"
                )

            # 7. 配置坐标轴
            ax.set_xlabel("时间（分钟）", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
            ax.set_ylabel("情绪水平", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")

            # 设置Y轴刻度为固定的三个情绪等级
            ax.set_yticks([1, 2, 3])
            ax.set_yticklabels(["消极", "中性", "积极"], fontsize=10)

            # 设置X轴刻度为时间点
            ax.set_xticks(time_points)
            ax.set_xticklabels([f"{t}" for t in time_points], fontsize=10)

            # 添加网格线
            ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.5)

            # 设置标题
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight="bold", pad=20)

            # 8. 保存图表并添加到文档
            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=150, facecolor="white")
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(7))

        # 柱形图+折线图2
        def add_secondary_indicators_chart(self, discourse_data, title="二级指标与优质课对比分析"):
            """
            生成二级指标对比图表：柱状图显示当前课程占比，折线图显示优质课指标
            核心修改：将"扩展"改为"拓展"（匹配新数据标签）
            """
            # 修正二级指标标签："扩展"→"拓展"（适配新数据）
            target_labels = [
                "面向群体", "面向个体", "群体回答", "个体回答",
                "复述", "深化", "拓展", "评价",  # 关键修改：扩展→拓展
                "发布指令", "学习指引"
            ]
            current_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)
            excellent_values = [0.0] * len(target_labels)
            label_index_map = {label: idx for idx, label in enumerate(target_labels)}

            # 解析输入数据（核心修改：移除百分号处理，直接读取数字）
            for primary in discourse_data.get("classifications", []):
                for secondary in primary.get("secondary_classifications", []):
                    category_name = secondary.get("secondary_category_name", "")
                    # 直接读取数字（新数据是float类型）
                    current_value = secondary.get("ratio", 0.0)
                    excellent_value = secondary.get("Quality_lessons", 0.0)

                    # 容错：确保是数字类型
                    if not isinstance(current_value, (int, float)):
                        current_value = 0.0
                    if not isinstance(excellent_value, (int, float)):
                        excellent_value = 0.0

                    if category_name in label_index_map:
                        idx = label_index_map[category_name]
                        current_values[idx] = current_value
                        excellent_values[idx] = excellent_value

            # 数据验证（保持不变）
            if sum(current_values) == 0 and sum(excellent_values) == 0:
                self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效二级指标分析数据可展示")
                return

            # 图表样式和绘制（保持不变）
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
            x = np.arange(len(target_labels))
            width = 0.4

            fig, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(9, 5.5))

            bars = ax1.bar(
                x - width / 2,
                current_values,
                width,
                label='当前课程占比',
                color='#45B7D1',
                edgecolor='black'
            )

            for bar in bars:
                height = bar.get_height()
                if height > 0:
                    ax1.annotate(
                        f'{height:.1f}%',
                        xy=(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, height),
                        xytext=(0, 2),
                        textcoords="offset points",
                        ha='center', va='bottom',
                        fontsize=7,
                        fontweight='bold'
                    )

            ax2 = ax1.twinx()
            line, = ax2.plot(
                x,
                excellent_values,
                color='#FF6B6B',
                marker='o',
                linestyle='--',
                linewidth=1.5,
                markersize=5,
                label='优质课指标'
            )

            for i, v in enumerate(excellent_values):
                if v > 0:
                    ax2.annotate(
                        f'{v:.1f}%',
                        xy=(i, v),
                        xytext=(0, 4),
                        textcoords="offset points",
                        ha='center', va='bottom',
                        fontsize=7,
                        fontweight='bold',
                        color='#FF6B6B'
                    )

            ax1.set_xlabel('二级指标', fontsize=10)
            ax1.set_ylabel('当前课程占比（%）', fontsize=10, color='#45B7D1')
            ax2.set_ylabel('优质课指标（%）', fontsize=10, color='#FF6B6B')
            ax1.set_xticks(x)
            ax1.set_xticklabels(target_labels, fontsize=8, rotation=60, ha='right')

            lines1, labels1 = ax1.get_legend_handles_labels()
            lines2, labels2 = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
            ax1.legend(lines1 + lines2, labels1 + labels2, loc='upper right', fontsize=9)

            ax1.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=15)

            max_value = max(max(current_values), max(excellent_values)) if (
                        max(current_values) or max(excellent_values)) else 10
            ax1.set_ylim(0, max_value * 1.3)
            ax2.set_ylim(0, max_value * 1.3)

            ax1.grid(axis='y', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)

            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150, facecolor='white')
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(6.5))

        # 新增：三角雷达图函数
        def add_triangular_radar_chart(self, labels, data, title="三角雷达图", legend_name=None):
            """
            添加多层浅色三角形底纹、外部数据指向的三角雷达图，适配A4一半宽度

            :param labels: 三个顶点的标签列表（长度必须为3）
            :param data: 数据值列表（长度必须为3）
            :param title: 图表标题
            :param legend_name: 图例名称（可选）
            """
            if len(labels) != 3 or len(data) != 3:
                print("❌ 三角雷达图需要 exactly 3 个标签和 3 个数据值")
                return

            # A4一半宽度（约10厘米）
            a4_half_width = 3
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(a4_half_width, a4_half_width), subplot_kw=dict(polar=True))

            # 计算三角形角度（等边分布）
            angles = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 3, endpoint=False).tolist()
            values = data + [data[0]]
            angles = angles + [angles[0]]
            labels = labels + [labels[0]]

            # 绘制多层浅色三角形底纹
            max_value = max(data) if max(data) > 0 else 1
            layer_count = 5  # 层数可调整
            for i in range(1, layer_count + 1):
                layer_values = [max_value * (i / layer_count) for _ in range(3)] + [max_value * (i / layer_count)]
                ax.plot(angles, layer_values, 'lightgray', linestyle='-', alpha=0.5, linewidth=1)

            # 绘制数据三角形
            ax.fill(angles, values, 'blue', alpha=0.5)
            ax.plot(angles, values, 'blue', linewidth=2)

            # 隐藏极坐标默认元素
            ax.grid(False)
            ax.spines['polar'].set_visible(False)
            ax.set_yticks([])

            # 设置顶点标签
            ax.set_thetagrids(np.degrees(angles[:3]), labels[:3], fontsize=10)

            # 添加数值指向标签
            for i in range(3):
                label_angle = angles[i]
                label_radius = values[i] * 1.1  # 数值标签在三角形外
                ax.text(
                    label_angle, label_radius,
                    f'{values[i]}',
                    ha='center', va='center',
                    fontsize=9, fontweight='bold',
                    color='black'
                )

            # 添加标题
            plt.title(title, fontsize=12, pad=10)

            # 图例设置
            if legend_name:
                ax.legend([legend_name], loc='upper right', bbox_to_anchor=(1.2, 1.1), fontsize=9)

            # 保存并添加到文档
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.tight_layout()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close()

            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)

            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(a4_half_width))

        def add_student_emotion_chart(self, emotion_data, title="学生情绪分布分析"):
            """
            生成学生情绪分布组合图表：
            - 左侧：情绪分布饼图（积极、中性、消极）
            - 右侧：横向线型图展示各情绪比例（修复颜色参数错误）
            """
            # 1. 数据解析与汇总
            try:
                total_negative = 0
                total_neutral = 0
                total_positive = 0

                for time_key in emotion_data:
                    time_data = emotion_data[time_key]
                    if isinstance(time_data, list) and len(time_data) > 0:
                        emotions = time_data[0]
                        total_negative += emotions.get("negative", 0)
                        total_neutral += emotions.get("neutral", 0)
                        total_positive += emotions.get("positive", 0)

                total = total_negative + total_neutral + total_positive
                if total == 0:
                    self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效学生情绪数据可展示")
                    return

                neg_ratio = total_negative / total * 100
                neu_ratio = total_neutral / total * 100
                pos_ratio = total_positive / total * 100

            except (TypeError, KeyError) as e:
                self.document.add_paragraph(f"⚠️ 学生情绪数据解析失败: {str(e)}")
                return

            # 2. 配置样式与中文显示
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False

            # 3. 创建组合图表
            fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12, 6))
            fig.suptitle(title, fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=1.05)

            # 情绪类别配置（修复颜色参数错误的核心）
            labels = ["积极", "中性", "消极"]
            colors = ["#2ecc71", "#f39c12", "#e74c3c"]  # 颜色列表
            values = [total_positive, total_neutral, total_negative]
            ratios = [pos_ratio, neu_ratio, neg_ratio]

            # 4. 左侧：绘制饼图
            wedges, texts, autotexts = ax1.pie(
                values,
                labels=labels,
                colors=colors,  # 饼图支持颜色列表
                autopct=lambda p: f'{p:.1f}%\n({int(p * total / 100)})',
                startangle=90,
                textprops={'fontsize': 11},
                wedgeprops={'edgecolor': 'white', 'linewidth': 2}
            )

            for text in texts:
                text.set_fontweight('bold')
            for autotext in autotexts:
                autotext.set_color('white')
                autotext.set_fontweight('bold')
                autotext.set_fontsize(10)

            ax1.axis('equal')
            ax1.set_title("情绪分布占比", fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=10)

            # 5. 右侧：绘制横向线型图（修复颜色参数传递方式）
            y_pos = np.arange(len(labels))

            # 为每个数据点单独绘制线条和标记（解决颜色列表问题）
            for i in range(len(labels)):
                # 绘制横向线条
                ax2.hlines(
                    y=y_pos[i],
                    xmin=0,
                    xmax=ratios[i],
                    color=colors[i],  # 传递单个颜色值而非列表
                    linewidth=5,
                    alpha=0.7
                )

                # 添加标记点
                ax2.plot(
                    ratios[i],
                    y_pos[i],
                    'o',
                    color=colors[i],  # 传递单个颜色值而非列表
                    markersize=10,
                    markeredgecolor='white',
                    markeredgewidth=2
                )

            # 添加数值标签
            for i, ratio in enumerate(ratios):
                ax2.annotate(
                    f'{ratio:.1f}%',
                    xy=(ratio + 1, i),
                    xytext=(0, 0),
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha='left',
                    va='center',
                    fontsize=11,
                    fontweight='bold',
                    color=colors[i]
                )

            # 配置右侧图表
            ax2.set_yticks(y_pos)
            ax2.set_yticklabels(labels, fontsize=11, fontweight='bold')
            ax2.set_xlabel('占比 (%)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
            ax2.set_xlim(0, max(ratios) + 10)
            ax2.set_title("情绪比例对比", fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=10)
            ax2.grid(axis='x', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)

            # 6. 调整布局并保存
            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150, facecolor='white')
            plt.close()

            # 7. 添加到文档
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(8))

        def add_student_focus_area_chart(self, focus_data, title="学生课堂专注度变化折线面积图"):
            """
            绘制学生课堂专注度折线面积图，展示不同时间点的抬头人数变化

            参数说明：
            - focus_data: 学生专注度数据，格式为字典，键为时间点（"1"到"n"），值为抬头人数
            - title: 图表标题
            """
            # 1. 数据解析与排序
            try:
                # 将时间点转换为整数并排序
                time_points = sorted([int(key) for key in focus_data.keys()])
                # 按排序后的时间点提取对应人数
                focus_values = [focus_data[str(t)] for t in time_points]

                # 数据验证
                if not focus_values or len(focus_values) != len(time_points):
                    self.document.add_paragraph("⚠️ 无有效学生专注度数据可展示")
                    return

            except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as e:
                self.document.add_paragraph(f"⚠️ 学生专注度数据解析失败: {str(e)}")
                return

            # 2. 配置样式与中文显示
            plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'Arial Unicode MS']
            plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False

            # 3. 创建图表
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))

            # 4. 绘制折线面积图
            # 绘制填充区域（面积部分）
            ax.fill_between(
                time_points,
                focus_values,
                color="#3498db",
                alpha=0.3,  # 透明度
                label="专注度分布"
            )

            # 绘制折线（边界线）
            ax.plot(
                time_points,
                focus_values,
                color="#3498db",
                linewidth=2.5,
                marker="o",  # 标记点
                markersize=7,
                markerfacecolor="#2980b9",
                markeredgewidth=2,
                markeredgecolor="white"
            )

            # 5. 添加数据标签（显示具体人数）
            for t, val in zip(time_points, focus_values):
                ax.annotate(
                    f"{val}人",
                    xy=(t, val),
                    xytext=(0, 10),  # 向上偏移10像素
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    ha="center",
                    va="bottom",
                    fontsize=9,
                    fontweight="bold",
                    bbox=dict(
                        boxstyle="round,pad=0.3",
                        fc="white",
                        ec="#3498db",
                        alpha=0.8
                    )
                )

            # 6. 配置坐标轴
            ax.set_xlabel("时间（分钟）", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
            ax.set_ylabel("抬头人数（专注人数）", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")

            # 设置X轴刻度
            ax.set_xticks(time_points)
            ax.set_xticklabels([f"{t}" for t in time_points], fontsize=10)

            # 设置Y轴范围（从0开始，留10%余量）
            max_val = max(focus_values) if focus_values else 0
            ax.set_ylim(0, max_val * 1.1)

            # 添加网格线
            ax.grid(axis="y", linestyle="--", alpha=0.5)

            # 添加图例
            ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=10)

            # 设置标题
            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=14, fontweight="bold", pad=20)

            # 7. 调整布局并保存
            plt.tight_layout()
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            plt.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=150, facecolor="white")
            plt.close()

            # 8. 添加到文档
            title_para = self.document.add_paragraph(title)
            title_para.runs[0].font.bold = True
            title_para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(3)
            self.document.add_picture(temp_path, width=Inches(7))

        # 第一章：课程基础信息
        def add_chapter1_content(self, video_info):
            self.add_chapter(1, "课程基础信息")

            info = video_info.get("video_basic_info", {})

            needed_keys = ["courseName", "school", "educationStage", "grade", "teachingAge", "title", "teachingTime",
                           "teacherName", "region", "courseType", "className", "teacherAge", "teacherTitle",
                           "courseScore"]
            # 只保留指定键的内容（过滤掉其他键）
            filtered_info = {key: info[key] for key in needed_keys if key in info}

            items = list(filtered_info.items())

            num_rows = (len(items) + 1) // 2

            table = self.document.add_table(rows=num_rows, cols=2)
            # table.style = 'Table Grid'  # 显示网格线
            table.autofit = True  # 自动适配内容
            for col in table.columns:
                col.width = Cm(8)

            for i in range(num_rows):
                if i < len(items):
                    key, value = items[i]
                    self._set_cell_text(table.cell(i, 0), key, value)
                j = i + num_rows
                if j < len(items):
                    key, value = items[j]
                    self._set_cell_text(table.cell(i, 1), key, value)

        # 设置表格单元格文本
        def _set_cell_text(self, cell, key, value):
            formatted_key = self._format_key(key)
            para = cell.paragraphs[0]
            para.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(0)  # 取消段后间距
            para.paragraph_format.word_wrap = True  # 自动换行

            # 键：加粗 + 10pt
            run_key = para.add_run(f"{formatted_key}：")
            run_key.font.bold = True
            run_key.font.size = Pt(10)  # 原9pt → 10pt
            run_key.font.name = 'SimHei'
            run_key.element.rPr.rFonts.set(qn('w:eastAsia'), 'SimHei')

            # 值：10pt + 超长文本自动换行
            run_val = para.add_run(str(value))
            run_val.font.size = Pt(10)
            run_val.font.name = 'SimHei'
            run_val.element.rPr.rFonts.set(qn('w:eastAsia'), 'SimHei')

        def _format_key(self, key):
            """将英文key转换为中文"""
            key_mapping = {
                "courseName": "课程名称",
                "school": "学校",
                "educationStage": "教育阶段",
                "grade": "年级",
                "teachingAge": "教龄（年）",
                "title": "教师职称",
                "teachingTime": "授课时间",
                "teacherName": "教师姓名",
                "region": "地区",
                "courseType": "课程类型",
                "className": "授课班级",
                "teacherAge": "教师年龄",
                "teacherTitle": "教师头衔",
                "courseScore": "课程评分"
            }
            return key_mapping.get(key, key)

        def add_discourse_wordclouds(self, other_discourse_data):
            """生成三个词云图：关键词、违规语、口头语（修复中文显示）"""
            # 解析数据
            try:
                if isinstance(other_discourse_data, str):
                    cleaned_data = re.sub(r'\n\s*', '', other_discourse_data)
                    other_discourse_data = json.loads(cleaned_data)

                analysis_data = other_discourse_data.get("other_discourse_analysis", {})
                keywords = analysis_data.get("keywords", {})
                # forbidden = analysis_data.get("forbidden", [])
                forbidden = []
                orals = analysis_data.get("orals", {})

                forbidden_dict = {word: 1 for word in forbidden} if forbidden else {}

            except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as e:
                self.document.add_paragraph(f"⚠️ 话语数据解析失败: {str(e)}")
                return

            # 生成关键词词云
            # self.document.add_paragraph("关键词词云图")
            self.document.add_heading("关键字", level=3)
            keywords_fig = generate_wordcloud(keywords, "课堂关键词分布")
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            keywords_fig.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close(keywords_fig)
            # 优化2：图像居中 + 强制最大宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH)  # 约束宽度

            # 生成口头语词云
            # self.document.add_paragraph("口头语词云图")
            self.document.add_heading("口头语", level=3)
            orals_fig = generate_wordcloud(orals, "课堂口头语分布")
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            orals_fig.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close(orals_fig)
            # 优化2：图像居中 + 强制最大宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH)  # 约束宽度

            # 生成违规语词云
            # self.document.add_paragraph("违规语词云图")
            self.document.add_heading("违规语", level=3)
            forbidden_fig = generate_wordcloud(forbidden_dict, "课堂违规语分布")
            temp_path = self._create_temp_image()
            forbidden_fig.savefig(temp_path, bbox_inches='tight', dpi=150)
            plt.close(forbidden_fig)
            # 优化2：图像居中 + 强制最大宽度
            img_para = self.document.add_paragraph()
            img_para.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER  # 居中
            img_run = img_para.add_run()
            img_run.add_picture(temp_path, width=self.MAX_IMG_WIDTH)  # 约束宽度

            self.add_text_content(a)

        def add_chapter5_content(self):

            self.document.add_page_break()

            self.add_chapter3_content()
            self.document.add_page_break()

            self.add_chapter4_content()

            self.document.add_page_break()

            self.add_chapter6_content()
            self.document.add_page_break()

            self.add_chapter7_content()

            docx_path = self.save_docx()
            pdf_path = self.convert_to_pdf(docx_path)

            self.clean_temp_files()

            return docx_path, pdf_path

    # 1. 获取前端传递的videoId
    video_id = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_id:
        return jsonify({"error": "缺少视频ID参数：video_id"}), 400

    # 2. 构建CSV文件路径（与video_to_txt接口保持一致的命名规则）
    json_filename = f"{video_id}_result.json"
    json_path = os.path.join(r"./整合结果", json_filename)

    info_filename = f"{video_id}_info.json"
    info_path = os.path.join(r"./uploads", info_filename)
    with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json_data = json.load(f)
        courseName = json_data['courseName']


    # 3. 检查CSV文件是否已存在
    if os.path.exists(json_path):
        print(f"{video_id}对应分析结果文件存在")
        file_path = json_path
    else:
        print(f"json文件不存在（videoId：{video_id}）")


    with open(file_path,'r',encoding='utf8') as f:
        json_load = json.load(f)  # dict

    course_info = {"video_basic_info":json_load['video_basic_info']}
    report = ReportGenerator(title=f"{courseName}")
    docx_path, pdf_path = report.generate_full_report(course_info)

    print("\n🎉 报告生成流程结束！")
    # if docx_path:
    #     print(f"📄 DOCX文件：{docx_path}")
    #     return jsonify({"code":200,"report_path":f"{docx_path}"})
    if pdf_path:
        print(f"📄 PDF文件：{pdf_path}")
        return jsonify({"code":200,"report_path":f"{pdf_path}"})





# 评价报告下载
# 配置允许下载的根目录（为了安全，限制只能下载该目录及其子目录下的文件）
import os
import zipfile
import tempfile
import requests
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify, send_from_directory, after_this_request

# 配置允许下载的根目录（安全限制）
ALLOWED_DOWNLOAD_ROOT = os.path.abspath('./output')
os.makedirs(ALLOWED_DOWNLOAD_ROOT, exist_ok=True)


@app.route('/api/download-file', methods=['GET'])
def download_file():
    # 1. 临时ZIP文件创建（delete=False：手动控制删除时机）
    temp_zip_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.zip', delete=False)
    temp_zip_path = temp_zip_file.name
    temp_zip_file.close()  # 先关闭文件句柄，避免后续打包时占用

    # 初始化变量
    valid_report_paths = []  # 有效报告路径（存绝对路径）
    failed_video_ids = []    # 失败的video_id详情

    try:
        # 2. 解析video_id参数（支持多个，逗号分隔）
        video_ids_str = request.args.get('video_id')
        if not video_ids_str:
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "请提供video_id参数"}), 400

        video_ids = [vid.strip() for vid in video_ids_str.split(',') if vid.strip()]
        if not video_ids:
            return jsonify({"status": "error", "message": "video_id参数不能为空或无效"}), 400

        # 3. 批量生成报告，收集有效（绝对）路径
        for video_id in video_ids:
            try:
                # 调用报告生成接口（确保返回的report_path是绝对路径）
                resp = requests.get(
                    "http://localhost:5000/api/report",
                    params={"video_id": video_id},
                    timeout=30
                )
                resp.raise_for_status()  # 触发4xx/5xx错误
                report_path = resp.json().get('report_path')

                # 关键：转换为绝对路径，避免相对路径导致的找不到文件
                report_abs_path = os.path.abspath(report_path)
                # 安全校验：确保报告文件在允许的下载根目录内（防止越权访问）
                if not report_abs_path.startswith(ALLOWED_DOWNLOAD_ROOT):
                    raise PermissionError(f"报告文件不在允许的下载目录内：{report_abs_path}")

                # 验证文件存在且可读取
                if not os.path.exists(report_abs_path) or not os.path.isfile(report_abs_path):
                    raise FileNotFoundError(f"报告文件不存在：{report_abs_path}")
                if not os.access(report_abs_path, os.R_OK):
                    raise PermissionError(f"无权限读取报告文件：{report_abs_path}")

                valid_report_paths.append((video_id, report_abs_path))
                # 替换print为Flask日志（避免混入响应）
                app.logger.info(f"✅ video_id [{video_id}] 报告生成成功：{report_abs_path}")

            except Exception as e:
                err_msg = str(e)
                failed_video_ids.append({"video_id": video_id, "error": err_msg})
                app.logger.error(f"❌ video_id [{video_id}] 报告生成失败：{err_msg}")
                continue

        # 4. 校验是否有有效文件可打包
        if not valid_report_paths:
            return jsonify({
                "status": "error",
                "message": "所有video_id均未生成成功报告",
                "failed_details": failed_video_ids
            }), 400

        # 5. 打包有效报告到临时ZIP（确保写入完整）
        with zipfile.ZipFile(temp_zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:
            for video_id, report_abs_path in valid_report_paths:
                report_filename = os.path.basename(report_abs_path)
                # 写入ZIP：确保文件完全读取（避免文件占用）
                zipf.write(report_abs_path, arcname=report_filename)
                app.logger.info(f"📦 已添加到ZIP：{report_filename}（源路径：{report_abs_path}）")

        # 6. 生成下载响应（关键：配置响应头+确保清理时机）
        zip_filename = f"reports_{'-'.join(video_ids[:5])}{'...' if len(video_ids)>5 else ''}.zip"
        directory, filename = os.path.split(temp_zip_path)

        # 响应发送完成后，立即清理临时文件（替换atexit，避免提前删除）
        @after_this_request
        def clean_up(response):
            try:
                os.remove(temp_zip_path)
                app.logger.info(f"🧹 临时ZIP文件已清理：{temp_zip_path}")
            except Exception as e:
                app.logger.warning(f"⚠️ 清理临时文件失败：{str(e)}")
            return response

        # 生成响应：手动指定二进制类型，避免浏览器解析错误
        response = send_from_directory(
            directory=directory,
            path=filename,
            as_attachment=True,
            download_name=zip_filename
        )

        # 关键：强制设置ZIP文件的MIME类型，确保浏览器正确识别
        response.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/zip'
        # 禁止浏览器缓存（避免下载旧文件）
        response.headers['Cache-Control'] = 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate'
        # 附加业务信息（通过自定义头传递，不影响文件下载）
        response.headers['X-Success-Count'] = str(len(valid_report_paths))
        response.headers['X-Failed-Count'] = str(len(failed_video_ids))
        if failed_video_ids:
            # 转义为JSON字符串，避免特殊字符问题
            import json
            response.headers['X-Failed-Details'] = json.dumps(failed_video_ids)

        app.logger.info(f"📤 ZIP包下载响应已生成：{zip_filename}（含{len(valid_report_paths)}个报告）")
        return response

    except Exception as e:
        # 捕获全局异常，确保错误时也清理临时文件
        app.logger.error(f"❌ 下载接口全局错误：{str(e)}", exc_info=True)
        # 清理临时文件（避免残留）
        if os.path.exists(temp_zip_path):
            try:
                os.remove(temp_zip_path)
                app.logger.info(f"🧹 异常场景下清理临时文件：{temp_zip_path}")
            except Exception as clean_e:
                app.logger.warning(f"⚠️ 异常场景下清理临时文件失败：{str(clean_e)}")
        # 返回错误信息
        return jsonify({
            "status": "error",
            "message": f"文件打包/下载失败：{str(e)}",
            "failed_video_ids": [f["video_id"] for f in failed_video_ids]
        }), 500





# 语音转写及编码文件下载接口
from io import BytesIO
import os
import re
import tempfile
import zipfile
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from urllib.parse import quote
from flask import Flask, request, make_response

# 配置文件存放目录
CSV1_DIR = "./csv_results"
CSV2_DIR = "./话语形式分析结果"
TXT_DIR = "./话语功能分析结果"
EMOTION_BEHAVIOR_DIR = "./师生情绪与行为"

def clean_data(data):
    """清洗数据，去除不可见字符和多余空格"""
    if pd.isna(data):
        return ""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        cleaned = re.sub(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x0c\x0e-\x1f]', '', data)
        cleaned = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', cleaned).strip()
        return cleaned
    return data
def extract_categories(form_str):
    """安全提取分类结果中的内容、一级、二级"""
    if pd.isna(form_str) or not str(form_str).strip():
        return "", "", ""
    form_str = clean_data(str(form_str))

    # 分割最后一部分（最多两次 - 分割）
    parts = form_str.rsplit('-', 2)
    if len(parts) < 3:
        return form_str, "", ""

    content = parts[0].strip()
    level1 = parts[1].strip()
    level2 = parts[2].strip()

    # 去除内容中的“角色: ”前缀
    if ":" in content:
        content = content.split(":", 1)[1].strip()

    return content, level1, level2
def extract_functions(func_str):
    """提取功能分类：内容 | 一级 | 二级"""
    if pd.isna(func_str) or not str(func_str).strip():
        return "", "", ""
    func_str = clean_data(str(func_str))

    parts = func_str.split('|', 2)
    if len(parts) < 3:
        return func_str, "", ""

    content = parts[0].strip()
    level1 = parts[1].strip()
    level2 = parts[2].strip()

    # 去除内容中的“角色：”或“角色:”
    if "：" in content:
        content = content.split("：", 1)[1].strip()
    elif ":" in content:
        content = content.split(":", 1)[1].strip()

    return content, level1, level2
def align_by_order(df_base, df_form, func_lines):
    """
    基于顺序对齐：原始转录 + 形式分类 + 功能分类
    假设三者顺序一致，按行一一对应
    """
    base_len = len(df_base)

    # 提取形式分类数据（按顺序）
    form_data = []
    for _, row in df_form.iterrows():
        _, level1, level2 = extract_categories(row['分类结果'])
        form_data.append((level1, level2))

    # 提取功能分类数据（按顺序）
    func_data = []
    for line in func_lines:
        line = line.strip()
        if line:
            _, level1, level2 = extract_functions(line)
            func_data.append((level1, level2))

    # 初始化列
    df_base['形式一级'] = ""
    df_base['形式二级'] = ""
    df_base['功能一级'] = ""
    df_base['功能二级'] = ""

    # 按索引顺序填充（只填充 min 长度，避免越界）
    min_len = min(base_len, len(form_data), len(func_data))
    for i in range(min_len):
        df_base.at[i, '形式一级'] = form_data[i][0]
        df_base.at[i, '形式二级'] = form_data[i][1]
        df_base.at[i, '功能一级'] = func_data[i][0]
        df_base.at[i, '功能二级'] = func_data[i][1]

    # 警告日志（可选）
    if len(form_data) != base_len:
        print(f"警告：形式分类行数不匹配：{len(form_data)} vs {base_len}")
    if len(func_data) != base_len:
        print(f"警告：功能分类行数不匹配：{len(func_data)} vs {base_len}")

    return df_base
def standardize_stat_columns(df, table_name):
    """标准化统计表格列名并处理特殊值"""
    df = df.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
    df = df.fillna('')

    base_required_cols = ['维度', '一级指标', '二级指标', '占比', '汇总', '优质课']
    missing_base_cols = [col for col in base_required_cols if col not in df.columns]
    if missing_base_cols:
        raise ValueError(f"{table_name}CSV文件缺少必要列：{', '.join(missing_base_cols)}")

    freq_candidates = ['频次（次）', '频次(次)', '频次（秒）', '频次(秒)']
    total_candidates = ['总计（次）', '总计(次)', '总计（秒）', '总计(秒)']

    freq_col = next((col for col in freq_candidates if col in df.columns), None)
    if not freq_col:
        raise ValueError(f"{table_name}CSV文件缺少频次列（需包含以下一种：{', '.join(freq_candidates)}）")

    total_col = next((col for col in total_candidates if col in df.columns), None)
    if not total_col:
        raise ValueError(f"{table_name}CSV文件缺少总计列（需包含以下一种：{', '.join(total_candidates)}）")

    df = df.rename(columns={freq_col: '频次', total_col: '总计'})
    return df
def write_stat_table(worksheet, start_row, table_name, df, title_format, header_format, content_format, merge_format):
    """写入统计表格（含表名称+数据），并处理维度和一级指标的合并居中"""
    col_count = len(df.columns)
    worksheet.merge_range(start_row, 0, start_row, col_count - 1, table_name, title_format)

    for col_idx, col_name in enumerate(df.columns):
        worksheet.write(start_row + 1, col_idx, col_name, header_format)

    if '维度' in df.columns:
        dimension_col = df.columns.get_loc('维度')
        first_level_col = df.columns.get_loc('一级指标')
        first_row = start_row + 2
        current_dimension = df.iloc[0]['维度']
        current_first_level = df.iloc[0]['一级指标']
        dim_start = 0
        fl_start = 0

        for i in range(1, len(df)):
            if df.iloc[i]['维度'] != current_dimension:
                if i - 1 > dim_start:
                    worksheet.merge_range(first_row + dim_start, dimension_col, first_row + i - 1, dimension_col,
                                          current_dimension, merge_format)
                current_dimension = df.iloc[i]['维度']
                dim_start = i

            if df.iloc[i]['一级指标'] != current_first_level:
                if i - 1 > fl_start:
                    worksheet.merge_range(first_row + fl_start, first_level_col, first_row + i - 1, first_level_col,
                                          current_first_level, merge_format)
                current_first_level = df.iloc[i]['一级指标']
                fl_start = i

        # 最后一组
        if len(df) - 1 > dim_start:
            worksheet.merge_range(first_row + dim_start, dimension_col, first_row + len(df) - 1, dimension_col,
                                  current_dimension, merge_format)
        if len(df) - 1 > fl_start:
            worksheet.merge_range(first_row + fl_start, first_level_col, first_row + len(df) - 1, first_level_col,
                                  current_first_level, merge_format)

    for row_idx, (_, row_data) in enumerate(df.iterrows(), start=start_row + 2):
        for col_idx, value in enumerate(row_data):
            if pd.isna(value) or value in [np.inf, -np.inf, 'nan', 'inf']:
                worksheet.write(row_idx, col_idx, '', content_format)
            else:
                worksheet.write(row_idx, col_idx, value, content_format)

    return start_row + len(df) + 3


@app.route('/api/download_merged', methods=['GET'])
def download_merged_file():
    video_ids_str = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_ids_str:
        return "缺少video_id参数", 400

    video_ids = [vid.strip() for vid in video_ids_str.split(',') if vid.strip()]
    if not video_ids:
        return "video_id参数不能为空或无效", 400

    try:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.zip', delete=False) as temp_zip_file:
            temp_zip_path = temp_zip_file.name
            valid_files = []
            failed_ids = []

            for video_id in video_ids:
                try:
                    csv1_path = os.path.join(CSV1_DIR, f"{video_id}_result.csv")
                    csv2_dialog_path = os.path.join(CSV2_DIR, f"话语形式_分类对话_{video_id}.csv")
                    func_txt_path = os.path.join(TXT_DIR, f"分类后文本_{video_id}.txt")
                    emotion_csv_path = os.path.join(EMOTION_BEHAVIOR_DIR, f"{video_id}_师生情绪.csv")
                    behavior_csv_path = os.path.join(EMOTION_BEHAVIOR_DIR, f"{video_id}_师生行为.csv")
                    form_summary_csv_path = os.path.join(CSV2_DIR, f"话语形式_总结_{video_id}.csv")
                    func_summary_csv_path = os.path.join(TXT_DIR, f"话语功能_分类结果汇总_{video_id}.csv")

                    all_needed_files = [csv1_path, csv2_dialog_path, func_txt_path,
                                        emotion_csv_path, behavior_csv_path, form_summary_csv_path,
                                        func_summary_csv_path]
                    missing_files = [os.path.basename(p) for p in all_needed_files if not os.path.exists(p)]
                    if missing_files:
                        raise FileNotFoundError(f"缺失文件: {', '.join(missing_files)}")

                    # 读取原始转录
                    df1 = pd.read_csv(csv1_path).map(clean_data)
                    df1 = df1.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna('')
                    required_base_cols = ['时间', '角色', '内容']
                    if not all(col in df1.columns for col in required_base_cols):
                        raise ValueError("原始转写CSV缺少必要列（时间/角色/内容）")

                    # 读取形式分类
                    df2_dialog = pd.read_csv(csv2_dialog_path).map(clean_data)
                    df2_dialog = df2_dialog.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna('')
                    if '分类结果' not in df2_dialog.columns:
                        raise ValueError("话语形式分类CSV缺少'分类结果'列")

                    # 读取功能分类文本（保留原始行）
                    func_data_lines = []
                    with open(func_txt_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        for line in f:
                            line = line.strip()
                            if line:
                                func_data_lines.append(line)

                    # ✅ 核心：顺序对齐
                    df1 = align_by_order(df1, df2_dialog, func_data_lines)
                    code_result_df = df1[['时间', '角色', '内容', '形式一级', '形式二级', '功能一级', '功能二级']]

                    # 读取统计表
                    emotion_df = pd.read_csv(emotion_csv_path).map(clean_data)
                    emotion_df = standardize_stat_columns(emotion_df, "师生情绪统计")

                    behavior_df = pd.read_csv(behavior_csv_path).map(clean_data)
                    behavior_df = standardize_stat_columns(behavior_df, "师生行为统计")

                    form_summary_df = pd.read_csv(form_summary_csv_path).map(clean_data)
                    form_summary_df = standardize_stat_columns(form_summary_df, "师生话语形式统计")

                    func_summary_df = pd.read_csv(func_summary_csv_path).map(clean_data)
                    func_summary_df = standardize_stat_columns(func_summary_df, "师生话语功能统计")

                    # 生成 Excel
                    excel_output = BytesIO()
                    with pd.ExcelWriter(excel_output, engine='xlsxwriter') as writer:
                        code_result_df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='编码结果展示表', startrow=2, index=False,
                                                header=False)
                        worksheet1 = writer.sheets['编码结果展示表']

                        title_format = writer.book.add_format({
                            'font_size': 18, 'bold': True, 'align': 'center', 'valign': 'vcenter',
                            'bg_color': '#F0F0F0', 'border': 1
                        })
                        header_format = writer.book.add_format({
                            'font_size': 12, 'bold': True, 'align': 'center', 'valign': 'vcenter',
                            'border': 1, 'bg_color': '#E6E6FA'
                        })
                        content_format = writer.book.add_format({
                            'font_size': 11, 'valign': 'vcenter', 'border': 1, 'text_wrap': True
                        })
                        merge_format = writer.book.add_format({
                            'font_size': 11, 'align': 'center', 'valign': 'vcenter',
                            'border': 1, 'text_wrap': True
                        })

                        worksheet1.merge_range('A1:G1', '语音转写及编码结果展示表', title_format)
                        headers = ['时间', '角色', '内容', '形式一级', '形式二级', '功能一级', '功能二级']
                        for col_num, h in enumerate(headers):
                            worksheet1.write(1, col_num, h, header_format)
                        col_widths = [12, 10, 45, 18, 18, 18, 18]
                        for i, w in enumerate(col_widths):
                            worksheet1.set_column(i, i, w)

                        # 频次统计表
                        worksheet2 = writer.book.add_worksheet('频次统计表')
                        stat_tables = [
                            ("师生情绪统计", emotion_df),
                            ("师生行为统计", behavior_df),
                            ("师生话语形式统计", form_summary_df),
                            ("师生话语功能统计", func_summary_df)
                        ]
                        current_row = 0
                        for name, df in stat_tables:
                            current_row = write_stat_table(worksheet2, current_row, name, df,
                                                           title_format, header_format, content_format, merge_format)
                        for i in range(8):
                            worksheet2.set_column(i, i, 12)

                    excel_output.seek(0)
                    valid_files.append((video_id, excel_output))
                    print(f"成功处理 video_id: {video_id}")

                except Exception as e:
                    failed_ids.append(f"{video_id}: {str(e)}")
                    print(f"处理 {video_id} 失败: {str(e)}")
                    continue

            if not valid_files:
                return f"所有video_id处理失败: {', '.join(failed_ids)}", 400

            # 打包 ZIP
            with zipfile.ZipFile(temp_zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:
                for video_id, stream in valid_files:
                    info_path = os.path.join(r"./uploads", f"{video_id}_info.json")
                    course_name = video_id  # 防错
                    if os.path.exists(info_path):
                        with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                            data = json.load(f)
                            course_name = data.get('courseName', video_id)
                    stream.seek(0)
                    zipf.writestr(f"语音转写编码_{course_name}.xlsx", stream.read())

            with open(temp_zip_path, 'rb') as f:
                zip_content = f.read()

            response = make_response(zip_content)
            zip_filename = f"语音转写编码汇总_{'-'.join(video_ids[:5])}{'...' if len(video_ids) > 5 else ''}.zip"
            encoded_filename = quote(zip_filename)
            response.headers["Content-Disposition"] = f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{encoded_filename}"
            response.headers["Content-type"] = "application/zip"
            response.headers["X-Success-Count"] = str(len(valid_files))
            response.headers["X-Failed-Count"] = str(len(failed_ids))
            if failed_ids:
                print(f"失败详情: {'; '.join(failed_ids)}")

            return response

    except Exception as e:
        return f"服务端处理出错: {str(e)}", 500

    finally:
        if 'temp_zip_path' in locals() and os.path.exists(temp_zip_path):
            try:
                os.remove(temp_zip_path)
            except Exception as clean_e:
                print(f"清理临时文件失败: {str(clean_e)}")




# 对话内容下载
@app.route('/api/download_csv', methods=['GET'])
def download_csv_zip():
    """
    下载指定 video_id 对应的 CSV 文件（打包为 ZIP）
    文件名格式：video_id_课程名称_result.csv
    请求示例：/api/download_csv?video_id=vid1,vid2,vid3
    """
    # 1. 获取并解析 video_id 参数
    video_ids_str = request.args.get('video_id')
    if not video_ids_str:
        return "错误：缺少 video_id 参数", 400

    # 去重、过滤空值，得到有效 video_id 列表
    video_ids = list(set([vid.strip() for vid in video_ids_str.split(',') if vid.strip()]))
    if not video_ids:
        return "错误：video_id 参数为空或无效", 400

    # 2. 查找 CSV 文件 + 读取课程名称，收集有效文件信息
    valid_files = []  # 格式：[(video_id, csv_path, course_name), ...]
    missing_video_ids = []

    for video_id in video_ids:
        # 2.1 查找 CSV 文件
        csv_path = os.path.join(CSV1_DIR, f"{video_id}_result.csv")
        if not (os.path.exists(csv_path) and os.path.isfile(csv_path)):
            missing_video_ids.append(video_id)
            continue
        UPLOADS_DIR = './uploads'
        # 2.2 读取课程名称（从 info.json 中获取）
        info_path = os.path.join(UPLOADS_DIR, f"{video_id}_info.json")
        course_name = video_id  # 默认用 video_id 替代
        if os.path.exists(info_path) and os.path.isfile(info_path):
            try:
                with open(info_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    # 优先取 courseName 字段，无则保留默认值
                    course_name = data.get('courseName', video_id).strip()
            except Exception as e:
                app.logger.warning(f"读取 {video_id}_info.json 失败：{str(e)}，使用默认名称")

        # 2.3 保存有效文件信息
        valid_files.append((video_id, csv_path, course_name))

    # 3. 检查是否有有效文件
    if not valid_files:
        return f"错误：所有 video_id 对应的文件均不存在！缺失：{', '.join(missing_video_ids)}", 404

    # 4. 创建临时 ZIP 文件（自动清理）
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.zip', delete=False) as temp_zip_file:
        temp_zip_path = temp_zip_file.name

        # 5. 打包所有有效 CSV 到 ZIP（文件名按要求格式化）
        with zipfile.ZipFile(temp_zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:
            for video_id, csv_path, course_name in valid_files:
                # 格式化 ZIP 内文件名：video_id_课程名称_result.csv
                # 处理特殊字符（避免文件名非法）
                safe_course_name = course_name.replace('/', '_').replace('\\', '_').replace(':', '_')
                zip_inner_name = f"{video_id}_{safe_course_name}_result.xlsx"
                # 写入 ZIP（arcname 为 ZIP 内显示的最终文件名）
                zipf.write(csv_path, arcname=zip_inner_name)

        # 6. 读取 ZIP 内容并返回给前端
        with open(temp_zip_path, 'rb') as f:
            zip_content = f.read()

        # 7. 构建响应（ZIP 文件名支持中文，自动编码）
        # ZIP 包名称：CSV汇总_课程1-课程2-...（最多5个课程名）
        course_names_short = [item[2] for item in valid_files[:5]]
        # zip_filename = f"对话内容汇总_{'-'.join(course_names_short)}{'...' if len(valid_files) > 5 else ''}.zip"
        zip_filename = f"对话内容汇总.zip"
        encoded_filename = quote(zip_filename)  # 处理中文文件名编码

        response = make_response(zip_content)
        response.headers["Content-Disposition"] = f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{encoded_filename}"
        response.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/zip"
        # 附加统计信息（前端可按需获取）
        response.headers["X-Valid-Count"] = str(len(valid_files))
        response.headers["X-Missing-IDs"] = ",".join(missing_video_ids) if missing_video_ids else "none"

        return response
